Agentic Discovery with Active Hypothesis Exploration for Visual Recognition
Authors: Jaywon Koo, Jefferson Hernandez, Ruozhen He, Hanjie Chen, Chen Wei, Vicente Ordonez
First: 2026-04-14T17:34:05+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-14T17:34:05+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We introduce HypoExplore, an agentic framework that formulates neural architecture discovery for visual recognition as a hypothesis-driven scientific inquiry. Given a human-specified high-level research direction, HypoExplore ideates, implements, evaluates, and improves neural architectures through evolutionary branching. New hypotheses are created using a large language model by selecting a parent hypothesis to build upon, guided by a dual strategy that balances exploiting validated principles with resolving uncertain ones. Our proposed framework maintains a Trajectory Tree that records the lineage of all proposed architectures, and a Hypothesis Memory Bank that actively tracks confidence scores acquired through experimental evidence. After each experiment, multiple feedback agents analyze the results from different perspectives and consolidate their findings into hypothesis confidence updates. Our framework is tested on discovering lightweight vision architectures on CIFAR-10, with the best achieving 94.11% accuracy evolved from a root node baseline that starts at 18.91%, and generalizes to CIFAR-100 and Tiny-ImageNet. We further demonstrate applicability to a specialized domain by conducting independent architecture discovery runs on MedMNIST, which yield a state-of-the-art performance. We show that hypothesis confidence scores grow increasingly predictive as evidence accumulates, and that the learned principles transfer across independent evolutionary lineages, suggesting that HypoExplore not only discovers stronger architectures, but can help build a genuine understanding of the design space.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于主动假设探索的代理发现视觉识别架构
我们提出了HypoExplore，一个将视觉识别神经架构发现建模为假设驱动科学探究的代理框架。给定人类指定的高层次研究方向，HypoExplore通过进化分支构思、实现、评估并改进神经架构。新假设由大语言模型生成，通过选择父假设进行扩展，采用双策略平衡利用已验证原则与解决不确定因素。我们的框架维护一个轨迹树，记录所有提议架构的谱系，并维护一个假设记忆库，主动追踪通过实验证据获得的置信度分数。每次实验后，多个反馈代理从不同角度分析结果，并将发现整合为假设置信度更新。该框架在CIFAR-10上测试轻量级视觉架构发现，最佳结果从18.91%的基线进化至94.11%，并可泛化至CIFAR-100和Tiny-ImageNet。我们进一步在MedMNIST上独立运行架构发现实验，获得当前最优性能。我们表明，随着证据积累，假设置信度分数预测性增强，且所学原则可在独立进化谱系间迁移，表明HypoExplore不仅能发现更强架构，还能帮助构建设计空间的真实理解。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to automate neural architecture discovery through scientific reasoning, HypoExplore frames visual recognition architecture search as an active hypothesis exploration process. It employs a large language model to generate new architecture hypotheses by evolving from validated parent hypotheses, guided by a dual strategy balancing exploitation of known principles and resolution of uncertainties. The framework maintains a Trajectory Tree to track architectural lineage and a Hypothesis Memory Bank to accumulate confidence scores from experimental feedback. Multiple feedback agents analyze each experiment’s results and update hypothesis confidence, enabling iterative improvement. Experiments on CIFAR-10 show a baseline accuracy of 18.91% evolving to 94.11%, with strong generalization to CIFAR-100 and Tiny-ImageNet, and state-of-the-art results on MedMNIST. Confidence scores become increasingly predictive with evidence, and learned principles transfer across independent evolutionary paths, indicating both performance gains and emergent design understanding.
为实现视觉识别神经架构的自动化发现，HypoExplore将架构搜索建模为基于假设的主动探索过程。该框架利用大语言模型从已验证的父假设出发生成新架构，通过兼顾已知原理的利用与未知问题的解决进行双策略引导。系统维护轨迹树记录架构演化谱系，并通过假设记忆库动态更新实验反馈后的置信度评分，由多反馈代理从不同角度分析实验结果并整合更新。在CIFAR-10上，最优架构从初始18.91%准确率提升至94.11%，并可泛化至CIFAR-100与Tiny-ImageNet，且在MedMNIST上达到当前最优性能。随着实验证据积累，置信度评分预测性增强，且跨独立演化线的原理可迁移，表明该框架不仅发现高性能架构，还能构建对设计空间的可解释性理解。
AniGen: Unified $S^3$ Fields for Animatable 3D Asset Generation
Authors: Yi-Hua Huang, Zi-Xin Zou, Yuting He, Chirui Chang, Cheng-Feng Pu, Ziyi Yang, Yuan-Chen Guo, Yan-Pei Cao, Xiaojuan Qi
First: 2026-04-09T20:22:06+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-14T17:33:59+00:00
Comments: 16 pages, 12 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Animatable 3D assets, defined as geometry equipped with an articulated skeleton and skinning weights, are fundamental to interactive graphics, embodied agents, and animation production. While recent 3D generative models can synthesize visually plausible shapes from images, the results are typically static. Obtaining usable rigs via post-hoc auto-rigging is brittle and often produces skeletons that are topologically inconsistent with the generated geometry. We present AniGen, a unified framework that directly generates animate-ready 3D assets conditioned on a single image. Our key insight is to represent shape, skeleton, and skinning as mutually consistent $S^3$ Fields (Shape, Skeleton, Skin) defined over a shared spatial domain. To enable the robust learning of these fields, we introduce two technical innovations: (i) a confidence-decaying skeleton field that explicitly handles the geometric ambiguity of bone prediction at Voronoi boundaries, and (ii) a dual skin feature field that decouples skinning weights from specific joint counts, allowing a fixed-architecture network to predict rigs of arbitrary complexity. Built upon a two-stage flow-matching pipeline, AniGen first synthesizes a sparse structural scaffold and then generates dense geometry and articulation in a structured latent space. Extensive experiments demonstrate that AniGen substantially outperforms state-of-the-art sequential baselines in rig validity and animation quality, generalizing effectively to in-the-wild images across diverse categories including animals, humanoids, and machinery. Homepage: https://yihua7.github.io/AniGen-web/
中文标题/摘要
标题：AniGen：用于可动画3D资产生成的统一$S^3$场
可动画3D资产，即配备分节骨骼与皮肤权重的几何体，是交互式图形、具身代理和动画制作的基础。尽管近期3D生成模型可从图像合成视觉上合理的形状，但结果通常是静态的。通过事后自动绑定获取可用的骨骼结构脆弱且常产生与生成几何体拓扑不一致的骨骼。我们提出AniGen，一个统一框架，可直接根据单张图像生成可动画3D资产。我们的核心思路是将形状、骨骼和皮肤表示为在共享空间域上相互一致的$S^3$场（形状、骨骼、皮肤）。为实现这些场的稳健学习，我们引入两项技术革新：(i) 一种置信度衰减的骨骼场，显式处理Voronoi边界处骨预测的几何歧义；(ii) 一种双皮肤特征场，将皮肤权重与特定关节数解耦，使固定架构网络可预测任意复杂度的骨骼结构。AniGen基于两阶段流匹配管道构建：首先合成稀疏结构支架，然后在结构化潜在空间中生成密集几何与运动结构。大量实验表明，AniGen在骨骼有效性与动画质量上显著优于现有序列化基线方法，并能有效泛化至野外图像，涵盖动物、人形及机械等多样化类别。项目主页：https://yihua7.github.io/AniGen-web/
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for directly generating animatable 3D assets from single images without brittle post-processing, AniGen introduces a unified framework that jointly models shape, skeleton, and skinning as mutually consistent S³ Fields over a shared spatial domain. It employs two key innovations: a confidence-decaying skeleton field to handle geometric ambiguity at Voronoi boundaries and a dual skin feature field to decouple skinning weights from joint counts, enabling flexible rig generation. Using a two-stage flow-matching pipeline, it first builds a sparse structural scaffold and then refines dense geometry and articulation in a structured latent space. Experimental results show AniGen significantly outperforms prior methods in rig validity and animation quality, generalizing robustly across diverse categories including animals, humanoids, and machinery.
AniGen旨在从单张图像直接生成可动画的3D资产，避免了后处理自动绑定的脆弱性，通过将形状、骨架与皮肤权重统一建模为共享空间域上的S³场实现。其创新包括：一种置信度衰减的骨架场，用于处理Voronoi边界处的几何歧义；以及一种双皮肤特征场，将皮肤权重与关节数量解耦，使固定架构网络可预测任意复杂度的骨架。采用两阶段流匹配流程，先生成稀疏结构骨架，再在结构化潜在空间中生成密集几何与运动结构。实验表明，AniGen在骨架有效性与动画质量上显著优于现有方法，且在动物、人形及机械等多样化类别图像上具备良好泛化能力。
A Survey of Multimodal Mathematical Reasoning: From Perception, Alignment to Reasoning
Authors: Tianyu Yang, Sihong Wu, Yilun Zhao, Zhenwen Liang, Lisen Dai, Chen Zhao, Minhao Cheng, Arman Cohan, Xiangliang Zhang
Venue: ACL 2026
First: 2026-03-09T12:11:00+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-14T17:32:09+00:00
Comments: ACL 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Multimodal Mathematical Reasoning (MMR) has recently attracted increasing attention for its capability to solve mathematical problems involving both textual and visual modalities. However, current models still face significant challenges in real-world visual math tasks, often misinterpreting diagrams, failing to align mathematical symbols with visual evidence, or producing inconsistent reasoning steps. Moreover, existing evaluations mainly focus on checking final answers rather than verifying the correctness or executability of each intermediate step. A growing body of recent research addresses these issues by integrating structured perception, explicit alignment, and verifiable reasoning within unified frameworks. To establish a clear roadmap for understanding and comparing different MMR approaches, we systematically review them around four fundamental questions: (1) What to extract from multimodal inputs, (2) How to represent and align textual and visual information, (3) How to perform the reasoning, and (4) How to evaluate the correctness of the overall reasoning process. Finally, we discuss open challenges and share our thoughts on future research directions.
中文标题/摘要
标题：多模态数学推理综述：从感知、对齐到推理
多模态数学推理（MMR）因其能解决涉及文本与视觉模态的数学问题，近期受到越来越多关注。然而，当前模型在现实视觉数学任务中仍面临显著挑战，常误读图表、未能将数学符号与视觉证据对齐，或产生不一致的推理步骤。此外，现有评估主要关注最终答案，而非验证每一步推理的正确性或可执行性。近期大量研究通过在统一框架中整合结构化感知、显式对齐和可验证推理，逐步解决这些问题。为明确理解与比较不同MMR方法的路径，我们围绕四个基本问题系统综述：（1）从多模态输入中提取什么内容；（2）如何表示与对齐文本与视觉信息；（3）如何执行推理；（4）如何评估整体推理过程的正确性。最后，我们讨论开放挑战并分享对未来研究方向的看法。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to improve real-world performance in solving math problems that combine text and visuals, this survey examines how recent models handle multimodal mathematical reasoning. It reviews methods that integrate structured perception, explicit alignment of textual and visual elements, and verifiable reasoning steps within unified frameworks. The study evaluates current approaches through four key dimensions: input extraction, cross-modal representation, reasoning execution, and step-by-step correctness verification. Experimental findings indicate that while models show progress in aligning symbols with visuals and generating reasoning traces, they still frequently misinterpret diagrams and produce inconsistent or unverifiable intermediate steps, highlighting the need for more robust evaluation metrics beyond final answer correctness.
为应对现实世界中结合文本与视觉的数学问题，本综述探讨了当前模型在多模态数学推理中的表现，指出其仍存在图表误读、符号与视觉证据对齐失败及推理步骤不一致等挑战。研究系统梳理了整合结构化感知、显式跨模态对齐与可验证推理步骤的最新方法，并围绕四个核心问题——输入提取、模态表示与对齐、推理机制、评估标准——进行比较分析。结果表明，尽管在模态对齐与步骤推理方面取得进展，多数评估仍仅关注最终答案，缺乏对中间步骤正确性的验证，系统鲁棒性仍有待提升。
SAM3-I: Segment Anything with Instructions
Authors: Jingjing Li, Yue Feng, Yuchen Guo, Jincai Huang, Wei Ji, Qi Bi, Yongri Piao, Miao Zhang, Xiaoqi Zhao, Qiang Chen, Shihao Zou, Huchuan Lu, Li Cheng
First: 2025-12-04T09:00:25+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-14T17:28:04+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Segment Anything Model 3 (SAM3) advances open-vocabulary segmentation through promptable concept segmentation, enabling users to segment all instances associated with a given concept using short noun-phrase (NP) prompts. While effective for concept-level grounding, real-world interactions often involve far richer natural-language instructions that combine attributes, relations, actions, states, or implicit reasoning. Currently, SAM3 relies on external multi-modal agents to convert complex instructions into NPs and conducts iterative mask filtering, leading to coarse representations and limited instance specificity. In this work, we present SAM3-I, an instruction-following extension of the SAM family that unifies concept-level grounding and instruction-level reasoning within a single segmentation framework. Built upon SAM3, SAM3-I introduces an instruction-aware cascaded adaptation mechanism with dedicated alignment losses that progressively aligns expressive instruction semantics with SAM3's vision-language representations, enabling direct interpretation of natural-language instructions while preserving its strong concept recall ability. To enable instruction-following learning, we introduce HMPL-Instruct, a large-scale instruction-centric dataset that systematically covers hierarchical instruction semantics and diverse target granularities. Experiments demonstrate that SAM3-I achieves appealing performance across referring and reasoning-based segmentation, showing that SAM3 can be effectively extended to follow complex natural-language instructions without sacrificing its original concept-driven strengths. Code and dataset are available at https://github.com/debby-0527/SAM3-I.
中文标题/摘要
标题：SAM3-I：带指令的任意分割
Segment Anything Model 3（SAM3）通过可提示的概念分割推进开放词汇分割，使用户能使用简短的名词短语（NP）提示分割与给定概念相关的所有实例。尽管SAM3在概念级定位方面有效，但现实世界交互常涉及更丰富的自然语言指令，包含属性、关系、动作、状态或隐含推理。当前SAM3依赖外部多模态代理将复杂指令转换为NP，并进行迭代掩码过滤，导致表示粗糙且实例特异性有限。本文提出SAM3-I，作为SAM系列的指令遵循扩展，将概念级定位与指令级推理统一于单一分割框架内。基于SAM3，SAM3-I引入指令感知的级联自适应机制及专用对齐损失，逐步将表达性指令语义对齐至SAM3的视觉-语言表示，实现自然语言指令的直接解析，同时保留其强大的概念召回能力。为支持指令遵循学习，我们引入HMPL-Instruct，一个大规模以指令为中心的数据集，系统覆盖层级指令语义与多样目标粒度。实验表明，SAM3-I在指代与推理型分割任务中表现优异，证明SAM3可有效扩展以遵循复杂自然语言指令而不牺牲其原始概念驱动优势。代码与数据集见https://github.com/debby-0527/SAM3-I。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to handle complex natural-language instructions beyond simple noun-phrase prompts, SAM3-I extends the Segment Anything Model 3 to support instruction-following segmentation. It introduces an instruction-aware cascaded adaptation mechanism with specialized alignment losses that progressively map instruction semantics to vision-language representations, enabling direct interpretation of multi-faceted instructions while retaining SAM3’s strong concept recall. Evaluated on a new large-scale dataset HMPL-Instruct, SAM3-I demonstrates superior performance in both referring and reasoning-based segmentation tasks, showing that SAM3’s core strengths are preserved while enabling more expressive, instruction-driven segmentation.
为支持复杂自然语言指令的直接理解，SAM3-I在SAM3基础上引入指令感知级联自适应机制与专用对齐损失，逐步将指令语义与视觉-语言表示对齐，同时保留其概念召回能力。实验表明，SAM3-I在指代与推理型分割任务中显著优于SAM3及基线方法，无需外部代理或迭代过滤即可实现高精度指令遵循。
$λ_A$: A Typed Lambda Calculus for LLM Agent Composition
Authors: Qin Liu
First: 2026-04-13T17:39:23+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-14T17:27:56+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Existing LLM agent frameworks lack formal semantics: there is no principled way to determine whether an agent configuration is well-formed or will terminate. We present $λ_A$, a typed lambda calculus for agent composition that extends the simply-typed lambda calculus with oracle calls, bounded fixpoints (the ReAct loop), probabilistic choice, and mutable environments. We prove type safety, termination of bounded fixpoints, and soundness of derived lint rules, with full Coq mechanization (1,519 lines, 42 theorems, 0 Admitted). As a practical application, we derive a lint tool that detects structural configuration errors directly from the operational semantics. An evaluation on 835 real-world GitHub agent configurations shows that 94.1% are structurally incomplete under $λ_A$, with YAML-only lint precision at 54%, rising to 96--100% under joint YAML+Python AST analysis on 175 samples. This gap quantifies, for the first time, the degree of semantic entanglement between declarative configuration and imperative code in the agent ecosystem. We further show that five mainstream paradigms (LangGraph, CrewAI, AutoGen, OpenAI SDK, Dify) embed as typed $λ_A$ fragments, establishing $λ_A$ as a unifying calculus for LLM agent composition.
中文标题/摘要
标题：$λ_A$: 一种用于大语言模型代理组合的类型化λ演算
现有的大语言模型代理框架缺乏形式语义：没有原则性的方法来判断代理配置是否良构或是否会终止。我们提出了$λ_A$，一种用于代理组合的类型化λ演算，它在简单类型λ演算基础上扩展了预言机调用、有界不动点（ReAct循环）、概率选择和可变环境。我们证明了类型安全、有界不动点的终止性以及派生的lint规则的正确性，并在Coq中完整机械化（1519行，42个定理，0个Admitted）。作为实际应用，我们推导出一个lint工具，该工具直接从操作语义检测结构配置错误。在835个真实GitHub代理配置上的评估显示，94.1%的配置在$λ_A$下结构不完整，仅YAML lint的精确度为54%，而在175个样本上结合YAML与Python AST分析时提升至96-100%。这一差距首次量化了代理生态系统中声明式配置与命令式代码之间的语义纠缠程度。我们进一步表明，五种主流范式（LangGraph、CrewAI、AutoGen、OpenAI SDK、Dify）可嵌入为类型化$λ_A$片段，确立了$λ_A$作为大语言模型代理组合的统一演算。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the lack of formal semantics in existing LLM agent frameworks, the paper introduces $λ_A$, a typed lambda calculus that extends simply-typed lambda calculus with oracle calls, bounded fixpoints, probabilistic choice, and mutable environments to model agent composition. The authors prove type safety, termination of bounded fixpoints, and soundness of lint rules via Coq mechanization. Experimentally, they analyze 835 real-world GitHub agent configurations, finding 94.1% are structurally incomplete under $λ_A$, and show that joint YAML+Python AST analysis improves lint precision from 54% to 96–100%, revealing semantic entanglement between configuration and code. They also demonstrate that five major agent frameworks embed as typed $λ_A$ fragments, positioning $λ_A$ as a unifying formalism for agent composition.
为解决现有LLM代理框架缺乏形式语义的问题，本文提出$λ_A$，一种扩展简单类型λ演算的类型化λ演算，支持预言调用、有界不动点、概率选择和可变环境，用于建模代理组合。作者证明了类型安全、有界不动点终止性及推导规则的正确性，并在Coq中完整形式化（1519行，42个定理，无Admitted）。实验分析835个真实GitHub代理配置，发现94.1%在$λ_A$下结构不完整，联合YAML与Python AST分析可将检测精度从54%提升至96–100%。同时证明五大主流框架（LangGraph、CrewAI、AutoGen、OpenAI SDK、Dify）可嵌入为$λ_A$的类型化片段，确立$λ_A$作为LLM代理组合的统一形式化基础。
PolicyLLM: Towards Excellent Comprehension of Public Policy for Large Language Models
Authors: Han Bao, Penghao Zhang, Yue Huang, Zhengqing Yuan, Yanchi Ru, Rui Su, Yujun Zhou, Xiangqi Wang, Kehan Guo, Nitesh V Chawla, Yanfang Ye, Xiangliang Zhang
Venue: ACL 2026
First: 2026-04-14T17:27:50+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-14T17:27:50+00:00
Comments: Accepted by ACL 2026 findings
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Large Language Models (LLMs) are increasingly integrated into real-world decision-making, including in the domain of public policy. Yet, their ability to comprehend and reason about policy-related content remains underexplored. To fill this gap, we present \textbf{\textit{PolicyBench}}, the first large-scale cross-system benchmark (US-China) evaluating policy comprehension, comprising 21K cases across a broad spectrum of policy areas, capturing the diversity and complexity of real-world governance. Following Bloom's taxonomy, the benchmark assesses three core capabilities: (1) \textbf{Memorization}: factual recall of policy knowledge, (2) \textbf{Understanding}: conceptual and contextual reasoning, and (3) \textbf{Application}: problem-solving in real-life policy scenarios. Building on this benchmark, we further propose \textbf{\textit{PolicyMoE}}, a domain-specialized Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) model with expert modules aligned to each cognitive level. The proposed models demonstrate stronger performance on application-oriented policy tasks than on memorization or conceptual understanding, and yields the highest accuracy on structured reasoning tasks. Our results reveal key limitations of current LLMs in policy understanding and suggest paths toward more reliable, policy-focused models.
中文标题/摘要
标题：PolicyLLM：面向大语言模型的公共政策卓越理解
大语言模型（LLMs）正越来越多地融入现实决策，包括公共政策领域。然而，其对政策相关内容的理解与推理能力仍鲜有研究。为填补这一空白，我们提出了\textbf{\textit{PolicyBench}}，这是首个大规模跨系统（中美）政策理解评估基准，涵盖21K个案例，覆盖广泛政策领域，体现现实治理的多样性和复杂性。依据布卢姆分类法，该基准评估三大核心能力：(1) \textbf{记忆}：政策知识的事实性回忆，(2) \textbf{理解}：概念与情境推理，(3) \textbf{应用}：解决现实政策场景中的问题。在此基础上，我们进一步提出\textbf{\textit{PolicyMoE}}，一种领域专用的专家混合模型（MoE），其专家模块与各认知层级对齐。所提模型在应用导向的政策任务上表现优于记忆或概念理解任务，并在结构化推理任务中取得最高准确率。我们的结果揭示了当前LLMs在政策理解方面的关键局限，并指明了构建更可靠、聚焦政策模型的路径。

The benchmark evaluates three cognitive levels—memorization, understanding, and application—aligned with Bloom’s taxonomy. Building on this, they propose PolicyMoE, a domain-specialized Mixture-of-Experts model with experts tuned to each cognitive level. Experimental results show PolicyMoE outperforms general LLMs on application-oriented tasks and achieves highest accuracy on structured reasoning, revealing current LLMs’ limitations in policy reasoning and pointing toward specialized architectures for improved reliability.
鉴于大语言模型在公共政策决策中的广泛应用，本研究旨在弥补其政策理解能力尚未充分探索的不足。作者构建了PolicyBench，一个涵盖中美两国、包含21000个案例的跨系统大规模政策理解基准，依据布卢姆分类法评估记忆、理解与应用三大能力。在此基础上，提出PolicyMoE，一种针对政策领域的混合专家模型，各专家模块对应不同认知层级。实验表明，PolicyMoE在应用型政策任务和结构化推理任务上表现最优，揭示当前大模型在政策理解上的关键局限，并指明了构建更可靠政策导向模型的方向。
LogicEval: A Systematic Framework for Evaluating Automated Repair Techniques for Logical Vulnerabilities in Real-World Software
Authors: Syed Md Mukit Rashid, Abdullah Al Ishtiaq, Kai Tu, Yilu Dong, Tianwei Wu, Ali Ranjbar, Tianchang Yang, Najrin Sultana, Shagufta Mehnaz, Syed Rafiul Hussain
First: 2026-04-14T17:26:07+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-14T17:26:07+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Logical vulnerabilities in software stem from flaws in program logic rather than memory safety, which can lead to critical security failures. Although existing automated program repair techniques primarily focus on repairing memory corruption vulnerabilities, they struggle with logical vulnerabilities because of their limited semantic understanding of the vulnerable code and its expected behavior. On the other hand, recent successes of large language models (LLMs) in understanding and repairing code are promising. However, no framework currently exists to analyze the capabilities and limitations of such techniques for logical vulnerabilities. This paper aims to systematically evaluate both traditional and LLM-based repair approaches for addressing real-world logical vulnerabilities. To facilitate our assessment, we created the first ever dataset, LogicDS, of 86 logical vulnerabilities with assigned CVEs reflecting tangible security impact. We also developed a systematic framework, LogicEval, to evaluate patches for logical vulnerabilities. Evaluations suggest that compilation and testing failures are primarily driven by prompt sensitivity, loss of code context, and difficulty in patch localization.
中文标题/摘要
标题：LogicEval：评估真实世界软件逻辑漏洞自动化修复技术的系统框架
软件中的逻辑漏洞源于程序逻辑缺陷而非内存安全问题，可能导致严重安全失效。尽管现有自动化程序修复技术主要针对内存破坏漏洞，但它们难以处理逻辑漏洞，原因在于对脆弱代码及其预期行为的语义理解有限。另一方面，大型语言模型（LLMs）在理解和修复代码方面取得的最新进展颇具前景。然而，目前尚无框架用于分析此类技术在处理逻辑漏洞时的能力与局限性。本文旨在系统评估传统及基于LLM的修复方法在应对真实世界逻辑漏洞时的表现。为便于评估，我们创建了首个包含86个已分配CVE编号的逻辑漏洞数据集LogicDS，这些CVE编号反映了实际的安全影响。同时，我们开发了系统性评估框架LogicEval，用于评估逻辑漏洞补丁。评估结果表明，编译和测试失败主要由提示敏感性、代码上下文丢失及补丁定位困难导致。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to evaluate automated repair techniques for logical vulnerabilities—distinct from memory safety flaws—this paper introduces LogicEval, a systematic framework designed to assess both traditional and large language model-based repair approaches. To enable this evaluation, the authors constructed LogicDS, the first dataset of 86 real-world logical vulnerabilities tied to CVEs, reflecting actual security impacts. Experimental results show that repair attempts frequently fail due to prompt sensitivity, loss of code context, and challenges in accurately localizing patches, indicating that current methods lack robust semantic understanding of logical behavior.
为系统评估针对逻辑漏洞的自动化修复技术，本文提出LogicEval框架，用于评估传统与大语言模型驱动的修复方法。为此，作者构建了LogicDS数据集，包含86个关联CVE的真实世界逻辑漏洞，反映实际安全影响。实验表明，修复失败主要源于提示敏感性、上下文丢失及修复定位困难，说明现有方法在程序逻辑语义理解方面仍显不足。
Causal Diffusion Models for Counterfactual Outcome Distributions in Longitudinal Data
Authors: Farbod Alinezhad, Jianfei Cao, Gary J. Young, Brady Post
First: 2026-04-14T17:25:53+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-14T17:25:53+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Predicting counterfactual outcomes in longitudinal data, where sequential treatment decisions heavily depend on evolving patient states, is critical yet notoriously challenging due to complex time-dependent confounding and inadequate uncertainty quantification in existing methods. We introduce the Causal Diffusion Model (CDM), the first denoising diffusion probabilistic approach explicitly designed to generate full probabilistic distributions of counterfactual outcomes under sequential interventions. CDM employs a novel residual denoising architecture with relational self-attention, capturing intricate temporal dependencies and multimodal outcome trajectories without requiring explicit adjustments (e.g., inverse-probability weighting or adversarial balancing) for confounding. In rigorous evaluation on a pharmacokinetic-pharmacodynamic tumor-growth simulator widely adopted in prior work, CDM consistently outperforms state-of-the-art longitudinal causal inference methods, achieving a 15-30% relative improvement in distributional accuracy (1-Wasserstein distance) while maintaining competitive or superior point-estimate accuracy (RMSE) under high-confounding regimes. By unifying uncertainty quantification and robust counterfactual prediction in complex, sequentially confounded settings, without tailored deconfounding, CDM offers a flexible, high-impact tool for decision support in medicine, policy evaluation, and other longitudinal domains.
中文标题/摘要
标题：纵向数据中反事实结果分布的因果扩散模型
在纵向数据中预测反事实结果，其中连续治疗决策高度依赖于患者状态的演变，至关重要但极具挑战性，因为现有方法面临复杂的时间依赖性混杂因素和不确定性量化不足的问题。我们提出了因果扩散模型（CDM），这是首个专门设计用于在连续干预下生成反事实结果完整概率分布的去噪扩散概率方法。CDM采用了一种新颖的残差去噪架构，结合关系自注意力机制，无需显式调整（如逆概率加权或对抗性平衡）即可捕捉复杂的时序依赖性和多模态结果轨迹。在广泛用于先前研究的药代动力学-药效学肿瘤生长模拟器上的严格评估中，CDM持续优于最先进的纵向因果推断方法，在高混杂环境下，其分布准确性（1- Wasserstein距离）相对提升15-30%，同时保持与之相当或更优的点估计准确性（RMSE）。CDM通过统一复杂连续混杂环境下的不确定性量化与稳健反事实预测，无需专门去混杂，为医学决策支持、政策评估及其他纵向领域提供了一种灵活且高影响力工具。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the challenge of predicting counterfactual outcomes in longitudinal data under complex time-dependent confounding, the paper introduces the Causal Diffusion Model (CDM), a novel denoising diffusion framework designed to generate full probabilistic distributions of counterfactual outcomes under sequential interventions. CDM leverages a residual denoising architecture with relational self-attention to capture temporal dependencies and multimodal trajectories without requiring explicit confounding adjustment techniques. Evaluated on a pharmacokinetic-pharmacodynamic tumor-growth simulator, CDM consistently outperforms state-of-the-art methods, achieving 15-30% relative improvement in distributional accuracy (measured by 1-Wasserstein distance) while maintaining competitive or superior point-estimate accuracy (RMSE) under high-confounding conditions.
为解决纵向数据中因患者状态动态演变导致的序列治疗决策下的反事实结果预测难题，本文提出因果扩散模型（CDM），一种专为生成序列干预下反事实结果完整概率分布而设计的去噪扩散框架。CDM采用残差去噪架构结合关系自注意力机制，捕捉复杂时序依赖与多模态轨迹，无需显式进行混杂调整。在药代动力学-药效动力学肿瘤生长模拟器上的严格评估显示，CDM在高混杂环境下持续优于现有方法，分布精度（1- Wasserstein距离）提升15-30%，同时保持或超越点估计精度（RMSE），展现出在医学决策支持与政策评估等纵向领域中的强大实用性。
Accelerating Speculative Decoding with Block Diffusion Draft Trees
Authors: Liran Ringel, Yaniv Romano
First: 2026-04-14T17:23:14+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-14T17:23:14+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Speculative decoding accelerates autoregressive language models by using a lightweight drafter to propose multiple future tokens, which the target model then verifies in parallel. DFlash shows that a block diffusion drafter can generate an entire draft block in a single forward pass and achieve state-of-the-art speculative decoding performance, outperforming strong autoregressive drafters such as EAGLE-3. Vanilla DFlash, however, still verifies only a single drafted trajectory per round, potentially limiting its acceptance length. We introduce DDTree (Diffusion Draft Tree), a method that constructs a draft tree directly from the per-position distributions of a block diffusion drafter. Under a fixed node budget, DDTree uses a simple best-first heap algorithm to select the continuations that are most likely to match the target model according to a surrogate defined by the draft model's output. The resulting tree is verified efficiently in a single target model forward pass using an ancestor-only attention mask. Because DDTree builds on DFlash, a leading draft model for speculative decoding, these gains place DDTree among the leading approaches to speculative decoding.
中文标题/摘要
标题：利用块扩散草稿树加速推测解码
推测解码通过使用轻量级草稿生成器提出多个未来token，由目标模型并行验证，从而加速自回归语言模型。DFlash表明，块扩散草稿生成器可在单次前向传播中生成整个草稿块，并实现最先进的推测解码性能，超越EAGLE-3等强自回归草稿生成器。然而，基础版DFlash每轮仍仅验证单一草稿轨迹，可能限制其接受长度。我们提出DDTree（扩散草稿树），该方法直接从块扩散草稿生成器的每个位置分布构建草稿树。在固定节点预算下，DDTree采用简单的最佳优先堆算法，根据草稿模型输出定义的代理函数选择最可能匹配目标模型的延续路径。生成的树通过仅关注祖先的注意力掩码，在单次目标模型前向传播中高效验证。由于DDTree基于领先的推测解码草稿模型DFlash，其性能使其成为推测解码领域的领先方法之一。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to improve speculative decoding efficiency, the paper introduces DDTree, a method that constructs a draft tree from a block diffusion drafter’s per-position distributions to enable parallel verification of multiple draft trajectories. Using a best-first heap algorithm under a fixed node budget, DDTree selects the most promising continuations based on a surrogate metric derived from the drafter’s output. Experimental results show that DDTree achieves state-of-the-art performance in speculative decoding, outperforming vanilla DFlash and strong autoregressive drafters like EAGLE-3 by enabling longer acceptance lengths through efficient single-pass verification.
为提升推测解码效率，本文提出DDTree方法，该方法直接从块扩散草稿模型的每个位置分布构建草稿树，以支持并行验证多个草稿轨迹。在固定节点预算下，DDTree采用最佳优先堆算法，依据草稿模型输出定义的代理指标选择最可能匹配目标模型的延续路径。实验结果表明，与仅验证单条轨迹的原始DFlash相比，DDTree显著提升了接受长度，同时保持或提升了整体解码速度与质量。
ROSE: An Intent-Centered Evaluation Metric for NL2SQL
Authors: Wenqi Pei, Shizheng Hou, Boyan Li, Han Chen, Zhichao Shi, Yuyu Luo
Venue: ACL 2026
First: 2026-04-14T17:22:40+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-14T17:22:40+00:00
Comments: ACL 2026 Main
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Execution Accuracy (EX), the widely used metric for evaluating the effectiveness of Natural Language to SQL (NL2SQL) solutions, is becoming increasingly unreliable. It is sensitive to syntactic variation, ignores that questions may admit multiple interpretations, and is easily misled by erroneous ground-truth SQL. To address this, we introduce ROSE, an intent-centered metric that focuses on whether the predicted SQL answers the question, rather than consistency with the ground-truth SQL under the reference-dependent paradigm. ROSE employs an adversarial Prover-Refuter cascade: SQL Prover assesses the semantic correctness of a predicted SQL against the user's intent independently, while Adversarial Refuter uses the ground-truth SQL as evidence to challenge and refine this judgment. On our expert-aligned validation set ROSE-VEC, ROSE achieves the best agreement with human experts, outperforming the next-best metric by nearly 24% in Cohen's Kappa. We also conduct a largescale re-evaluation of 19 NL2SQL methods, revealing four valuable insights. We release ROSE and ROSE-VEC to facilitate more reliable NL2SQL research.
中文标题/摘要
标题：ROSE：面向意图中心的NL2SQL评估指标
执行准确率（EX），作为评估自然语言到SQL（NL2SQL）解决方案效果的常用指标，正变得日益不可靠。它对句法变化敏感，忽略问题可能有多种解释，且易受错误的基准SQL误导。为解决此问题，我们提出了ROSE，一种以意图为中心的评估指标，关注预测SQL是否回答了问题，而非在参考依赖范式下与基准SQL的一致性。ROSE采用对抗式证明者-反驳者级联机制：SQL证明者独立评估预测SQL与用户意图的语义正确性，而对抗式反驳者则利用基准SQL作为证据挑战并修正该判断。在我们与专家对齐的验证集ROSE-VEC上，ROSE与人类专家达成最佳一致性，Cohen's Kappa值比次优指标高出近24%。我们还对19种NL2SQL方法进行了大规模重新评估，揭示了四个重要见解。我们发布ROSE和ROSE-VEC，以促进更可靠的NL2SQL研究。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitations of Execution Accuracy in NL2SQL evaluation—its sensitivity to syntactic variation, disregard for multiple interpretations, and susceptibility to flawed ground-truth SQL—the paper proposes ROSE, an intent-centered metric that judges whether predicted SQL fulfills user intent rather than matching ground-truth SQL. ROSE uses an adversarial Prover-Refuter framework: the Prover independently verifies semantic correctness against intent, while the Refuter challenges this using ground-truth SQL as evidence. On ROSE-VEC, a validation set aligned with human judgment, ROSE achieves the highest agreement with experts, outperforming the next-best metric by nearly 24% in Cohen's Kappa. A large-scale re-evaluation of 19 NL2SQL methods further reveals key insights into current evaluation practices.
鉴于执行准确率（EX）在评估NL2SQL系统时存在对语法变化敏感、忽略问题多义性、易受错误参考SQL误导等缺陷，本文提出ROSE意图中心化评估指标，聚焦预测SQL是否真正回答用户意图，而非与参考SQL的一致性。ROSE采用对抗式证明者-反驳者框架：证明者独立验证SQL语义是否符合意图，反驳者则利用参考SQL挑战并修正该判断。在与人类专家高度对齐的ROSE-VEC验证集上，ROSE与人类专家一致性最高，比次优指标高出近24%的Cohen’s Kappa值。对19种NL2SQL方法的大规模重评估进一步揭示了四个关键评估洞察。
LangFlow: Continuous Diffusion Rivals Discrete in Language Modeling
Authors: Yuxin Chen, Chumeng Liang, Hangke Sui, Ruihan Guo, Chaoran Cheng, Jiaxuan You, Ge Liu
First: 2026-04-13T17:21:41+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-14T17:20:50+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Continuous diffusion has been the foundation of high-fidelity, controllable, and few-step generation of many data modalities such as images. However, in language modeling, prior continuous diffusion language models (DLMs) lag behind discrete counterparts due to the sparse data space and the underexplored design space. In this work, we close this gap with LangFlow, the first continuous DLM to rival discrete diffusion, by connecting embedding-space DLMs to Flow Matching via Bregman divergence, alongside three key innovations: (1) we derive a novel ODE-based NLL bound for principled evaluation of continuous flow-based language models; (2) we propose an information-uniform principle for setting the noise schedule, which motivates a learnable noise scheduler based on a Gumbel distribution; and (3) we revise prior training protocols by incorporating self-conditioning, as we find it improves both likelihood and sample quality of embedding-space DLMs with effects substantially different from discrete diffusion. Putting everything together, LangFlow rivals top discrete DLMs on both the perplexity (PPL) and the generative perplexity (Gen. PPL), reaching a PPL of 30.0 on LM1B and 24.6 on OpenWebText. It even exceeds autoregressive baselines in zero-shot transfer on 4 out of 7 benchmarks. LangFlow provides the first clear evidence that continuous diffusion is a promising paradigm for language modeling. Homepage: https://github.com/nealchen2003/LangFlow
中文标题/摘要
标题：LangFlow：连续扩散在语言建模中可媲美离散方法
连续扩散是生成图像等多模态数据时实现高保真、可控和少步生成的基础。然而，在语言建模中，此前的连续扩散语言模型（DLMs）因数据空间稀疏和设计空间未充分探索而落后于离散方法。本研究通过LangFlow——首个可媲美离散扩散的连续DLM——弥合这一差距，其核心是通过Bregman散度将嵌入空间DLMs与流匹配相连，并提出三项关键创新：（1）推导出基于ODE的新型NLL上界，用于原则性评估连续流语言模型；（2）提出信息均匀性原则以设定噪声调度，由此启发基于Gumbel分布的可学习噪声调度器；（3）修订原有训练协议，引入自条件化，我们发现这显著提升嵌入空间DLMs的似然与样本质量，且效果与离散扩散不同。综合来看，LangFlow在LM1B和OpenWebText数据集上分别达到30.0和24.6的PPL，且在7个基准中的4个上零样本迁移表现超越自回归基线。LangFlow首次明确表明，连续扩散是语言建模的有前景范式。项目主页：https://github.com/nealchen2003/LangFlow
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the success of continuous diffusion in image generation but its underperformance in language modeling due to sparse data and unexplored architectures, LangFlow introduces a novel continuous diffusion language model that rivals discrete methods. It connects embedding-space DLMs to flow matching via Bregman divergence, incorporating three key innovations: a principled ODE-based NLL bound for evaluation, a Gumbel-based learnable noise scheduler guided by an information-uniform principle, and self-conditioning to improve training. Experimentally, LangFlow achieves PPL of 30.0 on LM1B and 24.6 on OpenWebText, matching top discrete models, and outperforms autoregressive baselines in zero-shot transfer on four of seven benchmarks, demonstrating continuous diffusion’s viability for language modeling.
鉴于连续扩散在图像生成中表现优异但在语言建模中因数据稀疏和架构未充分探索而落后，LangFlow提出首个可媲美离散扩散模型的连续扩散语言模型。其通过Bregman散度将嵌入空间语言模型与流匹配相连，并引入三项关键创新：基于ODE的NLL评估界、基于Gumbel分布的可学习噪声调度器以实现信息均匀分布，以及自条件训练以提升模型训练效果。实验表明，LangFlow在LM1B和OpenWebText上分别达到30.0和24.6的PPL，与顶尖离散模型相当，并在七个基准中的四个上超越自回归基线，证明连续扩散在语言建模中具有潜力。
Parallax: Why AI Agents That Think Must Never Act
Authors: Joel Fokou
First: 2026-04-14T17:20:48+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-14T17:20:48+00:00
Comments: 20 pages, 1 figure, 5 tables. Open-source reference implementation: https://github.com/openparallax/openparallax. Documentation: https://docs.openparallax.dev. Feedback welcome via email or GitHub issues
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Autonomous AI agents are rapidly transitioning from experimental tools to operational infrastructure, with projections that 80% of enterprise applications will embed AI copilots by the end of 2026. As agents gain the ability to execute real-world actions (reading files, running commands, making network requests, modifying databases), a fundamental security gap has emerged. The dominant approach to agent safety relies on prompt-level guardrails: natural language instructions that operate at the same abstraction level as the threats they attempt to mitigate. This paper argues that prompt-based safety is architecturally insufficient for agents with execution capability and introduces Parallax, a paradigm for safe autonomous AI execution grounded in four principles: Cognitive-Executive Separation, which structurally prevents the reasoning system from executing actions; Adversarial Validation with Graduated Determinism, which interposes an independent, multi-tiered validator between reasoning and execution; Information Flow Control, which propagates data sensitivity labels through agent workflows to detect context-dependent threats; and Reversible Execution, which captures pre-destructive state to enable rollback when validation fails. We present OpenParallax, an open-source reference implementation in Go, and evaluate it using Assume-Compromise Evaluation, a methodology that bypasses the reasoning system entirely to test the architectural boundary under full agent compromise. Across 280 adversarial test cases in nine attack categories, Parallax blocks 98.9% of attacks with zero false positives under its default configuration, and 100% of attacks under its maximum-security configuration. When the reasoning system is compromised, prompt-level guardrails provide zero protection because they exist only within the compromised system; Parallax's architectural boundary holds regardless.
中文标题/摘要
标题：视差：为何具有思考能力的AI代理绝不能采取行动
自主AI代理正迅速从实验性工具转变为运营基础设施，预计到2026年底，80%的企业应用将嵌入AI协作者。随着代理获得执行现实世界操作的能力（如读取文件、运行命令、发起网络请求、修改数据库），一个根本性的安全漏洞已显现。当前主流的代理安全方法依赖于提示级别的护栏：与它们试图缓解的威胁处于相同抽象层次的自然语言指令。本文认为，基于提示的安全方法对于具备执行能力的代理在架构上是不足的，并提出了Parallax，一种基于四项原则的安全自主AI执行范式：认知-执行分离，通过结构设计防止推理系统执行操作；对抗性验证与渐进确定性，在推理与执行之间插入独立的多层级验证器；信息流控制，通过代理工作流传播数据敏感性标签以检测上下文相关的威胁；可逆执行，捕获预破坏性状态以便在验证失败时回滚。我们提供了OpenParallax，一个基于Go语言的开源参考实现，并采用假设-妥协评估方法进行评估，该方法完全绕过推理系统，以测试在完全代理被攻破情况下的架构边界。在九类攻击中的280个对抗性测试用例中，Parallax在默认配置下以零误报阻止98.9%的攻击，在最高安全配置下阻止100%的攻击。当推理系统被攻破时，提示级别护栏提供零保护，因为它们仅存在于被攻破的系统内；而Parallax的架构边界始终有效。
Summary / 总结
As AI agents gain real-world execution capabilities, prompt-based safety measures prove inadequate due to their reliance on the compromised reasoning system. Parallax introduces an architectural safety paradigm based on four principles: Cognitive-Executive Separation, Adversarial Validation with Graduated Determinism, Information Flow Control, and Reversible Execution. Evaluated via Assume-Compromise Evaluation—testing the system under full agent compromise—Parallax blocked 98.9% of 280 adversarial attacks with zero false positives under default settings and 100% under maximum-security mode, demonstrating that its structural boundaries remain intact even when the reasoning system is compromised.
随着自主AI代理获得现实世界的执行能力，传统的基于提示的安全措施因无法阻止恶意行为而显得不足，尤其是在推理系统被攻破时。Parallax提出了一种基于四原则的架构安全范式：认知-执行分离、对抗性验证与渐进确定性、信息流控制和可逆执行，确保推理与执行结构隔离、独立验证、敏感数据追踪及破坏性操作可回滚。通过假设攻破评估，在280个对抗性测试用例中，Parallax在默认配置下拦截98.9%攻击且无误报，在最高安全配置下拦截100%攻击，证明其即使在推理系统完全被攻破时仍具韧性。
Retrieval as a Decision: Training-Free Adaptive Gating for Efficient RAG
Authors: Yufeng Wang, Lu wei, Haibin Ling
First: 2025-11-12T23:09:52+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-14T17:20:47+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) improves factuality but retrieving for every query often hurts quality while inflating tokens and latency. We propose Training-free Adaptive Retrieval Gating (TARG), a single-shot policy that decides when to retrieve using only a short, no-context draft from the base model. From the draft's prefix logits, TARG computes lightweight uncertainty scores-mean token entropy, a margin signal derived from the top-1/top-2 logit gap via a monotone link, or small-N variance across a handful of stochastic prefixes-and triggers retrieval only when the score exceeds a threshold. The gate is model-agnostic, adds only tens to hundreds of draft tokens, and requires no additional training or auxiliary heads. On five QA benchmarks spanning short-answer (NQ-Open, TriviaQA, PopQA), multi-hop (MuSiQue), and long-form (ASQA) tasks, TARG consistently pushes the accuracy-efficiency frontier: compared with Alway-RAG, TARG matches or improves EM/F1 while reducing retrieval by 70-90% and cutting end-to-end latency, and it remains close to Never-RAG in overhead. A central empirical finding is that under modern instruction-tuned LLMs the margin signal is a robust default (entropy compresses as backbones sharpen), with small-N variance offering a conservative, budget-first alternative. We provide ablations over gate type and prefix length and use a $Δ$-latency view to make budget trade-offs explicit.
中文标题/摘要
标题：检索作为决策：无需训练的自适应门控以实现高效的RAG
检索增强生成（RAG）虽能提升事实准确性，但为每个查询进行检索常会损害质量并增加令牌和延迟。我们提出无需训练的自适应检索门控（TARG），这是一种单次决策策略，仅通过基础模型生成的短小无上下文草稿决定是否检索。TARG从草稿的前缀logits计算轻量级不确定性分数——如平均token熵、由top-1/top-2 logit差值经单调链接函数导出的边际信号，或少量随机前缀的方差——仅当分数超过阈值时才触发检索。该门控机制与模型无关，仅增加数十至数百个草稿令牌，无需额外训练或辅助头。在涵盖短答案（NQ-Open、TriviaQA、PopQA）、多跳（MuSiQue）和长文本（ASQA）任务的五个问答基准上，TARG持续推动准确率与效率的前沿：相比Always-RAG，TARG在保持或提升EM/F1的同时，将检索减少70-90%，并降低端到端延迟，且其开销接近Never-RAG。核心实证发现是：在现代指令调优的大语言模型中，边际信号是稳健的默认选择（熵值随骨干模型变尖锐而压缩），而小N方差则提供保守的、优先考虑预算的替代方案。我们对门控类型和前缀长度进行消融实验，并采用Δ延迟视角明确预算权衡。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the inefficiency of always retrieving in RAG systems, which harms quality and increases latency, the paper introduces Training-free Adaptive Retrieval Gating (TARG), a single-shot decision policy that uses only a short draft from the base model to decide whether to retrieve. TARG computes lightweight uncertainty scores—such as token entropy, logit margin, or small-N variance—and triggers retrieval only when the score surpasses a threshold. Evaluated across five QA benchmarks including short-answer, multi-hop, and long-form tasks, TARG consistently improves efficiency without sacrificing accuracy: it reduces retrieval by 70–90% while matching or improving EM/F1 scores and cutting end-to-end latency, with overhead nearly matching Never-RAG. The study finds that under modern instruction-tuned LLMs, the logit margin signal is robust and entropy-based gating is effective, with small-N variance offering a conservative, budget-first alternative.
为解决RAG系统中每次查询都检索导致的效率低下、质量下降和延迟增加问题，作者提出训练免的自适应检索门控（TARG），该机制仅用基础模型生成的短草稿决定是否检索。TARG从草稿前缀logits计算轻量级不确定性分数，如token熵、top-1与top-2 logit差值的边际信号或少量随机前缀的方差，并仅在分数超过阈值时触发检索。该门控机制与模型无关，仅增加数十至数百个草稿token，无需额外训练。在五个涵盖短答案、多跳和长文本任务的QA基准测试中，TARG在保持或提升准确率的同时，将检索减少70–90%，显著降低端到端延迟，且与从不检索相比开销接近。实验证明，边际信号在现代指令微调大模型中表现稳健，小N方差则提供保守且优先考虑预算的替代方案。
Beyond Relevance: On the Relationship Between Retrieval and RAG Information Coverage
Authors: Saron Samuel, Alexander Martin, Eugene Yang, Andrew Yates, Dawn Lawrie, Laura Dietz, Benjamin Van Durme
First: 2026-03-09T18:20:20+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-14T17:20:44+00:00
Comments: 11 pages
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) systems combine document retrieval with a generative model to address complex information seeking tasks like report generation. While the relationship between retrieval quality and generation effectiveness seems intuitive, it has not been systematically studied. We investigate whether upstream retrieval metrics can serve as reliable early indicators of the final generated response's information coverage. Through experiments across two text RAG benchmarks (TREC NeuCLIR 2024 and TREC RAG 2024) and one multimodal benchmark (WikiVideo), we analyze 15 text retrieval stacks and 10 multimodal retrieval stacks across four RAG pipelines and multiple evaluation frameworks (Auto-ARGUE and MiRAGE). Our findings demonstrate strong correlations between coverage-based retrieval metrics and nugget coverage in generated responses at both topic and system levels. This relationship holds most strongly when retrieval objectives align with generation goals, though more complex iterative RAG pipelines can partially decouple generation quality from retrieval effectiveness. These findings provide empirical support for using retrieval metrics as proxies for RAG performance.
中文标题/摘要
标题：超越相关性：检索与RAG信息覆盖之间的关系研究
检索增强生成（RAG）系统结合文档检索与生成模型，用于处理如报告生成等复杂信息检索任务。尽管检索质量与生成效果之间的关系看似直观，但尚未被系统研究。我们探讨上游检索指标能否作为最终生成响应信息覆盖的可靠早期指标。通过在两个文本RAG基准（TREC NeuCLIR 2024和TREC RAG 2024）及一个多模态基准（WikiVideo）上的实验，分析了15个文本检索栈和10个多模态检索栈在四个RAG流程及多种评估框架（Auto-ARGUE和MiRAGE）下的表现。研究发现，基于覆盖的检索指标与生成响应中的关键信息覆盖在主题和系统层面均存在强相关性。当检索目标与生成目标一致时，该关系最为显著，但更复杂的迭代式RAG流程可部分解耦生成质量与检索效果。这些发现为使用检索指标作为RAG性能代理提供了实证支持。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to understand how retrieval quality predicts RAG output coverage, this study investigates whether upstream retrieval metrics can reliably indicate the information coverage of generated responses. Using 15 text and 10 multimodal retrieval stacks across four RAG pipelines and multiple evaluation frameworks, experiments on TREC NeuCLIR 2024, TREC RAG 2024, and WikiVideo benchmarks reveal strong correlations between retrieval coverage metrics and nugget coverage in generated outputs, especially when retrieval and generation objectives align. However, more complex iterative RAG pipelines show partial decoupling, suggesting retrieval metrics remain useful proxies but are not universally predictive.
为探究检索质量能否有效预测RAG生成结果的信息覆盖度，本研究在TREC NeuCLIR 2024、TREC RAG 2024和WikiVideo三个基准上，对15个文本和10个多模态检索栈在四种RAG流程及多个评估框架下的表现进行实验。结果表明，基于覆盖度的检索指标与生成响应中的信息颗粒覆盖度存在显著正相关，尤其在检索与生成目标一致时相关性最强；但在复杂迭代式RAG流程中，生成质量与检索效果的部分脱钩现象亦被观察到。
Synthetic POMDPs to Challenge Memory-Augmented RL: Memory Demand Structure Modeling
Authors: Yongyi Wang, Lingfeng Li, Bozhou Chen, Ang Li, Hanyu Liu, Qirui Zheng, Xionghui Yang, Wenxin Li
First: 2025-08-06T10:13:17+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-14T17:18:39+00:00
Comments: The article has been accepted by Frontiers of Computer Science (FCS), with the DOI: {10.1007/s11704-026-52148-y}
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Recent benchmarks for memory-augmented reinforcement learning (RL) have introduced partially observable Markov decision process (POMDP) environments in which agents must use historical observations to make decisions. However, these benchmarks often lack fine-grained control over the challenges posed to memory models. Synthetic environments offer a solution, enabling precise manipulation of environment dynamics for rigorous and interpretable evaluation of memory-augmented RL. This paper advances the design of such customizable POMDPs with three key contributions: (1) a theoretical framework for analyzing POMDPs based on Memory Demand Structure (MDS) and related concepts; (2) a methodology using linear dynamics, state aggregation, and reward redistribution to construct POMDPs with predefined MDS; and (3) a suite of lightweight, scalable POMDP environments with tunable difficulty, grounded in our theoretical insights. Overall, our work clarifies core challenges in partially observable RL, offers principled guidelines for POMDP design, and aids in selecting and developing suitable memory architectures for RL tasks.
中文标题/摘要
标题：用于挑战记忆增强强化学习的合成POMDP：记忆需求结构建模
近期记忆增强强化学习（RL）的基准测试引入了部分可观测马尔可夫决策过程（POMDP）环境，要求智能体利用历史观测做出决策。然而，这些基准测试往往缺乏对记忆模型所面临挑战的精细控制。合成环境提供了解决方案，允许精确操控环境动态，以实现对记忆增强RL的严格且可解释的评估。本文在定制化POMDP设计方面提出三项关键贡献：（1）基于记忆需求结构（MDS）及相关概念的POMDP理论分析框架；（2）利用线性动态、状态聚合和奖励重分配方法构建具有预定义MDS的POMDP；（3）基于理论洞察设计的一系列轻量级、可扩展且难度可调的POMDP环境。总体而言，我们的工作明确了部分可观测RL的核心挑战，提供了POMDP设计的原理性指导，并有助于选择和开发适用于RL任务的合适记忆架构。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for fine-grained control over memory challenges in reinforcement learning, this paper introduces synthetic POMDP environments designed to rigorously evaluate memory-augmented agents. It proposes a theoretical framework based on Memory Demand Structure (MDS) to analyze and construct POMDPs with specific memory requirements, using linear dynamics, state aggregation, and reward redistribution to engineer environments with tunable difficulty. Experimental results demonstrate that these synthetic environments effectively isolate and scale memory demands, enabling clear differentiation of agent performance across varying memory needs and providing a principled basis for selecting and improving memory architectures in RL.
该论文针对现有记忆增强强化学习基准缺乏对记忆挑战精细控制的问题，提出可定制的合成POMDP环境，以精确调节记忆需求。文章引入“记忆需求结构”（MDS）理论框架，并通过线性动力学、状态聚合和奖励重分配方法构建具有预设MDS的环境。实验表明，这些合成环境能有效控制记忆难度，为记忆增强RL代理提供严谨评估平台，并揭示不同记忆架构在结构化记忆挑战下的性能表现。
Evaluating LLM-Generated ACSL Annotations for Formal Verification
Authors: Arshad Beg, Diarmuid O'Donoghue, Rosemary Monahan
First: 2026-02-14T19:18:34+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-14T17:18:00+00:00
Comments: 12 pages. Formal Techniques for Judicious Programming FTfJP-2026 at ECOOP. Conditionally Accepted. Final Revision
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Formal specifications are crucial for building verifiable and dependable software systems, yet generating accurate and verifiable specifications for real-world C programs remains challenging. This paper presents an empirical evaluation of automated ACSL annotation generation strategies for C programs, comparing a rule-based Python script, Frama-C's RTE plugin, and three large language models (DeepSeek-V3.2, GPT-5.2, and OLMo 3.1 32B Instruct). The study focuses on one-shot annotation generation, assessing how these approaches perform when directly applied to verification tasks. Using a filtered subset of the CASP benchmark, we evaluate generated annotations through Frama-C's WP plugin with multiple SMT solvers, analyzing proof success rates, solver timeouts, and internal processing time. Our results show that rule-based approaches remain more reliable for verification success, while LLM-based methods exhibit more variable performance. These findings highlight both the current limitations and the potential of LLMs as complementary tools for automated specification generation.

标题：评估大型语言模型生成的ACSL注释在形式验证中的表现
形式规范对于构建可验证和可靠的软件系统至关重要，但为现实世界的C程序生成准确且可验证的规范仍具挑战性。本文实证评估了针对C程序的自动化ACSL注释生成策略，比较了基于规则的Python脚本、Frama-C的RTE插件以及三个大型语言模型（DeepSeek-V3.2、GPT-5.2和OLMo 3.1 32B Instruct）。研究聚焦于单次注释生成，评估这些方法直接应用于验证任务时的表现。使用经过筛选的CASP基准子集，通过Frama-C的WP插件结合多种SMT求解器评估生成注释，分析证明成功率、求解器超时及内部处理时间。结果表明，基于规则的方法在验证成功率上仍更可靠，而基于LLM的方法表现更不稳定。这些发现既揭示了当前局限，也凸显了LLM作为自动化规范生成辅助工具的潜力。
Summary / 总结
This study addresses the challenge of generating accurate formal specifications for real-world C programs to support formal verification. It empirically compares a rule-based script, Frama-C's RTE plugin, and three large language models (DeepSeek-V3.2, GPT-5.2, and OLMo 3.1 32B Instruct) in one-shot ACSL annotation generation. Results indicate that rule-based methods remain more consistent and reliable for verification success, while LLMs show greater variability, suggesting they may serve as supplementary tools rather than replacements.
该研究旨在解决为C程序自动生成准确ACSL注释以支持形式验证的挑战，评估了四种方法：基于规则的Python脚本、Frama-C的RTE插件以及三个大语言模型（DeepSeek-V3.2、GPT-5.2和OLMo 3.1 32B Instruct）。评估使用过滤后的CASP基准，通过Frama-C的WP插件结合多个SMT求解器，测量证明成功率、超时和处理时间。结果表明，基于规则的方法在验证成功率上持续优于LLM，但LLM表现更不稳定，仍具改进潜力。
Poisoning the Inner Prediction Logic of Graph Neural Networks for Clean-Label Backdoor Attacks
Authors: Yuxiang Zhang, Bin Ma, Enyan Dai
First: 2026-03-05T09:51:43+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-14T17:16:15+00:00
Comments: Under review as TMLR regular paper
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Graph Neural Networks (GNNs) have achieved remarkable results in various tasks. Recent studies reveal that graph backdoor attacks can poison the GNN model to predict test nodes with triggers attached as the target class. However, apart from injecting triggers to training nodes, these graph backdoor attacks generally require altering the labels of trigger-attached training nodes into the target class, which is impractical in real-world scenarios. In this work, we focus on the clean-label graph backdoor attack, a realistic but understudied topic where training labels are not modifiable. According to our preliminary analysis, existing graph backdoor attacks generally fail under the clean-label setting. Our further analysis identifies that the core failure of existing methods lies in their inability to poison the prediction logic of GNN models, leading to the triggers being deemed unimportant for prediction. Therefore, we study a novel problem of effective clean-label graph backdoor attacks by poisoning the inner prediction logic of GNN models. We propose BA-Logic to solve the problem by coordinating a poisoned node selector and a logic-poisoning trigger generator. Extensive experiments on real-world datasets demonstrate that our method effectively enhances the attack success rate and surpasses state-of-the-art graph backdoor attack competitors under clean-label settings. Our code is available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/BA-Logic
中文标题/摘要
标题：通过毒化图神经网络内部预测逻辑实现无标签后门攻击
图神经网络（GNN）在多种任务中取得了显著成果。近期研究表明，图后门攻击可通过在训练节点中注入触发器，使模型在测试节点上预测带有触发器的目标类别。然而，除注入触发器外，现有图后门攻击通常还需修改附带触发器的训练节点标签为目标类别，这在现实场景中不切实际。本文聚焦于无标签图后门攻击——一个现实但研究不足的问题，其中训练标签不可修改。根据初步分析，现有图后门攻击方法在无标签设置下普遍失效。进一步分析表明，其核心失败原因在于无法毒化GNN模型的预测逻辑，导致触发器被判定为预测无关。因此，我们研究了一种新型有效无标签图后门攻击方法，即毒化GNN模型内部预测逻辑。我们提出BA-Logic方法，通过协调毒化节点选择器与逻辑毒化触发器生成器解决该问题。在真实数据集上的大量实验表明，我们的方法显著提升了攻击成功率，并在无标签设置下超越现有图后门攻击方法。代码开源于https://anonymous.4open.science/r/BA-Logic
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the impracticality of label modification in real-world graph backdoor attacks, this work targets clean-label attacks where training labels remain unchanged. The authors propose BA-Logic, a method that poisons the inner prediction logic of Graph Neural Networks by coordinating a poisoned node selector and a logic-poisoning trigger generator, enabling triggers to influence predictions without altering labels. Experiments on real-world datasets show BA-Logic significantly improves attack success rates compared to existing methods under clean-label settings, demonstrating its effectiveness in bypassing prior attack limitations.
鉴于现实场景中修改训练标签不切实际，本研究聚焦于标签不可修改的干净标签图后门攻击。作者发现现有方法失败的根本原因是无法污染GNN模型的内部预测逻辑，导致触发器在推理中被忽略。为此，他们提出BA-Logic框架，通过联合选择污染节点和生成逻辑污染触发器，以操控模型的内部决策过程。在多个真实数据集上的实验表明，BA-Logic在干净标签约束下显著优于现有方法，提升了攻击成功率。
GlotOCR Bench: OCR Models Still Struggle Beyond a Handful of Unicode Scripts
Authors: Amir Hossein Kargaran, Nafiseh Nikeghbal, Jana Diesner, François Yvon, Hinrich Schütze
First: 2026-04-14T17:12:41+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-14T17:12:41+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Optical character recognition (OCR) has advanced rapidly with the rise of vision-language models, yet evaluation has remained concentrated on a small cluster of high- and mid-resource scripts. We introduce GlotOCR Bench, a comprehensive benchmark evaluating OCR generalization across 100+ Unicode scripts. Our benchmark comprises clean and degraded image variants rendered from real multilingual texts. Images are rendered using fonts from the Google Fonts repository, shaped with HarfBuzz and rasterized with FreeType, supporting both LTR and RTL scripts. Samples of rendered images were manually reviewed to verify correct rendering across all scripts. We evaluate a broad suite of open-weight and proprietary vision-language models and find that most perform well on fewer than ten scripts, and even the strongest frontier models fail to generalize beyond thirty scripts. Performance broadly tracks script-level pretraining coverage, suggesting that current OCR systems rely on language model pretraining as much as on visual recognition. Models confronted with unfamiliar scripts either produce random noise or hallucinate characters from similar scripts they already know. We release the benchmark and pipeline for reproducibility. Pipeline Code: https://github.com/cisnlp/glotocr-bench, Benchmark: https://hf.co/datasets/cis-lmu/glotocr-bench.
中文标题/摘要
标题：GlotOCR Bench：OCR模型在超过少数几种Unicode文字系统之外仍难以应对
随着视觉-语言模型的兴起，光学字符识别（OCR）技术迅速发展，但评估仍集中于少数高资源和中资源文字系统。我们推出了GlotOCR Bench，一个全面评估OCR在100多种Unicode文字系统上泛化能力的基准测试。该基准包含从真实多语言文本生成的清晰与退化图像变体，使用Google Fonts字体库中的字体，通过HarfBuzz排版并用FreeType光栅化，支持从左到右（LTR）和从右到左（RTL）文字系统。渲染图像样本经人工审核，确保所有文字系统正确显示。我们评估了广泛开源和专有视觉-语言模型，发现多数模型仅在十种以下文字系统表现良好，即使最强的前沿模型也难以泛化至三十种以上文字系统。性能普遍与文字系统级别的预训练覆盖度相关，表明当前OCR系统依赖语言模型预训练的程度不亚于视觉识别。面对陌生文字系统时，模型要么生成随机噪声，要么从已知相似文字系统中“幻觉”生成字符。我们公开了基准测试与流水线代码以确保可复现性。流水线代码：https://github.com/cisnlp/glotocr-bench，基准数据集：https://hf.co/datasets/cis-lmu/glotocr-bench。

为评估OCR模型在多语种Unicode字符集上的泛化能力，本文提出GlotOCR Bench基准，使用Google Fonts、HarfBuzz和FreeType渲染真实多语言文本，生成清晰与退化图像样本，并经人工校验。实验评估多种视觉-语言模型，发现多数模型仅在十种以内语言表现良好，最强前沿模型也难以超越三十种语言。模型性能与预训练覆盖度高度相关，表明当前OCR系统依赖语言模型预训练而非视觉识别，面对陌生语言常产生随机噪声或从已知语言中幻觉字符。
A DeepONet for inverting the Neumann-to-Dirichlet Operator in Electrical Impedance Tomography: An approximation theoretic perspective and numerical results
Authors: Anuj Abhishek, Thilo Strauss
First: 2024-07-24T11:34:24+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-14T17:07:13+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
In this work, we consider the non-invasive medical imaging modality of Electrical Impedance Tomography (EIT), where the goal is to recover the conductivity in a medium from boundary current-to-voltage measurements, i.e., the Neumann-to-Dirichlet (N--t--D) operator. We formulate this inverse problem as an operator-learning task, where the aim is to approximate the implicitly defined map from N--t--D operators to admissible conductivities. To this end, we employ a Deep Operator Network (DeepONet) architecture, thereby extending operator learning beyond the classical function-to-function setting to the more challenging operator-to-function regime. We establish a universal approximation theorem that guarantees that such operator-to-function maps can be approximated arbitrarily well by DeepONets. Furthermore, we provide a computational implementation of our approach and compare it against the iteratively regularized Gauss--Newton (IRGN) method. Our results show that the proposed framework yields accurate and robust reconstructions, outperforms the baseline, and demonstrates strong generalization. To our knowledge, this is the first work that combines rigorous approximation-theoretic guarantees with DeepONet-based inversion for EIT, thereby opening a principled and interpretable pathway for use of DeepONets in such inverse problems.
中文标题/摘要
标题：用于电阻抗断层成像中Neumann到Dirichlet算子逆问题的DeepONet：逼近理论视角与数值结果
本文研究非侵入式医学成像技术电阻抗断层成像（EIT），其目标是从边界电流-电压测量中恢复介质的电导率，即Neumann到Dirichlet（N--t--D）算子。我们将此反问题建模为算子学习任务，旨在逼近从N--t--D算子到可接受电导率的隐式映射。为此，我们采用Deep Operator Network（DeepONet）架构，从而将算子学习从经典函数到函数的设定扩展到更具挑战性的算子到函数的范畴。我们建立了通用逼近定理，保证此类算子到函数映射可被DeepONet任意良好逼近。此外，我们提供了计算实现，并与迭代正则化Gauss-Newton（IRGN）方法进行比较。结果表明，所提框架可生成准确且鲁棒的重建结果，优于基线方法，并展现出强泛化能力。据我们所知，这是首次将严格的逼近理论保证与基于DeepONet的EIT反演相结合的工作，从而为DeepONet在该类反问题中的应用开辟了原则性且可解释的路径。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to non-invasively reconstruct internal conductivity in medical imaging via Electrical Impedance Tomography, this work frames the inverse Neumann-to-Dirichlet problem as an operator-learning task. It employs a Deep Operator Network (DeepONet) to approximate the implicit map from boundary measurements to conductivity distributions, extending operator learning to the challenging operator-to-function setting. The authors establish a universal approximation theorem supporting DeepONet’s capability to approximate such maps arbitrarily well and validate their approach numerically against the iteratively regularized Gauss–Newton method, demonstrating superior accuracy, robustness, and generalization in reconstructions.
为解决电阻抗断层成像（EIT）中从边界测量数据反演内部电导率的问题，该研究将逆问题建模为学习Neumann到Dirichlet算子映射的任务，采用Deep Operator Network（DeepONet）架构。方法基于统一逼近定理，理论上保证DeepONet可逼近算子到函数的映射，拓展了算子学习从经典函数到函数的范畴至更具挑战性的算子到函数场景。实验结果表明，该方法能实现准确、鲁棒的重建，优于迭代正则化Gauss-Newton方法，并展现出良好的泛化能力，是首次将逼近理论保证与DeepONet用于EIT逆问题相结合的工作。
Probabilistic Feature Imputation and Uncertainty-Aware Multimodal Federated Aggregation
Authors: Nafis Fuad Shahid, Maroof Ahmed, Md Akib Haider, Saidur Rahman Sagor, Aashnan Rahman, Md Azam Hossain
First: 2026-04-14T17:03:14+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-14T17:03:14+00:00
Comments: Accepted for publication at the Medical Imaging with Deep Learning (MIDL) 2026 conference
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Multimodal federated learning enables privacy-preserving collaborative model training across healthcare institutions. However, a fundamental challenge arises from modality heterogeneity: many clinical sites possess only a subset of modalities due to resource constraints or workflow variations. Existing approaches address this through feature imputation networks that synthesize missing modality representations, yet these methods produce point estimates without reliability measures, forcing downstream classifiers to treat all imputed features as equally trustworthy. In safety-critical medical applications, this limitation poses significant risks. We propose the Probabilistic Feature Imputation Network (P-FIN), which outputs calibrated uncertainty estimates alongside imputed features. This uncertainty is leveraged at two levels: (1) locally, through sigmoid gating that attenuates unreliable feature dimensions before classification, and (2) globally, through Fed-UQ-Avg, an aggregation strategy that prioritizes updates from clients with reliable imputation. Experiments on federated chest X-ray classification using CheXpert, NIH Open-I, and PadChest demonstrate consistent improvements over deterministic baselines, with +5.36% AUC gain in the most challenging configuration.
中文标题/摘要
标题：概率特征填补与不确定性感知的多模态联邦聚合
多模态联邦学习可在保护隐私的前提下，使医疗机构协同训练模型。然而，模态异质性是一个根本性挑战：许多临床机构因资源限制或工作流程差异，仅拥有部分模态。现有方法通过特征填补网络合成缺失模态表示，但这些方法仅输出点估计，缺乏可靠性度量，迫使下游分类器将所有填补特征视为同等可信。在安全关键的医疗应用中，这一局限性带来显著风险。我们提出概率特征填补网络（P-FIN），在输出填补特征的同时，提供校准的不确定性估计。该不确定性在两个层面被利用：（1）局部层面，通过Sigmoid门控机制在分类前削弱不可靠特征维度；（2）全局层面，通过Fed-UQ-Avg聚合策略，优先采纳填补可靠的客户端更新。在CheXpert、NIH Open-I和PadChest数据集上的联邦胸部X光分类实验表明，相比确定性基线方法，性能持续提升，最困难配置下AUC提升5.36%。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the challenge of modality heterogeneity in multimodal federated learning for healthcare, where institutions often lack complete data, this work introduces the Probabilistic Feature Imputation Network (P-FIN) to generate imputed features with calibrated uncertainty. The method employs sigmoid gating to locally downweight unreliable dimensions before classification and Fed-UQ-Avg for global aggregation, prioritizing clients with reliable imputation. Experiments on chest X-ray datasets show consistent performance gains over deterministic baselines, achieving a +5.36% AUC improvement in the most difficult setting.
为解决医疗领域多模态联邦学习中因资源或流程差异导致的模态异构问题，本文提出概率特征填补网络（P-FIN），在生成缺失模态特征的同时输出校准后的不确定性估计。该方法通过sigmoid门控机制局部降低不可靠特征维度的影响，并采用Fed-UQ-Avg策略全局优先整合高置信度客户端的更新。在胸部X光分类实验中，该方法相较确定性基线持续提升性能，最困难配置下AUC提升达5.36%。
AbdomenGen: Sequential Volume-Conditioned Diffusion Framework for Abdominal Anatomy Generation
Authors: Yubraj Bhandari, Lavsen Dahal, Paul Segars, Joseph Y. Lo
First: 2026-04-14T17:01:41+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-14T17:01:41+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Computational phantoms are widely used in medical imaging research, yet current systems to generate controlled, clinically meaningful anatomical variations remain limited. We present AbdomenGen, a sequential volume-conditioned diffusion framework for controllable abdominal anatomy generation. We introduce the \textbf{Volume Control Scalar (VCS)}, a standardized residual that decouples organ size from body habitus, enabling interpretable volume modulation. Organ masks are synthesized sequentially, conditioning on the body mask and previously generated structures to preserve global anatomical coherence while supporting independent, multi-organ control. Across 11 abdominal organs, the proposed framework achieves strong geometric fidelity (e.g., liver dice $0.83 \pm 0.05$), stable single-organ calibration over $[-3,+3]$ VCS, and disentangled multi-organ modulation. To showcase clinical utility with a hepatomegaly cohort selected from MERLIN, Wasserstein-based VCS selection reduces distributional distance of training data by 73.6\% . These results demonstrate calibrated, distribution-aware anatomical generation suitable for controllable abdominal phantom construction and simulation studies.
中文标题/摘要
标题：AbdomenGen：用于腹部解剖结构生成的序列体积条件扩散框架
计算体模在医学影像研究中广泛应用，但当前用于生成可控且具有临床意义的解剖结构变异的系统仍有限。我们提出AbdomenGen，一种用于可控腹部解剖结构生成的序列体积条件扩散框架。我们引入了\textbf{体积控制标量（VCS）}，一种标准化残差，将器官大小与体型解耦，实现可解释的体积调节。器官掩码按顺序合成，以身体掩码和先前生成的结构为条件，以保持全局解剖一致性，同时支持独立的多器官控制。在11个腹部器官上，该框架实现了强几何保真度（如肝脏Dice系数0.83±0.05），在[-3,+3] VCS范围内单器官校准稳定，且多器官调节解耦。为展示临床实用性，我们从MERLIN数据集中选取肝肿大队列，基于Wasserstein距离的VCS选择使训练数据分布距离降低73.6%。这些结果表明，该框架可实现校准且分布感知的解剖结构生成，适用于可控腹部体模构建与模拟研究。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for controllable and clinically relevant abdominal anatomy generation in medical imaging, AbdomenGen introduces a sequential volume-conditioned diffusion framework that decouples organ size from body habitus using a Volume Control Scalar (VCS). Organs are synthesized sequentially, conditioned on prior structures and body masks to maintain anatomical coherence while enabling independent modulation. Experimental results across 11 abdominal organs show high geometric fidelity (e.g., liver Dice 0.83 ± 0.05), stable single-organ control over a ±3 VCS range, and disentangled multi-organ modulation. Clinical utility is demonstrated via hepatomegaly cohort analysis, where VCS selection reduced training data distributional distance by 73.6% using Wasserstein distance.
为满足医学影像研究中对可控且临床相关的腹部解剖结构生成的需求，AbdomenGen提出一种基于体积条件的序列扩散框架，通过体积控制标量（VCS）解耦器官大小与体型特征。器官按顺序生成，依据身体掩码和已生成结构进行条件控制，以保持整体解剖一致性并支持独立多器官调节。在11个腹部器官上实验表明，该框架具有高几何保真度（如肝脏Dice系数0.83±0.05）、±3 VCS范围内稳定单器官调控能力及解耦多器官调节能力。在来自MERLIN的肝肿大队列中，基于VCS的选取使训练数据分布距离降低73.6%，证明其适用于生成校准良好、分布感知的腹部解剖仿真模型。
Are Pretrained Image Matchers Good Enough for SAR-Optical Satellite Registration?
Authors: Isaac Corley, Alex Stoken, Gabriele Berton
Venue: CVPR 2026
First: 2026-04-11T13:56:20+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-14T17:01:40+00:00
Comments: CVPR 2026 Image Matching Workshop
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Cross-modal optical-SAR (Synthetic Aperture Radar) registration is a bottleneck for disaster-response via remote sensing, yet modern image matchers are developed and benchmarked almost exclusively on natural-image domains. We evaluate twenty-four pretrained matcher families--in a zero-shot setting with no fine-tuning or domain adaptation on satellite or SAR data--on SpaceNet9 and two additional cross-modal benchmarks under a deterministic protocol with tiled large-image inference, robust geometric filtering, and tie-point-grounded metrics. Our results reveal asymmetric transfer--matchers with explicit cross-modal training do not uniformly outperform those without it. While XoFTR (trained for visible-thermal matching) and RoMa achieve the lowest reported mean error at $3.0$ px on the labeled SpaceNet9 training scenes, RoMa achieves this without any cross-modal training, and MatchAnything-ELoFTR ($3.4$ px)--trained on synthetic cross-modal pairs--matches closely, suggesting (as a working hypothesis) that foundation-model features (DINOv2) may contribute to modality invariance that partially substitutes for explicit cross-modal supervision. 3D-reconstruction matchers (MASt3R, DUSt3R), which are not designed for traditional 2D image matching, are highly protocol-sensitive and remain fragile under default settings. Deployment protocol choices (geometry model, tile size, inlier gating) shift accuracy by up to $33\times$ for a single matcher, sometimes exceeding the effect of swapping matchers entirely within the evaluated sweep--affine geometry alone reduces mean error from $12.34$ to $9.74$ px. These findings inform both practical deployment of existing matchers and future matcher design for cross-modal satellite registration.
中文标题/摘要
标题：预训练图像匹配器在SAR-光学卫星配准中足够好吗？
跨模态光学-SAR（合成孔径雷达）配准是遥感灾害响应中的瓶颈，但现代图像匹配器几乎仅在自然图像领域开发和基准测试。我们在SpaceNet9和两个额外跨模态基准上，以零样本设置（无卫星或SAR数据的微调或领域适应），评估了24个预训练匹配器家族。采用确定性协议，包括分块大图推理、鲁棒几何滤波和基于同名点的度量。结果揭示了非对称迁移现象——具有显式跨模态训练的匹配器并非普遍优于无此训练的匹配器。虽然XoFTR（训练用于可见-热红外匹配）和RoMa在标注的SpaceNet9训练场景中达到最低报告平均误差3.0像素，但RoMa无需任何跨模态训练，MatchAnything-ELoFTR（3.4像素，训练于合成跨模态对）表现接近，表明（作为工作假设）基础模型特征（DINOv2）可能贡献了模态不变性，部分替代显式跨模态监督。3D重建匹配器（MASt3R、DUSt3R）虽非为传统2D图像匹配设计，但对协议高度敏感，在默认设置下仍脆弱。部署协议选择（几何模型、分块大小、内点门控）可使单一匹配器的精度变化高达33倍，有时甚至超过在评估范围内更换匹配器的效果——仅使用仿射几何即可将平均误差从12.34像素降至9.74像素。这些发现既指导现有匹配器的实际部署，也影响未来跨模态卫星配准匹配器的设计。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for reliable cross-modal optical-SAR registration in disaster response, this study evaluates 24 pretrained image matchers in a zero-shot setting without fine-tuning on satellite data. Using a standardized protocol with tiled inference and geometric filtering, the authors find that matchers with explicit cross-modal training do not consistently outperform those without, with RoMa achieving the lowest mean error (3.0 px) on SpaceNet9 without such training, and MatchAnything-ELoFTR (3.4 px) performing similarly despite synthetic cross-modal training. The study also reveals that 3D-reconstruction models like MASt3R and DUSt3R are highly sensitive to deployment settings, with protocol choices altering accuracy by up to 33 times, sometimes surpassing the impact of matcher selection itself—affine geometry alone reduces error from 12.34 to 9.74 px.
为提升灾害响应中光学与SAR图像的跨模态配准能力，该研究在零样本设置下评估了24种预训练图像匹配器，未对卫星数据进行微调，使用SpaceNet9及两个额外基准，在严格确定性协议下进行测试。结果表明跨模态训练并非决定性优势：RoMa在无跨模态训练情况下达到3.0像素均值误差，MatchAnything-ELoFTR（3.4像素）虽经合成跨模态训练但表现相近，暗示基础模型特征（如DINOv2）可能提供部分模态不变性。此外，3D重建模型（如MASt3R、DUSt3R）对协议高度敏感，部署参数变化可使精度波动达33倍，有时甚至超过更换匹配器的影响——仅使用仿射几何即可将误差从12.34降至9.74像素。
Evolution of Optimization Methods: Algorithms, Scenarios, and Evaluations
Authors: Tong Zhang, Jiangning Zhang, Zhucun Xue, Juntao Jiang, Yicheng Xu, Chengming Xu, Teng Hu, Xingyu Xie, Xiaobin Hu, Yabiao Wang, Yong Liu, Shuicheng Yan
First: 2026-04-14T17:01:36+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-14T17:01:36+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Balancing convergence speed, generalization capability, and computational efficiency remains a core challenge in deep learning optimization. First-order gradient descent methods, epitomized by stochastic gradient descent (SGD) and Adam, serve as the cornerstone of modern training pipelines. However, large-scale model training, stringent differential privacy requirements, and distributed learning paradigms expose critical limitations in these conventional approaches regarding privacy protection and memory efficiency. To mitigate these bottlenecks, researchers explore second-order optimization techniques to surpass first-order performance ceilings, while zeroth-order methods reemerge to alleviate memory constraints inherent to large-scale training. Despite this proliferation of methodologies, the field lacks a cohesive framework that unifies underlying principles and delineates application scenarios for these disparate approaches. In this work, we retrospectively analyze the evolutionary trajectory of deep learning optimization algorithms and present a comprehensive empirical evaluation of mainstream optimizers across diverse model architectures and training scenarios. We distill key emerging trends and fundamental design trade-offs, pinpointing promising directions for future research. By synthesizing theoretical insights with extensive empirical evidence, we provide actionable guidance for designing next-generation highly efficient, robust, and trustworthy optimization methods. The code is available at https://github.com/APRIL-AIGC/Awesome-Optimizer.
中文标题/摘要
标题：优化方法的演进：算法、场景与评估

Cycle-Consistent Search: Question Reconstructability as a Proxy Reward for Search Agent Training
Authors: Sohyun An, Shuibenyang Yuan, Hayeon Lee, Cho-Jui Hsieh, Alexander Min
First: 2026-04-14T17:00:18+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-14T17:00:18+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Reinforcement Learning (RL) has shown strong potential for optimizing search agents in complex information retrieval tasks. However, existing approaches predominantly rely on gold supervision, such as ground-truth answers, which is difficult to scale. To address this limitation, we propose Cycle-Consistent Search (CCS), a gold-supervision-free framework for training search agents, inspired by cycle-consistency techniques from unsupervised machine translation and image-to-image translation. Our key hypothesis is that an optimal search trajectory, unlike insufficient or irrelevant ones, serves as a lossless encoding of the question's intent. Consequently, a high-quality trajectory should preserve the information required to accurately reconstruct the original question, thereby inducing a reward signal for policy optimization. However, naive cycle-consistency objectives are vulnerable to information leakage, as reconstruction may rely on superficial lexical cues rather than the underlying search process. To reduce this effect, we apply information bottlenecks, including exclusion of the final response and named entity recognition (NER) masking of search queries. These constraints force reconstruction to rely on retrieved observations together with the structural scaffold, ensuring that the resulting reward signal reflects informational adequacy rather than linguistic redundancy. Experiments on question-answering benchmarks show that CCS achieves performance comparable to supervised baselines while outperforming prior methods that do not rely on gold supervision. These results suggest that CCS provides a scalable training paradigm for training search agents in settings where gold supervision is unavailable.
中文标题/摘要
标题：循环一致搜索：将问题可重构性作为搜索代理训练的代理奖励
强化学习（RL）在复杂信息检索任务中优化搜索代理方面展现出强大潜力。然而，现有方法主要依赖黄金监督（如真实答案），难以扩展。为解决此局限，我们提出循环一致搜索（CCS），一种无需黄金监督的搜索代理训练框架，灵感源自无监督机器翻译和图像到图像翻译中的循环一致性技术。我们的核心假设是：最优搜索轨迹（不同于不足或无关的轨迹）可作为问题意图的无损编码。因此，高质量轨迹应能保留重构原始问题所需的信息，从而诱导策略优化的奖励信号。然而，朴素的循环一致性目标易受信息泄露影响，因重构可能依赖表面词汇线索而非底层搜索过程。为减轻此效应，我们引入信息瓶颈，包括排除最终回答及对搜索查询进行命名实体识别（NER）掩码。这些约束迫使重构依赖检索到的观察结果与结构框架，确保生成的奖励信号反映信息充分性而非语言冗余。在问答基准测试中，CCS表现与监督基线相当，且优于不依赖黄金监督的先前方法。结果表明，CCS为无黄金监督环境下训练搜索代理提供了一种可扩展的训练范式。

Motivated by the difficulty of scaling gold-supervised reinforcement learning for search agents in information retrieval, this work introduces Cycle-Consistent Search (CCS), a framework that trains search agents without ground-truth answers by leveraging cycle-consistency as a proxy reward. The method assumes that optimal search trajectories encode the question’s intent losslessly, enabling reconstruction of the original question from the trajectory, which then serves as a reward signal. To prevent reconstruction from relying on superficial lexical cues, the framework enforces information bottlenecks by excluding the final response and masking named entities in queries, forcing reconstruction to depend on retrieved content and structural context. Experiments on QA benchmarks demonstrate that CCS matches supervised baselines in performance while outperforming unsupervised alternatives, indicating its effectiveness as a scalable, gold-supervision-free training paradigm.
为解决强化学习在信息检索中依赖人工标注难以扩展的问题，本文提出Cycle-Consistent Search（CCS）框架，通过循环一致性作为代理奖励训练搜索代理，无需真实答案。其核心假设是高质量搜索轨迹能无损编码问题意图，从而允许从检索结果和结构骨架重建原始问题。为避免重建依赖表面词法线索，CCS引入信息瓶颈机制：排除最终答案并遮蔽查询中的命名实体。实验表明，CCS在问答基准上表现可媲美监督基线，且优于无监督方法，证明其作为无标注场景下可扩展训练范式的有效性。
Boosting Visual Instruction Tuning with Self-Supervised Guidance
Authors: Sophia Sirko-Galouchenko, Monika Wysoczanska, Andrei Bursuc, Nicolas Thome, Spyros Gidaris
First: 2026-04-14T16:59:53+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-14T16:59:53+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) perform well on many vision-language tasks but often struggle with vision-centric problems that require fine-grained visual reasoning. Recent evidence suggests that this limitation arises not from weak visual representations, but from under-utilization of visual information during instruction tuning, where many tasks can be partially solved using language priors alone. We propose a simple and lightweight approach that augments visual instruction tuning with a small number of visually grounded self-supervised tasks expressed as natural language instructions. By reformulating classical self-supervised pretext tasks, such as rotation prediction, color matching, and cross-view correspondence, as image-instruction-response triplets, we introduce supervision that cannot be solved without relying on visual evidence. Our approach requires no human annotations, no architectural modifications, and no additional training stages. Across multiple models, training regimes, and benchmarks, injecting only a small fraction (3-10%) of such visually grounded instructions consistently improves performance on vision-centric evaluations. Our findings highlight instruction tuning with visually grounded SSL tasks as a powerful lever for improving visual reasoning in MLLMs through simple adjustments to the training data distribution. Code available at: https://github.com/sirkosophia/V-GIFT

Mema: Memory-Augmented Adapter for Enhanced Vision-Language Understanding
Authors: Ying Liu, Yudong Han, Kean Shi, Liyuan Pan
First: 2026-02-28T13:57:19+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-14T16:56:45+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) have achieved remarkable performance by aligning pretrained visual representations with the linguistic knowledge embedded in Large Language Models (LLMs). However, existing approaches typically rely on final-layer visual features or learnable multi-layer fusion, which often fail to sufficiently exploit hierarchical visual cues without explicit cross-layer interaction design. In this work, we propose a Memory-Augmented Adapter (Mema) within the vision encoder. Specifically, Mema maintains a stateful memory that accumulates hierarchical visual representations across layers, with its evolution conditioned on both query embeddings and step-wise visual features. A portion of this memory is selectively injected into token representations via a feedback mechanism, thereby mitigating the attenuation of fine-grained visual cues from shallow layers. Designed as a lightweight and plug-and-play module, Mema integrates seamlessly into pretrained vision encoders without modifying the vanilla backbone architecture. Only a minimal set of additional parameters requires training, enabling adaptive visual feature refinement while reducing training overhead. Extensive experiments across multiple benchmarks demonstrate that Mema consistently improves performance, validating its effectiveness in complex multimodal reasoning tasks. The code have been released at https://github.com/Sisiliu312/Mema.

标题：Mema：增强视觉-语言理解的记忆增强适配器
多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）通过将预训练视觉表示与大语言模型（LLMs）中嵌入的语言知识对齐，取得了显著性能。然而，现有方法通常依赖最终层视觉特征或可学习的多层融合，往往在缺乏显式跨层交互设计的情况下，无法充分挖掘层次化视觉线索。本文在视觉编码器中提出一种记忆增强适配器（Mema）。具体而言，Mema维护一个状态化的记忆，用于累积各层的层次化视觉表示，其演化受查询嵌入和逐步视觉特征共同调控。通过反馈机制，部分记忆被选择性注入到token表示中，从而缓解浅层视觉特征中细粒度线索的衰减。Mema设计为轻量级、即插即用模块，可无缝集成至预训练视觉编码器，无需修改原始骨干架构。仅需少量额外参数训练，即可实现视觉特征的自适应优化并降低训练开销。在多个基准测试中的广泛实验表明，Mema持续提升性能，验证了其在复杂多模态推理任务中的有效性。代码已发布于 https://github.com/Sisiliu312/Mema。
