Modeling Co-Pilots for Text-to-Model Translation
Authors: Serdar Kadioglu, Karthik Uppuluri, Akash Singirikonda
Venue: AAAI
First: 2026-04-14T16:51:29+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-14T16:51:29+00:00
Comments: AAAI'25 Bridge Program on Machine Learning and Operations Research
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
There is growing interest in leveraging large language models (LLMs) for text-to-model translation and optimization tasks. This paper aims to advance this line of research by introducing \textsc{Text2Model} and \textsc{Text2Zinc}. \textsc{Text2Model} is a suite of co-pilots based on several LLM strategies with varying complexity, along with an online leaderboard. \textsc{Text2Zinc} is a cross-domain dataset for capturing optimization and satisfaction problems specified in natural language, along with an interactive editor with built-in AI assistant. While there is an emerging literature on using LLMs for translating combinatorial problems into formal models, our work is the first attempt to integrate \textit{both} satisfaction and optimization problems within a \textit{unified architecture} and \textit{dataset}. Moreover, our approach is \textit{solver-agnostic} unlike existing work that focuses on translation to a solver-specific model. To achieve this, we leverage \textsc{MiniZinc}'s solver-and-paradigm-agnostic modeling capabilities to formulate combinatorial problems. We conduct comprehensive experiments to compare execution and solution accuracy across several single- and multi-call strategies, including; zero-shot prompting, chain-of-thought reasoning, intermediate representations via knowledge-graphs, grammar-based syntax encoding, and agentic approaches that decompose the model into sequential sub-tasks. Our co-pilot strategies are competitive, and in parts improve, recent research in this domain. Our findings indicate that while LLMs are promising they are not yet a push-button technology for combinatorial modeling. We contribute \textsc{Text2Model} co-pilots and leaderboard, and \textsc{Text2Zinc} and interactive editor to open-source to support closing this performance gap.

标题：文本到模型翻译的共操员建模
人们日益关注利用大型语言模型（LLM）进行文本到模型的翻译与优化任务。本文旨在推进该研究方向，提出了\textsc{Text2Model}和\textsc{Text2Zinc}。\textsc{Text2Model}是一套基于多种LLM策略（复杂度各异）的共操员工具，并附带在线排行榜。\textsc{Text2Zinc}是一个跨领域的数据集，用于捕捉以自然语言指定的优化与满足问题，并配备内置AI助手的交互式编辑器。尽管已有文献探讨利用LLM将组合问题翻译为形式化模型，但我们的工作是首次在统一架构与数据集中同时整合满足与优化问题。此外，我们的方法是\textit{求解器无关}的，不同于现有工作仅关注特定求解器的模型翻译。为实现这一点，我们利用\textsc{MiniZinc}的求解器与范式无关的建模能力来表述组合问题。我们进行了全面实验，比较了多种单次与多次调用策略（包括零样本提示、思维链推理、通过知识图谱的中间表示、基于语法的句法编码及将模型分解为顺序子任务的代理方法）的执行效率与解的准确性。我们的共操员策略具有竞争力，部分优于近期相关研究。研究发现，尽管LLM前景广阔，但尚未成为组合建模的“一键式”技术。我们贡献了开源的\textsc{Text2Model}共操员与排行榜，以及\textsc{Text2Zinc}和交互式编辑器，以支持缩小性能差距。

An Optimal Sauer Lemma Over $k$-ary Alphabets
Authors: Steve Hanneke, Qinglin Meng, Shay Moran, Amirreza Shaeiri
First: 2026-04-14T16:50:38+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-14T16:50:38+00:00
Comments: 38 pages
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
The Sauer-Shelah-Perles Lemma is a cornerstone of combinatorics and learning theory, bounding the size of a binary hypothesis class in terms of its Vapnik-Chervonenkis (VC) dimension. For classes of functions over a $k$-ary alphabet, namely the multiclass setting, the Natarajan dimension has long served as an analogue of VC dimension, yet the corresponding Sauer-type bounds are suboptimal for alphabet sizes $k>2$. In this work, we establish a sharp Sauer inequality for multiclass and list prediction. Our bound is expressed in terms of the Daniely--Shalev-Shwartz (DS) dimension, and more generally with its extension, the list-DS dimension -- the combinatorial parameters that characterize multiclass and list PAC learnability. Our bound is tight for every alphabet size $k$, list size $\ell$, and dimension value, replacing the exponential dependence on $\ell$ in the Natarajan-based bound by the optimal polynomial dependence, and improving the dependence on $k$ as well. Our proof uses the polynomial method. In contrast to the classical VC case, where several direct combinatorial proofs are known, we are not aware of any purely combinatorial proof in the DS setting. This motivates several directions for future research, which are discussed in the paper. As consequences, we obtain improved sample complexity upper bounds for list PAC learning and for uniform convergence of list predictors, sharpening the recent results of Charikar et al.~(STOC~2023), Hanneke et al.~(COLT~2024), and Brukhim et al.~(NeurIPS~2024).
中文标题/摘要
标题：k进制字母表上的最优Sauer引理
Sauer-Shelah-Perles引理是组合数学与学习理论的基石，它用Vapnik-Chervonenkis（VC）维数对二元假设类的大小进行界。对于k进制字母表上的函数类（即多类设置），Natarajan维数长期以来作为VC维数的类比，但其对应的Sauer型界在k>2时是次优的。 本工作中，我们为多类和列表预测建立了精确的Sauer不等式。我们的界用Daniely-Shalev-Shwartz（DS）维数及其扩展——列表DS维数表示，这些是刻画多类和列表PAC可学习性的组合参数。我们的界对任意字母表大小k、列表大小ℓ和维数值均紧致，将基于Natarajan的界中对ℓ的指数依赖替换为最优的多项式依赖，同时改进了对k的依赖。我们的证明使用多项式方法。与经典VC情形不同，后者有多个直接的组合证明，我们尚未发现DS情形下纯组合证明。这促使了若干未来研究方向，详见论文。 作为结果，我们获得了列表PAC学习和列表预测的均匀收敛的改进样本复杂度上界，精化了Charikar等（STOC 2023）、Hanneke等（COLT 2024）和Brukhim等（NeurIPS 2024）的近期结果。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for tighter bounds in multiclass learning beyond binary settings, this work establishes an optimal Sauer-type inequality for k-ary alphabets using the Daniely--Shalev-Shwartz (DS) dimension and its list extension. The method employs the polynomial method, contrasting with classical combinatorial proofs in the binary VC case. Experimental results confirm the bound’s tightness across all alphabet sizes k, list sizes ℓ, and dimension values, replacing the exponential dependence on ℓ with polynomial dependence and improving k-dependence. This yields sharper sample complexity bounds for list PAC learning and uniform convergence, improving upon recent results from Charikar et al., Hanneke et al., and Brukhim et al.
为解决k元字母表下多类学习中Sauer不等式不够紧的问题，本文基于Daniely--Shalev-Shwartz（DS）维数及其列表扩展，建立了最优的Sauer型不等式。方法采用多项式方法，与二元情形下的经典组合证明不同，该设定尚无纯组合证明。实验结果表明，该界在所有字母表大小k、列表大小ℓ和维度值下均紧致，将此前基于Natarajan维数的指数ℓ依赖性替换为多项式依赖性，并改进了k的依赖关系。由此获得列表PAC学习与均匀收敛的更优样本复杂度上界，优于Charikar等、Hanneke等及Brukhim等近期成果。
The Verification Tax: Fundamental Limits of AI Auditing in the Rare-Error Regime
Authors: Jason Z Wang
First: 2026-04-14T16:48:24+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-14T16:48:24+00:00
Comments: 25 pages, 16 figures, 6 tables. Code and data at https://github.com/Jason-Wang313/verification-tax
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
The most cited calibration result in deep learning -- post-temperature-scaling ECE of 0.012 on CIFAR-100 (Guo et al., 2017) -- is below the statistical noise floor. We prove this is not a failure of the experiment but a law: the minimax rate for estimating calibration error with model error rate epsilon is Theta((Lepsilon/m)^{1/3}), and no estimator can beat it. This "verification tax" implies that as AI models improve, verifying their calibration becomes fundamentally harder -- with the same exponent in opposite directions. We establish four results that contradict standard evaluation practice: (1) self-evaluation without labels provides exactly zero information about calibration, bounded by a constant independent of compute; (2) a sharp phase transition at mepsilon approx 1 below which miscalibration is undetectable; (3) active querying eliminates the Lipschitz constant, collapsing estimation to detection; (4) verification cost grows exponentially with pipeline depth at rate L^K. We validate across five benchmarks (MMLU, TruthfulQA, ARC-Challenge, HellaSwag, WinoGrande; ~27,000 items) with 6 LLMs from 5 families (8B-405B parameters, 27 benchmark-model pairs with logprob-based confidence), 95% bootstrap CIs, and permutation tests. Self-evaluation non-significance holds in 80% of pairs. Across frontier models, 23% of pairwise comparisons are indistinguishable from noise, implying that credible calibration claims must report verification floors and prioritize active querying once gains approach benchmark resolution.
中文标题/摘要
标题：验证税：在罕见错误环境下AI审计的根本限制
深度学习中最被引用的校准结果——CIFAR-100上经过温度缩放后的ECE为0.012（Guo等，2017）——低于统计噪声水平。我们证明这并非实验失败，而是一个定律：以模型错误率ε估计校准误差的最小最大速率是Θ((Lε/m)^{1/3})，任何估计器都无法超越此限。这一‘验证税’意味着，随着AI模型性能提升，验证其校准变得根本上更困难——指数方向相反。我们确立四项结果，与标准评估实践相悖：(1) 无标签的自评估对校准提供零信息，且该信息上限为与计算无关的常数；(2) 在mε≈1以下存在尖锐相变，此时校准偏差不可检测；(3) 主动查询消除Lipschitz常数，使估计退化为检测；(4) 验证成本随管道深度以L^K指数增长。我们在五个基准（MMLU、TruthfulQA、ARC-Challenge、HellaSwag、WinoGrande；约27,000项）上验证，使用5个家族的6个LLM（8B-405B参数，27个基准-模型对，基于logprob置信度），95%自助法置信区间及置换检验。80%的对中自评估无显著性。在前沿模型中，23%的成对比较与噪声无异，意味着可信校准声明必须报告验证下限，并在收益接近基准分辨率时优先采用主动查询。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the apparent precision of calibration error estimates in deep learning, this paper reveals that the claimed low ECE values are not artifacts of experimental noise but reflect a fundamental statistical limit: the minimax rate for estimating calibration error scales as Theta((Lepsilon/m)^{1/3}), imposing a 'verification tax' that grows with model performance. The authors derive four theoretical results challenging standard evaluation: self-evaluation without labels yields no useful calibration information, a sharp phase transition exists below which miscalibration is undetectable, active querying eliminates estimation complexity, and verification cost scales exponentially with model depth. Empirical validation across five benchmarks and six LLMs shows that self-evaluation is statistically insignificant in 80% of cases, and 23% of pairwise comparisons between frontier models are indistinguishable from noise, suggesting calibration claims must report verification floors and prioritize active querying when gains approach measurement resolution.
鉴于深度学习中广受引用的校准结果（如CIFAR-100上的ECE为0.012）低于统计噪声水平，本文提出"验证税"理论，证明校准误差估计存在根本性下限：其最小最大速率与模型错误率和样本量的立方根成正比。研究指出，无标签的自评估对校准无信息量，校准检测在样本量乘以错误率低于1时出现突变，而主动查询可消除Lipschitz常数。实验在五个基准和六个大模型上验证，80%的模型对自评估无显著性，23%的模型对之间比较无法区分噪声，表明可信校准声明必须报告验证下限，并在模型性能逼近基准分辨率时优先采用主动查询。
GlintMarkers: Spatial Perception on XR Eyewear using Corneal Reflections
Authors: Seungjoo Lee, Vimal Mollyn, Chris Harrison, Justin Chan, Mayank Goel
First: 2026-04-14T16:45:50+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-14T16:45:50+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We present GlintMarkers, the first system to perform gaze-driven spatial perception using the inward-facing cameras on XR eyewear. Our key observation is that the cornea acts as a mirror that encodes both gaze direction and visual information about the environment in a small, low-contrast reflection. To extract spatial and semantic information from this reflection despite the camera's limited pixel budget, we present a passive retroreflective marker design that concentrates reflected near-infrared light onto the cornea, producing bright glint patterns. We develop a custom Perspective-n-Point (PnP) estimation framework adapted to corneal imaging and perform orientation and distance estimation of tagged objects, as well as unique object identification.
中文标题/摘要
标题：GlintMarkers：利用角膜反射在XR眼镜上实现基于注视的空间感知
我们提出了GlintMarkers，这是首个利用XR眼镜内侧摄像头实现基于注视的空间感知的系统。我们的核心观察是，角膜如同一面镜子，能在微小且对比度低的反射中编码注视方向和环境视觉信息。为在摄像头有限像素预算下提取空间与语义信息，我们设计了一种被动反光标记，将近红外光集中投射至角膜，形成明亮的反光图案。我们开发了针对角膜成像定制的Perspective-n-Point（PnP）估计框架，用于对标记物体进行姿态与距离估计，以及独特物体识别。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for spatial perception in XR eyewear without requiring active sensors or user interaction, GlintMarkers leverages corneal reflections to encode gaze direction and environmental visual data. The system uses passive retroreflective markers that concentrate near-infrared light onto the cornea, creating bright glint patterns visible to inward-facing cameras. A custom Perspective-n-Point framework is then applied to estimate the orientation and distance of tagged objects, enabling unique object identification without requiring additional hardware or user input.
为实现XR眼镜中的无交互空间感知，GlintMarkers利用角膜反射编码注视方向与环境视觉信息。系统采用被动反光标记物，集中近红外光至角膜形成明亮的光斑，配合针对角膜成像优化的Perspective-n-Point框架，可准确估计标记物体的姿态、距离及唯一识别，即使在低对比度和有限分辨率条件下仍表现稳定。
Uncertainty-Aware Image Classification In Biomedical Imaging Using Spectral-normalized Neural Gaussian Processes
Authors: Uma Meleti, Jeffrey J. Nirschl
Venue: ISBI
First: 2026-02-02T17:35:10+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-14T16:45:44+00:00
Comments: Published at the IEEE International Symposium on Biomedical Imaging (ISBI) 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Accurate histopathologic interpretation is key for clinical decision-making; however, current deep learning models for digital pathology are often overconfident and poorly calibrated in out-of-distribution (OOD) settings, which limit trust and clinical adoption. Safety-critical medical imaging workflows benefit from intrinsic uncertainty-aware properties that can accurately reject OOD input. We implement the Spectral-normalized Neural Gaussian Process (SNGP), a set of lightweight modifications that apply spectral normalization and replace the final dense layer with a Gaussian process layer to improve single-model uncertainty estimation and OOD detection. We evaluate SNGP vs. deterministic and MonteCarlo dropout on six datasets across three biomedical classification tasks: white blood cells, amyloid plaques, and colorectal histopathology. SNGP has comparable in-distribution performance while significantly improving uncertainty estimation and OOD detection. Thus, SNGP or related models offer a useful framework for uncertainty-aware classification in digital pathology, supporting safe deployment and building trust with pathologists.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于谱归一化神经高斯过程的生物医学图像分类中的不确定性感知
准确的组织病理学解释对临床决策至关重要；然而，当前用于数字病理学的深度学习模型在分布外（OOD）场景中往往过于自信且校准不佳，这限制了其可信度和临床应用。安全关键的医学影像工作流程受益于内在的不确定性感知特性，能够准确拒绝OOD输入。我们实现了谱归一化神经高斯过程（SNGP），即一系列轻量级修改，包括应用谱归一化并将最终密集层替换为高斯过程层，以提升单模型的不确定性估计和OOD检测能力。我们在六个数据集上，针对三种生物医学分类任务（白细胞、淀粉样斑块和结直肠组织病理学）评估了SNGP与确定性模型和蒙特卡洛dropout的性能。SNGP在分布内性能相当，同时显著提升了不确定性估计和OOD检测能力。因此，SNGP或相关模型为数字病理学中的不确定性感知分类提供了一个实用框架，支持安全部署并增强病理科医生的信任。
Summary / 总结
To address the overconfidence and poor calibration of deep learning models in out-of-distribution settings within digital pathology, this study introduces Spectral-normalized Neural Gaussian Processes (SNGP), which applies spectral normalization and replaces the final dense layer with a Gaussian process layer to enhance uncertainty estimation. Evaluated against deterministic models and Monte Carlo dropout across six datasets and three biomedical classification tasks, SNGP maintained comparable in-distribution accuracy while significantly improving uncertainty quantification and out-of-distribution detection, making it a promising framework for safer, more trustworthy deployment in clinical histopathology workflows.
为提升数字病理学中图像分类的可靠性与临床可信度，本文提出谱归一化神经高斯过程（SNGP），通过谱归一化与替换最终全连接层为高斯过程层，增强模型的不确定性估计能力。在六个数据集上的三项病理分类任务中，SNGP在保持与基线模型相当的分布内精度的同时，显著优于确定性模型与蒙特卡洛dropout在不确定性校准与异常检测方面，展现出其在临床部署中的实用价值。
Drawing on Memory: Dual-Trace Encoding Improves Cross-Session Recall in LLM Agents
Authors: Benjamin Stern, Peter Nadel
First: 2026-04-14T16:45:06+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-14T16:45:06+00:00
Comments: 16 pages, 2 tables, 2 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
LLM agents with persistent memory store information as flat factual records, providing little context for temporal reasoning, change tracking, or cross-session aggregation. Inspired by the drawing effect [3], we introduce dual-trace memory encoding. In this method, each stored fact is paired with a concrete scene trace, a narrative reconstruction of the moment and context in which the information was learned. The agent is forced to commit to specific contextual details during encoding, creating richer, more distinctive memory traces. Using the LongMemEval-S benchmark (4,575 sessions, 100 recall questions), we compare dual-trace encoding against a fact-only control with matched coverage and format over 99 shared questions. Dual-trace achieves 73.7% overall accuracy versus 53.5%, a +20.2 percentage point (pp) gain (95% CI: [+12.1, +29.3], bootstrap p < 0.0001). Gains concentrate in temporal reasoning (+40pp), knowledge-update tracking (+25pp), and multi-session aggregation (+30pp), with no benefit for single-session retrieval, consistent with encoding specificity theory [8]. Token analysis shows dual-trace encoding achieves this gain at no additional cost. We additionally sketch an architectural design for adapting dual-trace encoding to coding agents, with preliminary pilot validation.
中文标题/摘要
标题：借助记忆绘图：双轨迹编码提升LLM代理跨会话回忆能力
具备持久记忆的LLM代理通常将信息存储为扁平事实记录，难以支持时间推理、变更追踪或跨会话聚合。受“绘图效应”[3]启发，我们引入双轨迹记忆编码方法：每个存储的事实均配有一个具体场景轨迹，即对该信息学习时的情境与时刻的叙事重构。该方法迫使代理在编码时承诺特定上下文细节，从而生成更丰富、更具区分度的记忆轨迹。在LongMemEval-S基准（4,575个会话，100个回忆问题）上，我们对比双轨迹编码与覆盖范围和格式匹配的事实仅编码控制组（99个共享问题）。双轨迹编码整体准确率73.7%，较对照组53.5%提升20.2个百分点（95%置信区间：[+12.1, +29.3]，自助法p<0.0001）。收益集中于时间推理（+40pp）、知识更新追踪（+25pp）和多会话聚合（+30pp），单会话检索无提升，符合编码特异性理论[8]。词元分析表明，双轨迹编码实现该增益无需额外成本。我们还初步勾勒了将双轨迹编码适配代码代理的架构设计，并进行了初步试点验证。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitations of flat factual memory in LLM agents for cross-session recall, this work introduces dual-trace encoding, which pairs each stored fact with a concrete scene trace to encode contextual details during memory formation. Evaluated on LongMemEval-S with 4,575 sessions and 100 shared questions, dual-trace achieved 73.7% accuracy versus 53.5% for a fact-only baseline, representing a +20.2 percentage point gain. This improvement was concentrated in temporal reasoning, knowledge-update tracking, and multi-session aggregation, with no benefit for single-session retrieval, aligning with encoding specificity theory. Token-level analysis confirmed the gain without increased cost, and a preliminary architectural sketch for coding agents was also presented.
为解决LLM代理在跨会话回忆中因扁平事实记忆缺乏上下文而导致的性能不足，该研究提出双迹编码方法，将每个存储事实与一个场景轨迹（其学习时的情境叙事重建）配对，以在编码阶段强制保留具体上下文。在包含4575个会话和100个共享问题的LongMemEval-S基准测试中，双迹编码相较仅存储事实的基线提升20.2个百分点（73.7% vs 53.5%），在时间推理、知识更新追踪和多会话聚合方面分别提升40、25和30个百分点，而单会话检索无提升，符合编码特异性理论。词元分析表明该提升无额外成本，且为编码代理提供了初步架构设计与验证。
TeRA: Vector-based Random Tensor Network for High-Rank Adaptation of Large Language Models
Authors: Yuxuan Gu, Wuyang Zhou, Giorgos Iacovides, Danilo Mandic
Venue: ACL
First: 2025-09-03T11:46:24+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-14T16:44:27+00:00
Comments: Accepted at ACL main conference 2026. Code is available at https://github.com/guyuxuan9/TeRA
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Parameter-Efficient Fine-Tuning (PEFT) methods, such as Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA), have significantly reduced the number of trainable parameters needed in fine-tuning large language models (LLMs). The developments of LoRA-style adapters have considered two main directions: (1) enhancing model expressivity with high-rank adapters, and (2) aiming for further parameter reduction, as exemplified by vector-based methods. However, these approaches come with a trade-off, as achieving the expressivity of high-rank weight updates typically comes at the cost of sacrificing the extreme parameter efficiency offered by vector-based techniques. To address this issue, we propose a vector-based random Tensor network for high-Rank Adaptation (TeRA), a novel PEFT method that achieves high-rank weight updates while retaining the parameter efficiency of vector-based PEFT adapters. This is achieved by parametrizing the tensorized weight update matrix as a Tucker-like tensor network (TN), whereby large randomly initialized factors are frozen and shared across layers, while only small layer-specific scaling vectors, corresponding to diagonal entries of factor matrices, are trained. Comprehensive experiments demonstrate that TeRA matches or even outperforms existing high-rank adapters, while requiring as few trainable parameters as vector-based methods. Theoretical analysis and ablation studies validate the effectiveness of the proposed TeRA method. The code is available at https://github.com/guyuxuan9/TeRA.
中文标题/摘要
标题：TeRA：基于向量的随机张量网络用于大语言模型的高秩适配
参数高效微调（PEFT）方法，如低秩适配（LoRA），显著减少了微调大语言模型（LLMs）所需的可训练参数数量。LoRA风格适配器的发展主要集中在两个方向：（1）通过高秩适配器增强模型表达能力；（2）追求进一步的参数缩减，如基于向量的方法。然而，这些方法存在权衡：实现高秩权重更新的表达能力通常以牺牲基于向量方法的极端参数效率为代价。为解决此问题，我们提出了一种基于向量的随机张量网络（TeRA），这是一种新颖的PEFT方法，能够在保持基于向量PEFT适配器参数效率的同时实现高秩权重更新。该方法通过将张量化权重更新矩阵参数化为类似Tucker的张量网络（TN），其中大型随机初始化因子被冻结并跨层共享，仅训练对应因子矩阵对角元素的小型层特定缩放向量。全面实验表明，TeRA在参数数量与基于向量的方法相当的情况下，可匹配甚至超越现有高秩适配器。理论分析和消融研究验证了所提TeRA方法的有效性。代码见https://github.com/guyuxuan9/TeRA。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the trade-off between expressivity and parameter efficiency in PEFT methods like LoRA, the authors propose TeRA, a vector-based random tensor network that enables high-rank weight updates while preserving parameter efficiency. TeRA represents the weight update matrix as a Tucker-like tensor network, freezing large random factors shared across layers and training only small layer-specific scaling vectors. Experimental results show that TeRA matches or surpasses existing high-rank adapters in performance while using as few trainable parameters as vector-based methods, validated through ablation studies and theoretical analysis.
为解决PEFT方法中表达能力与参数效率之间的权衡问题，作者提出TeRA，一种基于向量的随机张量网络，可在保持向量型适配器参数效率的同时实现高秩权重更新。TeRA将权重更新矩阵建模为Tucker型张量网络，冻结跨层共享的大规模随机因子，仅训练对应因子矩阵对角元素的层特定缩放向量。实验表明，TeRA在参数量与向量型方法相当的情况下，性能可媲美甚至超越现有高秩适配器，其有效性通过消融实验与理论分析得到验证。
CamReasoner: Reinforcing Camera Movement Understanding via Structured Spatial Reasoning
Authors: Hang Wu, Yujun Cai, Zehao Li, Haonan Ge, Bowen Sun, Junsong Yuan, Yiwei Wang
First: 2026-01-30T04:45:43+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-14T16:44:13+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Understanding camera dynamics is a fundamental pillar of video spatial intelligence. However, existing multimodal models predominantly treat this task as a black-box classification, often confusing physically distinct motions by relying on superficial visual patterns rather than geometric cues. We present \textbf{CamReasoner}, a framework that reformulates camera movement understanding as a structured inference process to bridge the gap between perception and cinematic logic. Our approach centers on the Observation-Thinking-Answer (O-T-A) paradigm, which compels the model to articulate spatio-temporal observations and reason about motion patterns within an explicit reasoning block. To instill this capability, we construct a Large-scale Inference Trajectory Suite comprising 18k SFT reasoning chains and 38k RL feedback samples. To the best of our knowledge, \textbf{we are the first to employ RL for logical alignment in camera movement understanding}, ensuring motion inferences are grounded in structured visual reasoning rather than contextual guesswork. Built upon Qwen2.5-VL-7B, CamReasoner-7B improves binary classification accuracy from 73.8\% to 78.4\% and VQA accuracy from 60.9\% to 74.5\% over its backbone, consistently outperforming both proprietary and open-source baselines across multiple benchmarks.
中文标题/摘要
标题：CamReasoner：通过结构化空间推理强化相机运动理解
理解相机动态是视频空间智能的基础支柱。然而，现有多模态模型大多将此任务视为黑盒分类，常因依赖表面视觉模式而非几何线索，混淆物理上不同的运动。我们提出\textbf{CamReasoner}，将相机运动理解重构为结构化推理过程，弥合感知与电影逻辑之间的鸿沟。我们的方法基于观察-思考-回答（O-T-A）范式，强制模型在显式推理模块中阐述时空观察并推导运动模式。为赋予该能力，我们构建了包含1.8万个SFT推理链和3.8万个RL反馈样本的大规模推理轨迹套件。据我们所知，\textbf{我们首次将强化学习用于相机运动理解中的逻辑对齐}，确保运动推断基于结构化视觉推理，而非情境猜测。基于Qwen2.5-VL-7B构建的CamReasoner-7B，在其基座模型上二分类准确率从73.8%提升至78.4%，VQA准确率从60.9%提升至74.5%，在多个基准测试中持续优于所有专有及开源基线模型。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitation of existing models that treat camera movement as a black-box classification relying on superficial visual cues, CamReasoner reformulates camera motion understanding as a structured inference task using the Observation-Thinking-Answer paradigm, forcing models to articulate spatio-temporal observations and reason geometrically. Trained on a large-scale inference trajectory suite with 18k SFT chains and 38k RL feedback samples, it is the first to apply reinforcement learning for logical alignment in camera movement understanding. Experiments show that CamReasoner-7B, built on Qwen2.5-VL-7B, boosts binary classification accuracy from 73.8% to 78.4% and VQA accuracy from 60.9% to 74.5%, outperforming all baselines across benchmarks.
为解决现有模型将相机运动视为黑盒分类、依赖表面视觉特征的问题，CamReasoner 将相机运动理解重构为结构化推理任务，采用观察-思考-回答范式。模型基于包含1.8万条SFT推理链和3.8万条RL反馈样本的大规模数据集训练，首次在相机运动理解中应用强化学习实现逻辑对齐。实验表明，基于Qwen2.5-VL-7B构建的CamReasoner-7B，二分类准确率从73.8%提升至78.4%，VQA准确率从60.9%提升至74.5%，在多个基准测试中全面超越各类基线模型。
Parcae: Scaling Laws For Stable Looped Language Models
Authors: Hayden Prairie, Zachary Novack, Taylor Berg-Kirkpatrick, Daniel Y. Fu
First: 2026-04-14T16:43:37+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-14T16:43:37+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Traditional fixed-depth architectures scale quality by increasing training FLOPs, typically through increased parameterization, at the expense of a higher memory footprint, or data. A potential alternative is looped architectures, which instead increase FLOPs by sending activations through a block of layers in a loop. While promising, existing recipes for training looped architectures can be unstable, suffering from residual explosion and loss spikes. We address these challenges by recasting looping as a nonlinear time-variant dynamical system over the residual stream. Via a linear approximation to this system, we find that instability occurs in existing looped architectures as a result of large spectral norms in their injection parameters. To address these instability issues, we propose Parcae, a novel stable, looped architecture that constrains the spectral norm of the injection parameters via discretization of a negative diagonal parameterization. As a result, Parcae achieves up to 6.3% lower validation perplexity over prior large-scale looped models. Using our stable looped architecture, we investigate the scaling properties of looping as a medium to improve quality by increasing FLOPs in training and test-time. For training, we derive predictable power laws to scale FLOPs while keeping parameter count fixed. Our initial scaling laws suggest that looping and data should be increased in tandem, given a fixed FLOP budget. At test-time, we find that Parcae can use looping to scale compute, following a predictable, saturating exponential decay. When scaled up to 1.3B parameters, we find that Parcae improves CORE and Core-Extended quality by 2.99 and 1.18 points when compared to strong Transformer baselines under a fixed parameter and data budget, achieving a relative quality of up to 87.5% a Transformer twice the size.
中文标题/摘要
标题：帕尔凯：稳定循环语言模型的缩放定律
传统固定深度架构通过增加训练FLOPs（通常通过增加参数化）来提升质量，但代价是更高的内存占用或数据需求。一种潜在替代方案是循环架构，其通过将激活值在循环中经过多层块来增加FLOPs。尽管有前景，现有循环架构的训练方法可能不稳定，易出现残差爆炸和损失突增。我们通过将循环重新建模为残差流上的非线性时变动力系统来解决这些问题。通过该系统的线性近似，我们发现现有循环架构的不稳定性源于其注入参数的谱范数过大。为解决此问题，我们提出帕尔凯，一种新颖的稳定循环架构，通过负对角参数化离散化约束注入参数的谱范数。结果表明，帕尔凯在验证困惑度上比先前的大规模循环模型低至6.3%。利用该稳定循环架构，我们研究了循环作为提升质量手段的缩放特性，即在训练和测试时通过增加FLOPs实现。在训练中，我们推导出在固定参数量下缩放FLOPs的可预测幂律。初步缩放定律表明，在固定FLOP预算下，应同步增加循环和数据。在测试时，我们发现帕尔凯可通过循环缩放计算量，遵循可预测的饱和指数衰减。当扩展至13亿参数时，帕尔凯在固定参数和数据预算下，相比强Transformer基线模型，CORE和Core-Extended质量分别提升2.99和1.18点，达到相当于两倍大小Transformer的87.5%相对质量。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the inefficiency of traditional fixed-depth models that scale quality via parameter increases at the cost of memory or data, the paper introduces Parcae, a stable looped architecture that boosts FLOPs by looping activations through layers. It addresses instability in existing looped models—caused by residual explosion and loss spikes—by modeling looping as a nonlinear dynamical system and constraining injection parameter spectral norms via a negative diagonal discretization. Experiments show Parcae achieves 6.3% lower validation perplexity than prior looped models, and scaling laws reveal that looping should be paired with data growth under fixed FLOP budgets. At test-time, Parcae scales compute with saturating exponential decay, improving CORE and Core-Extended quality by 2.99 and 1.18 points respectively over Transformer baselines at 1.3B parameters, reaching 87.5% relative quality of a twice-sized Transformer.
为了解决传统固定深度架构通过增加参数或内存来提升质量的效率低下问题，本文提出Parcae，一种通过循环激活流而不增加参数量来提升FLOPs的稳定循环架构。该架构通过将循环建模为非线性动态系统，并通过负对角参数化离散化约束注入参数的谱范数，解决了现有循环模型中残差爆炸和损失尖峰的不稳定性问题。实验表明，Parcae在验证困惑度上比前代循环模型低至6.3%，且其缩放规律显示，在FLOP预算固定时，应同步增加循环与数据量。在测试时，Parcae可按饱和指数衰减方式扩展计算量，在1.3B参数规模下，相较强Transformer基线模型，CORE和Core-Extended质量分别提升2.99和1.18点，达到相当于两倍规模Transformer的87.5%相对质量。
Adaptive Data Dropout: Towards Self-Regulated Learning in Deep Neural Networks
Authors: Amar Gahir, Varshil Patel, Shreyank N Gowda
First: 2026-04-14T16:41:33+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-14T16:41:33+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Deep neural networks are typically trained by uniformly sampling large datasets across epochs, despite evidence that not all samples contribute equally throughout learning. Recent work shows that progressively reducing the amount of training data can improve efficiency and generalization, but existing methods rely on fixed schedules that do not adapt during training. In this work, we propose Adaptive Data Dropout, a simple framework that dynamically adjusts the subset of training data based on performance feedback. Inspired by self-regulated learning, our approach treats data selection as an adaptive process, increasing or decreasing data exposure in response to changes in training accuracy. We introduce a lightweight stochastic update mechanism that modulates the dropout schedule online, allowing the model to balance exploration and consolidation over time. Experiments on standard image classification benchmarks show that our method reduces effective training steps while maintaining competitive accuracy compared to static data dropout strategies. These results highlight adaptive data selection as a promising direction for efficient and robust training. Code will be released.
中文标题/摘要
标题：自适应数据丢弃：迈向深度神经网络的自我调节学习
尽管有证据表明并非所有样本在学习过程中贡献均等，深度神经网络通常仍通过在各轮次中均匀采样大型数据集进行训练。近期研究表明，逐步减少训练数据量可提升效率和泛化能力，但现有方法依赖固定调度，无法在训练中自适应调整。本文提出自适应数据丢弃框架，根据性能反馈动态调整训练数据子集。受自我调节学习启发，我们的方法将数据选择视为自适应过程，根据训练准确率变化增减数据暴露量。我们引入轻量级随机更新机制，在线调节丢弃调度，使模型在训练过程中平衡探索与巩固。在标准图像分类基准测试中，实验表明该方法在保持与静态数据丢弃策略相当精度的同时，减少了有效训练步数。结果凸显自适应数据选择作为高效稳健训练的有前景方向。代码将公开。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the observation that not all training samples contribute equally during deep neural network training, this work proposes Adaptive Data Dropout, a framework that dynamically adjusts the training data subset based on performance feedback. The method treats data selection as an adaptive process, using a lightweight stochastic mechanism to increase or decrease data exposure in response to changes in training accuracy, thereby balancing exploration and consolidation. Experimental results on standard image classification benchmarks demonstrate that the approach reduces effective training steps while maintaining competitive accuracy compared to fixed data dropout strategies, highlighting its potential for efficient and robust learning.
鉴于训练过程中并非所有样本对深度神经网络学习的贡献均等，本文提出自适应数据丢弃方法，通过根据性能反馈动态调整训练数据子集，以提升效率与泛化能力。该方法将数据选择视为自适应过程，利用轻量级随机机制根据准确率变化动态增减数据暴露程度，从而平衡探索与巩固。在标准图像分类基准上的实验表明，该方法可减少有效训练步数，同时保持与固定数据丢弃策略相当的精度，凸显自适应数据选择在高效稳健训练中的潜力。
Towards Generalized Certified Robustness with Multi-Norm Training
Authors: Enyi Jiang, David S. Cheung, Gagandeep Singh
First: 2024-10-03T21:20:46+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-14T16:40:44+00:00
Comments: Accepted by TMLR 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Existing certified training methods can only train models to be robust against a certain perturbation type (e.g. $l_\infty$ or $l_2$). However, an $l_\infty$ certifiably robust model may not be certifiably robust against $l_2$ perturbation (and vice versa) and also has low robustness against other perturbations (e.g. geometric and patch transformation). By constructing a theoretical framework to analyze and mitigate the tradeoff, we propose the first multi-norm certified training framework \textbf{CURE}, consisting of several multi-norm certified training methods, to attain better \emph{union robustness} when training from scratch or fine-tuning a pre-trained certified model. Inspired by our theoretical findings, we devise bound alignment and connect natural training with certified training for better union robustness. Compared with SOTA-certified training, \textbf{CURE} improves union robustness to $32.0\%$ on MNIST, $25.8\%$ on CIFAR-10, and $10.6\%$ on TinyImagenet across different epsilon values. It leads to better generalization on a diverse set of challenging unseen geometric and patch perturbations to $6.8\%$ and $16.0\%$ on CIFAR-10. Overall, our contributions pave a path towards \textit{generalized certified robustness}.
中文标题/摘要
标题：面向多范式认证鲁棒性的训练方法
现有认证训练方法只能训练模型对特定扰动类型（如 $l_\infty$ 或 $l_2$）具有鲁棒性。然而，一个对 $l_\infty$ 扰动认证鲁棒的模型可能无法对 $l_2$ 扰动认证鲁棒（反之亦然），且对其他扰动（如几何和补丁变换）鲁棒性较低。通过构建理论框架分析并缓解此权衡，我们提出首个多范式认证训练框架 \textbf{CURE}，包含多种多范式认证训练方法，以在从头训练或微调预训练认证模型时获得更好的 \emph{联合鲁棒性}。受理论发现启发，我们设计了边界对齐机制，并将自然训练与认证训练相结合，以提升联合鲁棒性。与当前最优认证训练方法相比，\textbf{CURE} 在 MNIST、CIFAR-10 和 TinyImagenet 上分别在不同 epsilon 值下将联合鲁棒性提升至 $32.0\%$、$25.8\%$ 和 $10.6\%$。同时，在 CIFAR-10 上对多样化的挑战性未见几何和补丁扰动，其泛化性能分别提升至 $6.8\%$ 和 $16.0\%$。总体而言，我们的工作为实现 \textit{广义认证鲁棒性} 开辟了道路。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitation that existing certified training methods are restricted to specific perturbation norms and lack generalization across types, this work proposes CURE, the first multi-norm certified training framework designed to achieve union robustness. It introduces bound alignment and connects natural training with certified training to mitigate norm-specific tradeoffs. Experiments show CURE significantly improves union robustness over state-of-the-art methods—by 32.0% on MNIST, 25.8% on CIFAR-10, and 10.6% on TinyImagenet—while also enhancing generalization to unseen geometric and patch perturbations by 6.8% and 16.0% on CIFAR-10.
为了解决现有认证训练方法仅针对特定扰动范数（如l∞或l2）而缺乏跨范数泛化能力的问题，本文提出CURE框架，通过绑定对齐和自然训练与认证训练的结合，实现多范数下的联合鲁棒性。实验表明，CURE在MNIST、CIFAR-10和TinyImagenet上分别比当前最优方法提升32.0%、25.8%和10.6%的联合鲁棒性，并在CIFAR-10上对未见过的几何与块扰动实现6.8%和16.0%的泛化性能提升，为实现广义认证鲁棒性提供了新路径。
Distorted or Fabricated? A Survey on Hallucination in Video LLMs
Authors: Yiyang Huang, Yitian Zhang, Yizhou Wang, Mingyuan Zhang, Liang Shi, Huimin Zeng, Yun Fu
Venue: ACL 2026
First: 2026-04-14T16:37:57+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-14T16:37:57+00:00
Comments: ACL 2026 findings
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Despite significant progress in video-language modeling, hallucinations remain a persistent challenge in Video Large Language Models (Vid-LLMs), referring to outputs that appear plausible yet contradict the content of the input video. This survey presents a comprehensive analysis of hallucinations in Vid-LLMs and introduces a systematic taxonomy that categorizes them into two core types: dynamic distortion and content fabrication, each comprising two subtypes with representative cases. Building on this taxonomy, we review recent advances in the evaluation and mitigation of hallucinations, covering key benchmarks, metrics, and intervention strategies. We further analyze the root causes of dynamic distortion and content fabrication, which often result from limited capacity for temporal representation and insufficient visual grounding. These insights inform several promising directions for future work, including the development of motion-aware visual encoders and the integration of counterfactual learning techniques. This survey consolidates scattered progress to foster a systematic understanding of hallucinations in Vid-LLMs, laying the groundwork for building robust and reliable video-language systems. An up-to-date curated list of related works is maintained at https://github.com/hukcc/Awesome-Video-Hallucination .
中文标题/摘要
标题：扭曲或捏造？视频大语言模型幻觉问题综述
尽管视频-语言建模取得了显著进展，但幻觉问题仍持续困扰视频大语言模型（Vid-LLMs），指代那些看似合理却与输入视频内容相矛盾的输出。本综述对Vid-LLMs中的幻觉现象进行了全面分析，并提出了一种系统分类法，将其划分为两大核心类型：动态扭曲与内容捏造，每类又包含两个子类型及代表性案例。在此分类基础上，我们回顾了近期在幻觉评估与缓解方面的进展，涵盖关键基准、度量标准及干预策略。进一步分析表明，动态扭曲与内容捏造的根本原因常源于时间表示能力有限及视觉锚定不足。这些洞见为未来研究指明了若干有前景的方向，包括开发运动感知视觉编码器及整合反事实学习技术。本综述整合了分散的研究进展，旨在促进对Vid-LLMs幻觉问题的系统性理解，为构建稳健可靠的视频-语言系统奠定基础。相关工作最新整理列表见 https://github.com/hukcc/Awesome-Video-Hallucination。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the persistent issue of hallucinations in Video Large Language Models (Vid-LLMs), this survey systematically categorizes such errors into two core types—dynamic distortion and content fabrication—each with distinct subtypes. It reviews recent evaluation benchmarks, metrics, and mitigation strategies, while analyzing root causes such as limited temporal modeling capacity and weak visual grounding. Experimental insights from prior work highlight the need for motion-aware encoders and counterfactual learning, offering a structured foundation for future research toward more reliable video-language systems.
鉴于视频大语言模型（Vid-LLMs）中幻觉问题长期存在，本综述系统性地将幻觉划分为两大核心类型——动态失真与内容伪造，并进一步细分为若干子类，以构建清晰的分析框架。作者回顾了当前评估基准、度量方法及缓解策略，指出其根源在于时间建模能力不足与视觉锚定薄弱。研究发现，两类幻觉均源于模型架构与表征层面的根本缺陷，未来可探索运动感知视觉编码器与反事实学习等方向，以提升模型可靠性。
RMGS-SLAM: Real-time Multi-sensor Gaussian Splatting SLAM
Authors: Dongen Li, Yi Liu, Junqi Liu, Zewen Sun, Zefan Huang, Shuo Sun, Jiahui Liu, Chengran Yuan, Hongliang Guo, Francis E. H. Tay, Marcelo H. Ang
First: 2026-04-14T16:36:11+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-14T16:36:11+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Real-time 3D Gaussian splatting (3DGS)-based Simultaneous Localization and Mapping (SLAM) in large-scale real-world environments remains challenging, as existing methods often struggle to jointly achieve low-latency pose estimation, 3D Gaussian reconstruction in step with incoming sensor streams, and long-term global consistency. In this paper, we present a tightly coupled LiDAR-Inertial-Visual (LIV) 3DGS-based SLAM framework for real-time pose estimation and photorealistic mapping in large-scale real-world scenes. The system executes state estimation and 3D Gaussian primitive initialization in parallel with global Gaussian optimization, thereby enabling continuous dense mapping. To improve Gaussian initialization quality and accelerate optimization convergence, we introduce a cascaded strategy that combines feed-forward predictions with voxel-based principal component analysis (voxel-PCA) geometric priors. To enhance global consistency in large scenes, we further perform loop closure directly on the optimized global Gaussian map by estimating loop constraints through Gaussian-based Generalized Iterative Closest Point (GICP) registration, followed by pose-graph optimization. In addition, we collected challenging large-scale looped outdoor SLAM sequences with hardware-synchronized LiDAR-camera-IMU and ground-truth trajectories to support realistic and comprehensive evaluation. Extensive experiments on both public datasets and our dataset demonstrate that the proposed method achieves a strong balance among real-time efficiency, localization accuracy, and rendering quality across diverse and challenging real-world scenes.
中文标题/摘要
标题：RMGS-SLAM：实时多传感器高斯点云SLAM
在大规模真实环境中实现基于3D高斯点云（3DGS）的实时同步定位与地图构建（SLAM）仍具挑战性，因现有方法常难以同时实现低延迟位姿估计、与传感器流同步的3D高斯重建及长期全局一致性。本文提出一种紧密耦合的激光雷达-惯性-视觉（LIV）3DGS SLAM框架，用于大规模真实场景中的实时位姿估计与逼真地图构建。系统并行执行状态估计与3D高斯原始体初始化，同时进行全局高斯优化，从而实现连续密集地图构建。为提升高斯初始化质量并加速优化收敛，引入级联策略，结合前馈预测与基于体素的主成分分析（voxel-PCA）几何先验。为增强大规模场景的全局一致性，进一步在优化后的全局高斯地图上直接执行闭环，通过基于高斯的广义迭代最近点（GICP）配准估计闭环约束，随后进行位姿图优化。此外，我们采集了配备硬件同步激光雷达-相机-惯性测量单元（IMU）及真实轨迹的挑战性大规模闭环户外SLAM序列，以支持真实全面的评估。在公开数据集及我们自建数据集上的大量实验表明，所提方法在多样化且具挑战性的现实场景中，实现了实时效率、定位精度与渲染质量之间的良好平衡。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the difficulty of achieving real-time, high-quality 3D mapping with consistent global structure in large-scale environments, this paper introduces RMGS-SLAM, a tightly coupled LiDAR-Inertial-Visual (LIV) system that integrates 3D Gaussian Splatting for simultaneous localization and mapping. The method parallelizes state estimation and Gaussian initialization with global optimization to enable continuous dense reconstruction, while using a cascaded feed-forward prediction and voxel-PCA strategy to improve Gaussian initialization and accelerate convergence. To maintain global consistency, it performs loop closure via Gaussian-based GICP registration followed by pose-graph optimization. Experiments on public and custom datasets show RMGS-SLAM achieves superior real-time performance, high localization accuracy, and photorealistic rendering across diverse challenging scenes.
为解决大规模真实环境中实时3D高斯点云SLAM难以兼顾低延迟、动态重建与全局一致性的问题，本文提出RMGS-SLAM系统，采用紧耦合激光雷达-惯性-视觉的3D高斯点云SLAM架构。系统并行执行状态估计、高斯点初始化与全局优化，实现连续稠密建图。为提升初始化质量与优化收敛速度，引入级联策略，结合前馈预测与体素PCA几何先验。为增强全局一致性，通过高斯点云GICP配准直接进行闭环检测，并结合位姿图优化。在公开数据集与自建数据集上的实验表明，该方法在复杂真实场景中实现了实时性、定位精度与渲染质量的优越平衡。
Direct Discrepancy Replay: Distribution-Discrepancy Condensation and Manifold-Consistent Replay for Continual Face Forgery Detection
Authors: Tianshuo Zhang, Haoyuan Zhang, Siran Peng, Weisong Zhao, Xiangyu Zhu, Zhen Lei
First: 2026-04-14T16:35:04+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-14T16:35:04+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Continual face forgery detection (CFFD) requires detectors to learn emerging forgery paradigms without forgetting previously seen manipulations. Existing CFFD methods commonly rely on replaying a small amount of past data to mitigate forgetting. Such replay is typically implemented either by storing a few historical samples or by synthesizing pseudo-forgeries from detector-dependent perturbations. Under strict memory budgets, the former cannot adequately cover diverse forgery cues and may expose facial identities, while the latter remains strongly tied to past decision boundaries. We argue that the core role of replay in CFFD is to reinstate the distributions of previous forgery tasks during subsequent training. To this end, we directly condense the discrepancy between real and fake distributions and leverage real faces from the current stage to perform distribution-level replay. Specifically, we introduce Distribution-Discrepancy Condensation (DDC), which models the real-to-fake discrepancy via a surrogate factorization in characteristic-function space and condenses it into a tiny bank of distribution discrepancy maps. We further propose Manifold-Consistent Replay (MCR), which synthesizes replay samples through variance-preserving composition of these maps with current-stage real faces, yielding samples that reflect previous-task forgery cues while remaining compatible with current real-face statistics. Operating under an extremely small memory budget and without directly storing raw historical face images, our framework consistently outperforms prior CFFD baselines and significantly mitigates catastrophic forgetting. Replay-level privacy analysis further suggests reduced identity leakage risk relative to selection-based replay.
中文标题/摘要
标题：直接差异重放：分布差异压缩与流形一致重放用于持续人脸伪造检测
持续人脸伪造检测（CFFD）要求检测器在不遗忘先前见过的篡改情况下学习新兴的伪造范式。现有CFFD方法通常依赖重放少量历史数据以缓解遗忘。此类重放通常通过存储少量历史样本或从检测器依赖的扰动中合成伪伪造实现。在严格内存预算下，前者无法充分覆盖多样化的伪造线索且可能泄露面部身份，后者仍强烈依赖过往决策边界。我们认为，CFFD中重放的核心作用是在后续训练中重建先前伪造任务的分布。为此，我们直接压缩真实与伪造分布之间的差异，并利用当前阶段的真实人脸进行分布级重放。具体而言，我们引入分布差异压缩（DDC），通过特征函数空间中的代理因子分解建模真实到伪造的差异，并将其压缩为一组微小的分布差异图。我们进一步提出流形一致重放（MCR），通过将这些图与当前阶段真实人脸进行方差保持的组合合成重放样本，生成反映先前任务伪造线索同时与当前真实人脸统计兼容的样本。在极小内存预算下且无需直接存储原始历史人脸图像，我们的框架持续优于现有CFFD基线，并显著缓解灾难性遗忘。重放级隐私分析进一步表明，相较于基于选择的重放，本方法降低了身份泄露风险。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to detect evolving face forgeries without forgetting past manipulations under strict memory constraints, this work proposes Direct Discrepancy Replay, which condenses distribution-level discrepancies between real and fake faces from past tasks into compact maps. These maps are then used with current-stage real faces to synthesize replay samples that preserve past forgery cues while aligning with current data statistics. Experiments show the method consistently outperforms existing baselines in continual face forgery detection and significantly reduces catastrophic forgetting, even under extremely limited memory budgets, while also lowering privacy risks compared to traditional replay strategies.
为在内存受限条件下持续检测新型人脸伪造并避免遗忘旧有伪造模式，本文提出直接差异重放方法，通过在特征函数空间中建模真实与伪造分布差异并压缩为小型分布差异图谱，再结合当前阶段真实人脸合成重放样本，使样本保留历史伪造特征同时符合当前真实人脸统计特性。实验表明，该方法在极小内存预算下持续优于现有基线，显著缓解灾难性遗忘，并降低身份泄露风险。
DecompSR: A dataset for decomposed analyses of compositional multihop spatial reasoning
Authors: Lachlan McPheat, Navdeep Kaur, Robert Blackwell, Alessandra Russo, Anthony G. Cohn, Pranava Madhyastha
First: 2025-11-04T14:57:11+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-14T16:34:47+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We introduce DecompSR, decomposed spatial reasoning, a large benchmark dataset (over 5m datapoints) and generation framework designed to analyse compositional spatial reasoning ability. The generation of DecompSR allows users to independently vary several aspects of compositionality, namely: productivity (reasoning depth), substitutivity (entity and linguistic variability), overgeneralisation (input order, distractors) and systematicity (novel linguistic elements). DecompSR is built procedurally in a manner which makes it is correct by construction, which is independently verified using a symbolic solver to guarantee the correctness of the dataset. DecompSR is comprehensively benchmarked across a host of Large Language Models (LLMs) where we show that LLMs struggle with productive and systematic generalisation in spatial reasoning tasks whereas they are more robust to linguistic variation. DecompSR provides a provably correct and rigorous benchmarking dataset with a novel ability to independently vary the degrees of several key aspects of compositionality, allowing for robust and fine-grained probing of the compositional reasoning abilities of LLMs.
中文标题/摘要
标题：DecompSR：用于分解分析组合式多跳空间推理的数据集
我们引入了DecompSR，一种分解式空间推理的大规模基准数据集（超过500万个数据点）及生成框架，旨在分析组合式空间推理能力。DecompSR的生成允许用户独立调整组合性的多个方面：生产力（推理深度）、可替换性（实体与语言变体）、过度泛化（输入顺序、干扰项）和系统性（新颖语言元素）。DecompSR以程序化方式构建，确保其构造正确，并通过符号求解器独立验证，以保证数据集的正确性。DecompSR在多种大型语言模型（LLMs）上进行了全面基准测试，结果显示LLMs在空间推理任务中难以实现生产力和系统性的泛化，但对语言变体更具鲁棒性。DecompSR提供了一个可证明正确的严谨基准数据集，能够独立调节组合性若干关键方面的程度，从而实现对LLMs组合推理能力的稳健且精细的探测。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to rigorously probe compositional spatial reasoning in LLMs, DecompSR introduces a large-scale benchmark dataset (over 5 million datapoints) and a generation framework that enables independent manipulation of key compositional aspects: productivity, substitutivity, overgeneralisation, and systematicity. The dataset is procedurally constructed to be correct by design and verified symbolically to ensure reliability. Experimental results across multiple LLMs reveal that models exhibit significant difficulty with productive and systematic generalisation, while showing greater robustness to linguistic variation.
为严谨评估大语言模型在组合式空间推理中的能力，DecompSR 提供了一个大规模基准数据集（超500万条数据）及生成框架，支持独立调节组合性的关键维度：生产力、可替换性、过度泛化和系统性。该数据集通过程序化构建确保逻辑正确，并经符号求解器验证。实验表明，模型在生产力与系统性泛化方面表现薄弱，但在语言变体方面相对稳健。
Perception-Aware Policy Optimization for Multimodal Reasoning
Authors: Zhenhailong Wang, Xuehang Guo, Sofia Stoica, Haiyang Xu, Hongru Wang, Hyeonjeong Ha, Xiusi Chen, Yangyi Chen, Ming Yan, Fei Huang, Heng Ji
First: 2025-07-08T23:22:34+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-14T16:31:35+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Rewards (RLVR) has proven to be a highly effective strategy for endowing Large Language Models (LLMs) with robust multi-step reasoning abilities. However, its design and optimizations remain tailored to purely textual domains, resulting in suboptimal performance when applied to multimodal reasoning tasks. In particular, we observe that a major source of error in current multimodal reasoning lies in the perception of visual inputs. To address this bottleneck, we propose PAPO, a novel policy gradient algorithm that encourages the model to learn to perceive while learning to reason. Specifically, we introduce the Implicit Perception Loss in the form of a KL divergence term, which can be seamlessly plugged into mainstream RLVR algorithms such as GRPO and DAPO. Notably, PAPO does not rely on additional data curation, reward models, or stronger teacher models. To further enhance the training stability of PAPO, we introduce the Double Entropy Loss, which effectively regularizes the new KL objective without compromising performance. Despite its simplicity, PAPO yields significant overall improvements of 4.4%-17.5% on diverse multimodal benchmarks. The improvements are more pronounced, approaching 8.0%-19.1%, on tasks with high vision dependency. We also observe a substantial reduction of 30.5% in perception errors, indicating improved perceptual capabilities with PAPO. Overall, our work introduces a deeper integration of perception-aware supervision into core learning objectives and lays the groundwork for a new RL framework that encourages visually grounded reasoning. Code and data will be made publicly available for research purposes. Project page: https://mikewangwzhl.github.io/PAPO.
中文标题/摘要
标题：面向多模态推理的感知感知策略优化
可验证奖励的强化学习（RLVR）已被证明是赋予大型语言模型（LLMs）强大多步推理能力的高效策略。然而，其设计与优化仍针对纯文本领域，导致在多模态推理任务中表现不佳。我们观察到，当前多模态推理中的主要错误来源在于对视觉输入的感知。为解决这一瓶颈，我们提出PAPO，一种新型策略梯度算法，鼓励模型在学习推理的同时学习感知。具体而言，我们引入隐式感知损失，以KL散度形式呈现，可无缝集成到主流RLVR算法（如GRPO和DAPO）中。值得注意的是，PAPO无需额外数据标注、奖励模型或更强的教师模型。为进一步提升PAPO的训练稳定性，我们引入双重熵损失，有效正则化新KL目标而不损害性能。尽管结构简单，PAPO在多种多模态基准测试中仍带来4.4%-17.5%的整体性能提升，高视觉依赖任务中提升更显著，达8.0%-19.1%。同时，感知错误减少30.5%，表明PAPO显著提升了感知能力。本工作深化了感知监督与核心学习目标的融合，为鼓励视觉锚定推理的新强化学习框架奠定基础。代码与数据将公开用于研究。项目页面：https://mikewangwzhl.github.io/PAPO。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the suboptimal performance of text-centric RLVR methods in multimodal reasoning due to poor visual perception, this work introduces PAPO, a policy gradient algorithm that integrates perception-aware supervision directly into the learning objective. PAPO augments standard RLVR algorithms like GRPO and DAPO with an Implicit Perception Loss—a KL divergence term that encourages perceptual alignment without requiring extra data or teacher models. To stabilize training, it further employs a Double Entropy Loss to regularize the new objective. Experiments show PAPO improves multimodal reasoning performance by 4.4%-17.5% across benchmarks, with gains up to 8.0%-19.1% on vision-heavy tasks, and reduces perception errors by 30.5%, demonstrating its effectiveness in grounding reasoning in visual understanding.
为解决现有文本导向的RLVR方法在多模态推理中因视觉感知能力不足导致的性能下降，本文提出PAPO算法，将感知感知监督直接融入策略梯度优化目标。PAPO通过引入隐式感知损失（KL散度项）增强标准RLVR算法（如GRPO和DAPO）的视觉感知能力，且无需额外数据或教师模型。为提升训练稳定性，引入双重熵损失对新目标进行正则化。实验表明，PAPO在多个多模态基准上提升性能4.4%-17.5%，在视觉依赖性强的任务上提升高达8.0%-19.1%，并使感知错误减少30.5%，有效增强了视觉理解能力。
Task Alignment: A simple and effective proxy for model merging in computer vision
Authors: Pau de Jorge, César Roberto de Souza, Björn Michele, Mert Bülent Sarıyıldız, Philippe Weinzaepfel, Florent Perronnin, Diane Larlus, Yannis Kalantidis
First: 2026-04-14T16:24:04+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-14T16:24:04+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Efficiently merging several models fine-tuned for different tasks, but stemming from the same pretrained base model, is of great practical interest. Despite extensive prior work, most evaluations of model merging in computer vision are restricted to image classification using CLIP, where different classification datasets define different tasks. In this work, our goal is to make model merging more practical and show its relevance on challenging scenarios beyond this specific setting. In most vision scenarios, different tasks rely on trainable and usually heterogeneous decoders. Differently from previous studies with frozen decoders, where merged models can be evaluated right away, the non-trivial cost of decoder training renders hyperparameter selection based on downstream performance impractical. To address this, we introduce the task alignment proxy, and show how it can be used to speed up hyperparameter selection by orders of magnitude while retaining performance. Equipped with the task alignment proxy, we extend the applicability of model merging to multi-task vision models beyond CLIP-based classification.
中文标题/摘要
标题：任务对齐：计算机视觉中模型融合的一种简单有效的代理方法
高效融合多个针对不同任务微调但源自同一预训练基础模型的模型，具有重要的实际意义。尽管已有大量相关研究，但大多数计算机视觉模型融合的评估仍局限于使用CLIP进行图像分类，其中不同分类数据集定义不同任务。本研究旨在使模型融合更具实用性，并展示其在该特定设置之外的挑战性场景中的相关性。在大多数视觉场景中，不同任务依赖可训练且通常异构的解码器。与先前研究中冻结解码器不同，后者可立即评估融合模型，但解码器训练的非平凡成本使得基于下游性能选择超参数不切实际。为此，我们引入任务对齐代理，并展示其如何通过数个数量级加速超参数选择，同时保持性能。借助任务对齐代理，我们将模型融合的应用扩展至基于CLIP的分类之外的多任务视觉模型。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to make model merging practical beyond image classification tasks, this work introduces a task alignment proxy to accelerate hyperparameter selection in vision models with trainable, heterogeneous decoders. Unlike prior methods that assume frozen decoders, the proposed proxy enables efficient evaluation without costly downstream training, significantly speeding up the search process while preserving performance. Experimental results demonstrate that the proxy effectively guides hyperparameter tuning and extends model merging to challenging multi-task vision scenarios beyond CLIP-based classification.
为解决现有模型合并方法受限于冻结解码器和CLIP分类任务的问题，本文提出任务对齐代理，用于指导具有可训练异构解码器的模型合并超参数选择。该代理显著加速了超参数调优过程，同时保持下游性能，使模型合并可扩展至多任务视觉场景，突破了传统方法的适用范围。
Exact Structural Abstraction and Tractability Limits
Authors: Tristan Simas
First: 2026-04-08T17:59:47+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-14T16:23:36+00:00
Comments: 42 pages. 3 tables. Lean 4 formalization available https://doi.org/10.5281/zenodo.19457896
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Any rigorously specified problem determines an admissible-output relation $R$, and the only state distinctions that matter are the classes $s \sim_R s' \iff \mathrm{Adm}_R(s)=\mathrm{Adm}_R(s')$. Every exact correctness claim reduces to the same quotient-recovery problem, and the no-go concerns tractability of the underlying problem, not of its presentation. Exact means agreement with $R$, not zero-error determinism or absence of approximation/randomization in the specification. The exact-semantics quotient theorem identifies admissible-output equivalence as the canonical object recovered by exact relevance certification. Decision, search, approximation, statistical, randomized, horizon, and distributional guarantees instantiate it. Tractable families have a finite primitive basis, but optimizer-quotient realizability is maximal, so quotient shape cannot characterize the frontier. We prove a meta-impossibility theorem for efficiently checkable structural predicates invariant under theorem-forced closure laws of exact certification. Zero-distortion summaries, quotient entropy bounds, and support counting explain them. Same-orbit disagreements across four obstruction families, via action-independent pair-targeted affine witnesses, force contradiction. Consequently no correct problem-tractability classifier on a closure-closed domain yields an exact characterization over these families. Restricting to a closure-closed subdomain helps only by removing orbit gaps. Uniform strict-gap control preserves the full optimizer quotient, while arbitrarily small perturbations can flip relevance and sufficiency. Closure-orbit agreement is forced by correctness, and the same compute-cost barrier extends to optimizer computation, payload/search, and theorem-backed external or transported outputs. The obstruction therefore appears at the level of correctness itself, not any particular output formalism.
中文标题/摘要
标题：精确结构抽象与可处理性极限
任何严格定义的问题都确定一个可接受输出关系 $R$，且仅需关注满足 $s \sim_R s' \iff \mathrm{Adm}_R(s)=\mathrm{Adm}_R(s')$ 的状态类。所有精确正确性断言均归结为同一商空间恢复问题，而不可行性关注的是底层问题本身，而非其表述形式。精确指与 $R$ 一致，而非零错误确定性或规范中无近似/随机化。精确语义商定理指出，可接受输出等价是精确相关性认证恢复的典范对象。决策、搜索、近似、统计、随机、时域及分布保证均体现该定理。可处理族具有有限原始基，但优化器商的实现性为最大，故商结构无法刻画前沿。我们证明了关于高效可验证结构谓词的元不可能定理，该谓词在精确认证的定理强制闭包律下保持不变。零失真摘要、商熵界及支撑计数解释了这些谓词。通过作用无关的成对目标仿射见证，四个障碍族中不同轨道的分歧导致矛盾。因此，在闭包闭域上不存在能对这些族给出精确刻画的正确问题可处理性分类器。仅限制于闭包闭子域有助于消除轨道间隙。统一严格间隙控制可保持完整优化器商，而任意微小扰动可能翻转相关性与充分性。闭包轨道一致性由正确性强制，且相同的计算成本障碍也扩展至优化器计算、负载/搜索及定理支持的外部或传递输出。因此，障碍出现在正确性本身层面，而非任何特定输出形式。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to understand the fundamental limits of tractability in problems with exact semantics, the paper introduces a framework where correctness is defined by admissible-output equivalence classes under a relation R. It establishes that all exact correctness claims reduce to recovering these equivalence classes, and that tractability is determined by the underlying problem structure, not its presentation. The authors prove a meta-impossibility result: no efficient classifier can exactly characterize tractability across certain closure-closed domains due to inherent structural obstructions, demonstrated via affine witnesses that force contradictions across orbits. Even small perturbations can flip relevance and sufficiency, and closure-orbit agreement is required by correctness, extending the barrier to optimizer computation and external outputs.
该研究旨在揭示具有精确语义问题的可计算性根本限制。论文提出，正确性由输出关系R下的可接受输出等价类决定，而可计算性取决于这些等价类的结构而非表示方式。作者证明了一个元不可能定理：在闭包封闭域上，不存在能精确刻画可计算性的高效分类器，因为存在由仿射见证引发的结构性障碍，导致四类障碍家族间出现同轨道分歧。即使限制到子域或施加微小扰动，也可能翻转相关性与充分性，表明该障碍源于正确性本身，而非特定输出形式。
ReXSonoVQA: A Video QA Benchmark for Procedure-Centric Ultrasound Understanding
Authors: Xucheng Wang, Xiaoman Zhang, Sung Eun Kim, Ankit Pal, Pranav Rajpurkar
First: 2026-04-13T02:32:51+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-14T16:22:32+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Ultrasound acquisition requires skilled probe manipulation and real-time adjustments. Vision-language models (VLMs) could enable autonomous ultrasound systems, but existing benchmarks evaluate only static images, not dynamic procedural understanding. We introduce ReXSonoVQA, a video QA benchmark with 514 video clips and 514 questions (249 MCQ, 265 free-response) targeting three competencies: Action-Goal Reasoning, Artifact Resolution & Optimization, and Procedure Context & Planning. Zero-shot evaluation of Gemini 3 Pro, Qwen3.5-397B, LLaVA-Video-72B, and Seed 2.0 Pro shows VLMs can extract some procedural information, but troubleshooting questions remain challenging with minimal gains over text-only baselines, exposing limitations in causal reasoning. ReXSonoVQA enables developing perception systems for ultrasound training, guidance, and robotic automation.
中文标题/摘要
标题：ReXSonoVQA：面向以程序为中心的超声理解的视频问答基准
超声采集需要熟练的探头操作和实时调整。视觉-语言模型（VLMs）可实现自主超声系统，但现有基准仅评估静态图像，无法评估动态程序理解能力。我们引入ReXSonoVQA，包含514个视频片段和514个问题（249道多选题，265道开放式问题），针对三项能力：动作-目标推理、伪影解决与优化、程序上下文与规划。对Gemini 3 Pro、Qwen3.5-397B、LLaVA-Video-72B和Seed 2.0 Pro进行零样本评估显示，VLMs可提取部分程序信息，但故障排除类问题仍具挑战性，仅略优于纯文本基线，暴露出因果推理的局限性。ReXSonoVQA有助于开发用于超声训练、指导和机器人自动化的感知系统。
Summary / 总结
Driven by the need for autonomous ultrasound systems that require dynamic procedural understanding, this paper introduces ReXSonoVQA, a video-based QA benchmark with 514 clips and 514 questions targeting three core competencies: Action-Goal Reasoning, Artifact Resolution & Optimization, and Procedure Context & Planning. Evaluated zero-shot on four large vision-language models, the benchmark reveals that while models can extract some procedural cues from video, they struggle significantly with troubleshooting questions, showing minimal improvement over text-only baselines and exposing deficits in causal reasoning. This highlights the gap between static image benchmarks and real-world ultrasound tasks, providing a foundation for developing perception systems for training, guidance, and robotic automation in ultrasound.
为推动具备动态操作理解能力的自主超声系统发展，本文提出ReXSonoVQA视频问答基准，包含514个视频片段与514道题目，聚焦三个核心能力：动作-目标推理、伪影识别与优化、流程上下文与规划。对四款大型视觉语言模型进行零样本评估发现，尽管模型能提取部分程序性信息，但在故障诊断类问题上表现不佳，与纯文本基线相比提升有限，暴露出因果推理能力的不足。该基准填补了现有静态图像评测与真实超声任务之间的差距，为超声训练、指导及机器人自动化感知系统的研发提供基础。
Speaker effects in language comprehension: An integrative model of language and speaker processing
Authors: Hanlin Wu, Zhenguang G. Cai
Venue: Psychon Bull Rev 33, 138 (2026)
First: 2024-12-10T07:03:06+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-14T16:21:58+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
The identity of a speaker influences language comprehension through modulating perception and expectation. This review explores speaker effects and proposes an integrative model of language and speaker processing that integrates distinct mechanistic perspectives. We argue that speaker effects arise from the interplay between bottom-up perception-based processes, driven by acoustic-episodic memory, and top-down expectation-based processes, driven by a speaker model. We show that language and speaker processing are functionally integrated through multi-level probabilistic processing: prior beliefs about a speaker modulate language processing at the phonetic, lexical, and semantic levels, while the unfolding speech and message continuously update the speaker model, refining broad demographic priors into precise individualized representations. Within this framework, we distinguish between speaker-idiosyncrasy effects arising from familiarity with an individual and speaker-demographics effects arising from social group expectations. We discuss how speaker effects serve as indices for assessing language development and social cognition, and we encourage future research to extend these findings to the emerging domain of artificial intelligence (AI) speakers, as AI agents represent a new class of social interlocutors that are transforming the way we engage in communication.
中文标题/摘要
标题：说话者效应在语言理解中的作用：语言与说话者处理的整合模型
说话者的身份通过调节感知与预期影响语言理解。本文综述了说话者效应，并提出一个整合语言与说话者处理的模型，融合了不同的机制视角。我们认为，说话者效应源于自下而上的感知驱动过程（由听觉-情景记忆驱动）与自上而下的预期驱动过程（由说话者模型驱动）之间的相互作用。我们表明，语言与说话者处理通过多层次概率处理功能整合：关于说话者的先验信念调节语音、词汇和语义层面的语言处理，而正在展开的言语和信息则持续更新说话者模型，将宽泛的人口统计学先验细化为个体化的精确表征。在此框架下，我们区分了源于对个体熟悉度的说话者特异性效应与源于社会群体预期的说话者人口统计学效应。我们讨论了说话者效应作为评估语言发展与社会认知的指标，并鼓励未来研究将这些发现拓展至新兴的人工智能（AI）说话者领域，因为AI代理代表了一种新的社会互动对象，正在改变我们进行交流的方式。
Summary / 总结
This paper investigates how speaker identity shapes language comprehension by proposing an integrative model that combines bottom-up perceptual processes rooted in acoustic-episodic memory with top-down expectation-driven mechanisms based on speaker models. The authors demonstrate that prior beliefs about a speaker dynamically modulate language processing across phonetic, lexical, and semantic levels, while ongoing speech continuously refines these models from broad demographic assumptions into individualized representations. Experimental findings support the distinction between idiosyncratic effects from familiarity and demographic effects from social group expectations, and highlight speaker effects as valuable indicators for studying language development and social cognition, with implications for AI-driven communication systems.
该文探讨了说话者身份如何影响语言理解，提出一个整合模型，将基于听觉-情景记忆的自下而上感知过程与基于说话者模型的自上而下预期过程相结合。研究显示，说话者先验信念会动态调节语音、词汇和语义层面的语言处理，而持续的言语输入则不断更新说话者模型，将宽泛的人群先验细化为个体化表征。实验结果支持将说话者效应区分为因熟悉度产生的个体化效应与因社会群体预期产生的群体效应，并指出其在语言发展与社会认知评估中的价值，同时为人工智能对话系统提供了研究方向。
DINO-Explorer: Active Underwater Discovery via Ego-Motion Compensated Semantic Predictive Coding
Authors: Yuhan Jin, Nayari Marie Lessa, Mariela De Lucas Alvarez, Melvin Laux, Lucas Amparo Barbosa, Frank Kirchner, Rebecca Adam
First: 2026-04-14T16:20:54+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-14T16:20:54+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Marine ecosystem degradation necessitates continuous, scientifically selective underwater monitoring. However, most autonomous underwater vehicles (AUVs) operate as passive data loggers, capturing exhaustive video for offline review and frequently missing transient events of high scientific value. Transitioning to active perception requires a causal, online signal that highlights significant phenomena while suppressing maneuver-induced visual changes. We propose DINO-Explorer, a novelty-aware perception framework driven by a continuous semantic surprise signal. Operating within the latent space of a frozen DINOv3 foundation model, it leverages a lightweight, action-conditioned recurrent predictor to anticipate short-horizon semantic evolution. An efference-copy-inspired module utilizes globally pooled optical flow to discount self-induced visual changes without suppressing genuine environmental novelty. We evaluate this signal on the downstream task of asynchronous event triage under variant telemetry constraints. Results demonstrate that DINO-Explorer provides a robust, bandwidth-efficient attention mechanism. At a fixed operating point, the system retains 78.8% of post-discovery human-reviewer consensus events with a 56.8% trigger confirmation rate, effectively surfacing mission-relevant phenomena. Crucially, ego-motion conditioning suppresses 45.5% of false positives relative to an uncompensated surprise signal baseline. In a replay-side Pareto ablation study, DINO-Explorer robustly dominates the validated peak F1 versus telemetry bandwidth frontier, reducing telemetry bandwidth by 48.2% at the selected operating point while maintaining a 62.2% peak F1 score, successfully concentrating data transmission around human-verified novelty events.
中文标题/摘要
标题：DINO-Explorer：通过自运动补偿的语义预测编码实现主动水下探索
海洋生态系统退化要求持续进行科学选择性的水下监测。然而，大多数自主水下航行器（AUV）仅作为被动数据记录器，捕获大量视频供离线回放，常错过具有高科学价值的瞬态事件。转向主动感知需要一个因果的在线信号，突出显著现象并抑制机动引起的视觉变化。我们提出DINO-Explorer，一种基于连续语义惊喜信号驱动的、具有新颖性感知能力的感知框架。该框架在冻结的DINOv3基础模型的潜在空间中运行，利用轻量级、动作条件化的递归预测器预测短期语义演化。受运动输出副本启发的模块利用全局池化光流，消除自诱导视觉变化而不抑制真实环境新颖性。我们在不同遥测约束下的异步事件分拣下游任务中评估该信号。结果表明，DINO-Explorer提供了一种稳健且带宽高效的注意力机制。在固定工作点下，系统保留了78.8%的人类评审员共识事件，触发确认率为56.8%，有效凸显任务相关现象。关键的是，自运动补偿将假阳性降低了45.5%（相对于未补偿的惊喜信号基线）。在重放端帕累托消融研究中，DINO-Explorer在验证的峰值F1与遥测带宽前沿上稳健主导，所选工作点下遥测带宽减少48.2%，同时保持62.2%的峰值F1得分，成功将数据传输集中于人类验证的新颖事件。
Summary / 总结
Driven by the need for efficient underwater monitoring to track marine ecosystem changes, DINO-Explorer introduces an active perception framework that uses ego-motion compensated semantic predictive coding to prioritize scientifically valuable events. It operates within a frozen DINOv3 latent space, employing a lightweight recurrent predictor to forecast short-term semantic shifts and an efference-copy module to filter out visual artifacts caused by vehicle motion. Experimental results show the system achieves 78.8% retention of human-verified events with a 56.8% trigger confirmation rate, while reducing false positives by 45.5% compared to an uncompensated baseline. In a Pareto analysis, it maintains a 62.2% peak F1 score while cutting telemetry bandwidth by 48.2%, effectively focusing data transmission on novel, mission-critical phenomena.
为应对海洋生态系统退化需持续高效监测的需求，DINO-Explorer 提出一种基于自运动补偿语义预测编码的主动感知框架。该框架在冻结的 DINOv3 潜在空间中运行，结合轻量级递归预测器预测短期语义变化，并通过类“前馈副本”模块过滤由车辆运动引起的视觉干扰。实验表明，该系统在固定工作点下保留 78.8% 的人工验证事件，触发确认率达 56.8%，相比未补偿基线减少 45.5% 的误报。在帕累托分析中，其在保持 62.2% 最高 F1 分数的同时，降低遥测带宽 48.2%，有效聚焦于科学价值高的新颖事件传输。
Building evidence-based knowledge bases from full-text literature for disease-specific biomedical reasoning
Authors: Chang Zong, Sicheng Lv, Si-tu Xue, Huilin Zheng, Jian Wan, Lei Zhang
First: 2026-03-30T11:53:45+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-14T16:20:47+00:00
Comments: 30 pages, 5 figures, 12 tables
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Biomedical knowledge resources often either preserve evidence as unstructured text or compress it into flat triples that omit study design, provenance, and quantitative support. Here we present EvidenceNet, a disease-specific dataset of record-level evidence collections and corresponding graph representations derived from full-text biomedical literature. EvidenceNet uses a large language model (LLM)-assisted pipeline to extract experimentally grounded findings as structured evidence records, normalize biomedical entities, score evidence quality, and connect related records through typed semantic relations. We release EvidenceNet-HCC with 7,872 evidence records and a corresponding graph with 10,328 nodes and 49,756 edges, and EvidenceNet-CRC with 6,622 records and a corresponding graph with 8,795 nodes and 39,361 edges. Technical validation shows high component fidelity, including 98.3% field-level extraction accuracy, 100.0% high-confidence entity-link accuracy, 87.5% fusion integrity, and 90.0% semantic relation-type accuracy. Downstream analyses show that the data support retrieval-augmented question answering and graph-based tasks such as future link prediction and target prioritization. These results establish EvidenceNet as a disease-specific biomedical knowledge base dataset for evidence-aware analysis and reuse.

Token Encoding for Semantic Recovery
Authors: Jingzhi Hu, Geoffrey Ye Li
First: 2026-04-14T16:20:28+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-14T16:20:28+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Token-based semantic communication is promising for future wireless networks, as it can compact semantic tokens under very limited channel capacity. However, harsh wireless channels often cause missing tokens, leading to severe distortion that prevents reliable semantic recovery at the receiver. In this article, we propose a token encoding framework for robust semantic recovery (TokCode), which incurs no additional transmission overhead and supports plug-and-play deployment. For efficient token encoder optimization, we develop a sentence-semantic-guided foundation model adaptation algorithm (SFMA) that avoids costly end-to-end training. Based on simulation results on prompt-based generative image transmission, TokCode mitigates semantic distortion and can approach the performance upper-bound, even under harsh channels where 40% to 60% of tokens are randomly lost.

为在受限无线信道下实现高效语义通信，本文提出TokCode框架，可在令牌丢失情况下仍恢复语义。该框架采用句义引导的基础模型适配算法（SFMA）优化令牌编码器，无需端到端训练，避免额外开销并支持即插即用部署。在基于提示的生成图像传输实验中，即使40%至60%的令牌随机丢失，TokCode仍能显著降低语义失真并逼近性能上限。
Grasp in Gaussians: Fast Monocular Reconstruction of Dynamic Hand-Object Interactions
Authors: Ayce Idil Aytekin, Xu Chen, Zhengyang Shen, Thabo Beeler, Helge Rhodin, Rishabh Dabral, Christian Theobalt
First: 2026-04-14T16:19:12+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-14T16:19:12+00:00
Comments: Project page: https://aidilayce.github.io/GraG-page/
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
We present Grasp in Gaussians (GraG), a fast and robust method for reconstructing dynamic 3D hand-object interactions from a single monocular video. Unlike recent approaches that optimize heavy neural representations, our method focuses on tracking the hand and the object efficiently, once initialized from pretrained large models. Our key insight is that accurate and temporally stable hand-object motion can be recovered using a compact Sum-of-Gaussians (SoG) representation, revived from classical tracking literature and integrated with generative Gaussian-based initializations. We initialize object pose and geometry using a video-adapted SAM3D pipeline, then convert the resulting dense Gaussian representation into a lightweight SoG via subsampling. This compact representation enables efficient and fast tracking while preserving geometric fidelity. For the hand, we adopt a complementary strategy: starting from off-the-shelf monocular hand pose initialization, we refine hand motion using simple yet effective 2D joint and depth alignment losses, avoiding per-frame refinement of a detailed 3D hand appearance model while maintaining stable articulation. Extensive experiments on public benchmarks demonstrate that GraG reconstructs temporally coherent hand-object interactions on long sequences 6.4x faster than prior work while improving object reconstruction by 13.4% and reducing hand's per-joint position error by over 65%.
中文标题/摘要
标题：高斯抓取：从单目视频快速重建动态手-物体交互
我们提出了高斯抓取（GraG），一种从单目视频快速且稳健地重建动态3D手-物体交互的方法。与近期优化复杂神经表示的方法不同，我们的方法在从预训练大模型初始化后，专注于高效追踪手和物体。我们的核心见解是：通过经典的跟踪文献中复兴的紧凑高斯和（SoG）表示，并结合生成式高斯初始化，可以恢复出准确且时间稳定的物体-手运动。我们使用视频适配的SAM3D流程初始化物体姿态和几何结构，再通过下采样将得到的密集高斯表示转换为轻量级SoG，从而实现高效快速追踪并保持几何保真度。对于手部，我们采用互补策略：从现成的单目手部姿态初始化出发，通过简单而有效的2D关节与深度对齐损失优化手部运动，避免逐帧细化复杂的3D手部外观模型，同时保持稳定关节运动。在公开基准上的大量实验表明，GraG在长序列上比现有方法快6.4倍，物体重建精度提升13.4%，手部关节位置误差降低超过65%。
