Motivated by the need for fast and accurate reconstruction of dynamic hand-object interactions from monocular video, Grasp in Gaussians (GraG) introduces a method that leverages a compact Sum-of-Gaussians (SoG) representation for efficient tracking, initialized from pretrained large models. It first estimates object pose and geometry via a video-adapted SAM3D pipeline, then converts the dense Gaussian output into a lightweight SoG through subsampling. For the hand, it uses off-the-shelf monocular pose initialization and refines motion with 2D joint and depth alignment losses, avoiding detailed 3D appearance optimization. Experiments show GraG achieves 6.4x faster reconstruction than prior methods, improves object reconstruction by 13.4%, and reduces hand joint position error by over 65% on long sequences.
为实现从单目视频中高效准确重建动态手-物体交互，GraG提出一种基于高斯和紧凑表示的方法。它通过视频适配的SAM3D管线初始化物体姿态与几何，并通过下采样转化为轻量级SoG表示，以实现高效跟踪并保持几何保真度。对于手部，它从现成单目姿态初始化出发，利用2D关节与深度对齐损失进行运动优化，避免逐帧细化复杂3D外观模型。实验表明，GraG在长序列上比前序方法快6.4倍，物体重建精度提升13.4%，手部关节位置误差降低超65%。
MoshiRAG: Asynchronous Knowledge Retrieval for Full-Duplex Speech Language Models
Authors: Chung-Ming Chien, Manu Orsini, Eugene Kharitonov, Neil Zeghidour, Karen Livescu, Alexandre Défossez
First: 2026-04-14T16:17:52+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-14T16:17:52+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Speech-to-speech language models have recently emerged to enhance the naturalness of conversational AI. In particular, full-duplex models are distinguished by their real-time interactivity, including handling of pauses, interruptions, and backchannels. However, improving their factuality remains an open challenge. While scaling the model size could address this gap, it would make real-time inference prohibitively expensive. In this work, we propose MoshiRAG, a modular approach that combines a compact full-duplex interface with selective retrieval to access more powerful knowledge sources. Our asynchronous framework enables the model to identify knowledge-demanding queries and ground its responses in external information. By leveraging the natural temporal gap between response onset and the delivery of core information, the retrieval process can be completed while maintaining a natural conversation flow. With this approach, MoshiRAG achieves factuality comparable to the best publicly released non-duplex speech language models while preserving the interactivity inherent to full-duplex systems. Moreover, our flexible design supports plug-and-play retrieval methods without retraining and demonstrates strong performance on out-of-domain mathematical reasoning tasks.
中文标题/摘要
标题：MoshiRAG：面向全双工语音语言模型的异步知识检索
近期出现的语音到语音语言模型旨在提升对话式AI的自然度。特别是全双工模型以其实时交互性著称，包括处理停顿、打断和应答反馈。然而，提升其事实性仍是一个开放挑战。虽然扩大模型规模可缓解此问题，但会使得实时推理成本过高。本文提出MoshiRAG，一种模块化方法，结合紧凑的全双工接口与选择性检索，以访问更强大的知识源。我们的异步框架使模型能识别知识需求型查询，并基于外部信息支撑其回答。通过利用响应启动与核心信息传递之间的自然时间间隔，检索过程可在保持自然对话流程的同时完成。该方法使MoshiRAG在保持全双工系统固有交互性的同时，实现了与当前最佳公开非全双工语音语言模型相当的事实性。此外，我们的灵活设计支持无需再训练即可插拔式使用多种检索方法，并在跨领域数学推理任务中表现出色。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to enhance factuality in full-duplex speech language models without sacrificing real-time interactivity, this work introduces MoshiRAG, a modular system that combines a compact full-duplex interface with asynchronous knowledge retrieval. The method identifies knowledge-demanding queries during conversation and retrieves relevant external information in the temporal gap between response initiation and core content delivery, enabling grounding in external knowledge without disrupting conversational flow. Experimental results show that MoshiRAG achieves factuality matching the best publicly available non-duplex models while preserving full-duplex interactivity, and its flexible architecture supports plug-and-play retrieval methods with strong performance on out-of-domain mathematical reasoning tasks.
为在不牺牲实时交互性的前提下提升全双工语音语言模型的事实准确性，本文提出MoshiRAG，该系统结合紧凑的全双工接口与异步知识检索。方法在对话中识别需要知识支持的查询，并利用响应启动与核心信息送达之间的自然时间间隙完成外部信息检索，从而在不打断对话流程的情况下实现知识增强。实验表明，MoshiRAG在事实准确性上可媲美当前最优的非全双工模型，同时保留全双工交互特性，其灵活架构支持即插即用的检索方法，并在跨领域数学推理任务中表现优异。
ASTRA: Let Arbitrary Subjects Transform in Video Editing
Authors: Fei Shen, Weihao Xu, Rui Yan, Dong Zhang, Xiangbo Shu, Jinhui Tang, Maocheng Zhao
First: 2025-10-01T17:59:56+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-14T16:17:31+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
While existing video editing methods excel with single subjects, they struggle in dense, multi-subject scenes, frequently suffering from attention dilution and mask boundary entanglement that cause attribute leakage and temporal instability. To address this, we propose ASTRA, a training-free framework for seamless, arbitrary-subject video editing. Without requiring model fine-tuning, ASTRA precisely manipulates multiple designated subjects while strictly preserving non-target regions. It achieves this via two core components: a prompt-guided multimodal alignment module that generates robust conditions to mitigate attention dilution, and a prior-based mask retargeting module that produces temporally coherent mask sequences to resolve boundary entanglement. Functioning as a versatile plug-and-play module, ASTRA seamlessly integrates with diverse mask-driven video generators. Extensive experiments on our newly constructed benchmark, MSVBench, demonstrate that ASTRA consistently outperforms state-of-the-art methods. Code, models, and data are available at https://github.com/XWH-A/ASTRA.
中文标题/摘要
标题：ASTRA：让任意主体在视频编辑中自由变换
现有视频编辑方法在处理单主体场景时表现优异，但在密集多主体场景中常因注意力分散和掩码边界纠缠导致属性泄露与时间不稳定。为此，我们提出ASTRA，一种无需训练的无缝任意主体视频编辑框架。无需模型微调，ASTRA可精准操控多个指定主体，同时严格保留非目标区域。其核心包含两个模块：基于提示的多模态对齐模块，生成鲁棒条件以缓解注意力分散；基于先验的掩码重定向模块，生成时序一致的掩码序列以解决边界纠缠。ASTRA作为通用即插即用模块，可无缝集成至各类掩码驱动的视频生成器。在我们新构建的MSVBench基准测试中，ASTRA持续优于现有最先进方法。代码、模型与数据见https://github.com/XWH-A/ASTRA。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitations of existing video editing methods in handling dense, multi-subject scenes where attention dilution and mask boundary entanglement cause attribute leakage and temporal instability, ASTRA introduces a training-free framework for arbitrary-subject video editing. It manipulates multiple designated subjects while preserving non-target regions through a prompt-guided multimodal alignment module that reduces attention dilution and a prior-based mask retargeting module that ensures temporally coherent masks. Evaluated on MSVBench, ASTRA consistently outperforms state-of-the-art methods across diverse editing tasks without requiring model fine-tuning.
为了解决现有视频编辑方法在密集多主体场景中因注意力分散和掩码边界纠缠导致的属性泄露与时间不稳定问题，ASTRA 提出了一种无需训练的任意主体视频编辑框架。它通过提示引导的多模态对齐模块缓解注意力分散，通过基于先验的掩码重定向模块生成时序一致的掩码序列以解决边界纠缠，从而在不微调模型的前提下精准操控多个指定主体并保持非目标区域不变。在新构建的 MSVBench 数据集上，ASTRA 在多种编辑任务中持续优于现有最先进方法。
Subspace-Guided Feature Reconstruction for Unsupervised Anomaly Localization
Authors: Katsuya Hotta, Chao Zhang, Yoshihiro Hagihara, Takuya Akashi
First: 2023-09-25T06:58:57+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-14T16:17:05+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Unsupervised anomaly localization aims to identify anomalous regions that deviate from normal sample patterns. Most recent methods perform feature matching or reconstruction for the target sample with pre-trained deep neural networks. However, they still struggle to address challenging anomalies because the deep embeddings stored in the memory bank can be less powerful and informative. Specifically, prior methods often overly rely on the finite resources stored in the memory bank, which leads to low robustness to unseen targets. In this paper, we propose a novel subspace-guided feature reconstruction framework to pursue adaptive feature approximation for anomaly localization. It first learns to construct low-dimensional subspaces from the given nominal samples, and then learns to reconstruct the given deep target embedding by linearly combining the subspace basis vectors using the self-expressive model. Our core is that, despite the limited resources in the memory bank, the out-of-bank features can be alternatively ``mimicked'' to adaptively model the target. Moreover, we propose a sampling method that leverages the sparsity of subspaces and allows the feature reconstruction to depend only on a small resource subset, contributing to less memory overhead. Extensive experiments on three benchmark datasets demonstrate that our approach generally achieves state-of-the-art anomaly localization performance.
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitation of existing unsupervised anomaly localization methods that rely heavily on finite memory bank resources, this paper proposes a subspace-guided feature reconstruction framework. The method first learns low-dimensional subspaces from nominal samples, then reconstructs target embeddings by linearly combining subspace basis vectors via a self-expressive model, enabling adaptive approximation even when target features are not stored in the memory bank. Experimental results across three benchmarks show that this approach achieves state-of-the-art performance in anomaly localization while reducing memory overhead through sparse subspace sampling.
为了解决现有无监督异常定位方法过度依赖有限记忆库资源导致鲁棒性差的问题，本文提出了一种子空间引导的特征重建框架。该方法首先从正常样本中学习低维子空间，再通过自表达模型线性组合子空间基向量重建目标特征，从而在目标特征未存于记忆库时仍能自适应建模。在三个基准数据集上的实验表明，该方法在异常定位性能上达到当前最优，同时通过子空间稀疏采样降低了内存开销。
Edu-MMBias: A Three-Tier Multimodal Benchmark for Auditing Social Bias in Vision-Language Models under Educational Contexts
Authors: Ruijia Li, Mingzi Zhang, Zengyi Yu, Yuang Wei, Bo Jiang
First: 2026-04-11T13:12:22+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-14T16:14:36+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
As Vision-Language Models (VLMs) become integral to educational decision-making, ensuring their fairness is paramount. However, current text-centric evaluations neglect the visual modality, leaving an unregulated channel for latent social biases. To bridge this gap, we present Edu-MMBias, a systematic auditing framework grounded in the tri-component model of attitudes from social psychology. This framework diagnoses bias across three hierarchical dimensions: cognitive, affective, and behavioral. Utilizing a specialized generative pipeline that incorporates a self-correct mechanism and human-in-the-loop verification, we synthesize contamination-resistant student profiles to conduct a holistic stress test on state-of-the-art VLMs. Our extensive audit reveals critical, counter-intuitive patterns: models exhibit a compensatory class bias favoring lower-status narratives while simultaneously harboring deep-seated health and racial stereotypes. Crucially, we find that visual inputs act as a safety backdoor, triggering a resurgence of biases that bypass text-based alignment safeguards and revealing a systematic misalignment between latent cognition and final decision-making. The contributions of this paper are available at: https://anonymous.4open.science/r/EduMMBias-63B2.
Summary / 总结
Driven by the need to audit social bias in Vision-Language Models (VLMs) used in education, this study introduces Edu-MMBias, a three-tier multimodal benchmark grounded in social psychology’s tri-component model of attitudes. It evaluates bias across cognitive, affective, and behavioral dimensions using a generative pipeline with self-correction and human verification to create contamination-resistant student profiles. Experiments reveal that VLMs exhibit compensatory bias favoring lower-status narratives while embedding strong health and racial stereotypes, and that visual inputs can trigger bias resurgence, bypassing text-based safeguards and exposing misalignment between latent cognition and final decisions.
为审计教育场景中视觉语言模型（VLM）的社会偏见，本研究提出Edu-MMBias，一个基于社会心理学态度三成分模型的三层次多模态基准。该框架通过自校正与人工验证生成抗污染的学生档案，从认知、情感和行为三个维度评估偏见。实验发现，模型虽补偿性偏向低地位叙事，却深藏健康与种族刻板印象，且视觉输入可绕过文本对齐机制，触发偏见反弹，揭示潜藏认知与最终决策间的系统性错位。
Safety at Scale: A Comprehensive Survey of Large Model and Agent Safety
Authors: Xingjun Ma, Yifeng Gao, Yixu Wang, Ruofan Wang, Xin Wang, Ye Sun, Yifan Ding, Hengyuan Xu, Yunhao Chen, Yunhan Zhao, Hanxun Huang, Yige Li, Yutao Wu, Jiaming Zhang, Xiang Zheng, Yang Bai, Zuxuan Wu, Xipeng Qiu, Jingfeng Zhang, Yiming Li, Xudong Han, Haonan Li, Jun Sun, Cong Wang, Jindong Gu, Baoyuan Wu, Siheng Chen, Tianwei Zhang, Yang Liu, Mingming Gong, Tongliang Liu, Shirui Pan, Cihang Xie, Tianyu Pang, Yinpeng Dong, Ruoxi Jia, Yang Zhang, Shiqing Ma, Xiangyu Zhang, Neil Gong, Chaowei Xiao, Sarah Erfani, Tim Baldwin, Bo Li, Masashi Sugiyama, Dacheng Tao, James Bailey, Yu-Gang Jiang
First: 2025-02-02T05:14:22+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-14T16:10:41+00:00
Comments: 706 papers, 60 pages, 3 figures, 14 tables; GitHub: https://github.com/xingjunm/Awesome-Large-Model-Safety
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
The rapid advancement of large models, driven by their exceptional abilities in learning and generalization through large-scale pre-training, has reshaped the landscape of Artificial Intelligence (AI). These models are now foundational to a wide range of applications, including conversational AI, recommendation systems, autonomous driving, content generation, medical diagnostics, and scientific discovery. However, their widespread deployment also exposes them to significant safety risks, raising concerns about robustness, reliability, and ethical implications. This survey provides a systematic review of current safety research on large models, covering Vision Foundation Models (VFMs), Large Language Models (LLMs), Vision-Language Pre-training (VLP) models, Vision-Language Models (VLMs), Diffusion Models (DMs), and large-model-powered Agents. Our contributions are summarized as follows: (1) We present a comprehensive taxonomy of safety threats to these models, including adversarial attacks, data poisoning, backdoor attacks, jailbreak and prompt injection attacks, energy-latency attacks, data and model extraction attacks, and emerging agent-specific threats. (2) We review defense strategies proposed for each type of attacks if available and summarize the commonly used datasets and benchmarks for safety research. (3) Building on this, we identify and discuss the open challenges in large model safety, emphasizing the need for comprehensive safety evaluations, scalable and effective defense mechanisms, and sustainable data practices. More importantly, we highlight the necessity of collective efforts from the research community and international collaboration. Our work can serve as a useful reference for researchers and practitioners, fostering the ongoing development of comprehensive defense systems and platforms to safeguard AI models.
Summary / 总结
Driven by the rapid deployment of large models across critical domains like autonomous driving and medical diagnostics, this survey addresses the urgent need for safety assurance as these models become foundational to AI systems. It systematically categorizes safety threats across major model types—including LLMs, VLMs, and diffusion models—covering adversarial attacks, prompt injection, data extraction, and agent-specific vulnerabilities. The paper reviews existing defense strategies and benchmark datasets, then identifies key open challenges such as scalable defenses and sustainable data practices. Experimental findings are not presented as the work is a survey; however, it synthesizes existing research to highlight the necessity of coordinated global efforts to build robust safety frameworks for large models.
随着大模型在自动驾驶和医疗诊断等关键领域广泛应用，本综述旨在应对这些模型作为AI系统基础所引发的安全挑战。文章系统梳理了针对各类大模型（如LLM、VLM、扩散模型）的安全威胁，包括对抗攻击、数据投毒、越狱攻击及代理特有漏洞，并回顾了各威胁类型对应的防御策略与基准数据集。同时指出当前主要挑战：缺乏可扩展的防御机制、评估体系不完善、数据实践不可持续。文章强调，构建大模型安全体系需全球研究界协同合作。
Pi-HOC: Pairwise 3D Human-Object Contact Estimation
Authors: Sravan Chittupalli, Ayush Jain, Dong Huang
First: 2026-04-14T16:09:56+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-14T16:09:56+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Resolving real-world human-object interactions in images is a many-to-many challenge, in which disentangling fine-grained concurrent physical contact is particularly difficult. Existing semantic contact estimation methods are either limited to single-human settings or require object geometries (e.g., meshes) in addition to the input image. Current state-of-the-art leverages powerful VLM for category-level semantics but struggles with multi-human scenarios and scales poorly in inference. We introduce Pi-HOC, a single-pass, instance-aware framework for dense 3D semantic contact prediction of all human-object pairs. Pi-HOC detects instances, creates dedicated human-object (HO) tokens for each pair, and refines them using an InteractionFormer. A SAM-based decoder then predicts dense contact on SMPL human meshes for each human-object pair. On the MMHOI and DAMON datasets, Pi-HOC significantly improves accuracy and localization over state-of-the-art methods while achieving 20x higher throughput. We further demonstrate that predicted contacts improve SAM-3D image-to-mesh reconstruction via a test-time optimization algorithm and enable referential contact prediction from language queries without additional training.
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the difficulty of accurately estimating fine-grained, concurrent 3D human-object contacts in real-world images, especially in multi-human settings, Pi-HOC introduces a single-pass, instance-aware framework that predicts dense 3D semantic contact for all human-object pairs. It detects human instances, generates dedicated HO tokens for each pair, refines them via an InteractionFormer, and uses a SAM-based decoder to predict contact on SMPL meshes. Experiments on MMHOI and DAMON show Pi-HOC achieves significantly higher accuracy and localization than prior methods while offering 20x faster inference. Additionally, predicted contacts enhance SAM-3D mesh reconstruction and enable language-guided contact prediction without retraining.
为解决真实图像中多人场景下细粒度、并发的人物体接触估计难题——尤其在无物体几何信息条件下——Pi-HOC提出一种单次推理、实例感知的框架，可对所有人物体对进行密集3D语义接触预测。该框架检测人体实例，为每对人物体生成专用HO令牌，通过InteractionFormer进行优化，并借助SAM解码器预测SMPL网格上的接触点。在MMHOI和DAMON数据集上，Pi-HOC在精度与定位上显著优于现有方法，推理速度提升20倍。此外，预测的接触点可提升SAM-3D的图像到网格重建效果，并支持无需额外训练的语言引导接触预测。
Sparse Growing Transformer: Training-Time Sparse Depth Allocation via Progressive Attention Looping
Authors: Yao Chen, Yilong Chen, Yinqi Yang, Junyuan Shang, Zhenyu Zhang, Zefeng Zhang, Shuaiyi Nie, Shuohuan Wang, Yu Sun, Hua Wu, HaiFeng Wang, Tingwen Liu
First: 2026-03-25T06:55:33+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-14T16:04:50+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Existing approaches to increasing the effective depth of Transformers predominantly rely on parameter reuse, extending computation through recursive execution. Under this paradigm, the network structure remains static along the training timeline, and additional computational depth is uniformly assigned to entire blocks at the parameter level. This rigidity across training time and parameter space leads to substantial computational redundancy during training. In contrast, we argue that depth allocation during training should not be a static preset, but rather a progressively growing structural process. Our systematic analysis reveals a deep-to-shallow maturation trajectory across layers, where high-entropy attention heads play a crucial role in semantic integration. Motivated by this observation, we introduce the Sparse Growing Transformer (SGT). SGT is a training-time sparse depth allocation framework that progressively extends recurrence from deeper to shallower layers via targeted attention looping on informative heads. This mechanism induces structural sparsity by selectively increasing depth only for a small subset of parameters as training evolves. Extensive experiments across multiple parameter scales demonstrate that SGT consistently outperforms training-time static block-level looping baselines under comparable settings, while reducing the additional training FLOPs overhead from approximately 16--20% to only 1--3% relative to a standard Transformer backbone.
中文标题/摘要
标题：稀疏增长变换器：通过渐进注意力循环实现训练时稀疏深度分配
现有提升Transformer有效深度的方法主要依赖参数复用，通过递归执行扩展计算。在此范式下，网络结构在训练期间保持静态，额外计算深度在参数层面均匀分配至整个块。这种在训练时间和参数空间中的刚性导致训练期间大量计算冗余。相反，我们认为训练期间的深度分配不应是静态预设，而应是一个渐进增长的结构化过程。我们的系统性分析揭示了层间从深层到浅层的成熟轨迹，其中高熵注意力头在语义整合中起关键作用。受此启发，我们提出稀疏增长变换器（SGT）。SGT是一种训练时稀疏深度分配框架，通过在信息性头上的目标注意力循环，从深层渐进扩展至浅层递归。该机制通过仅在训练过程中选择性增加小部分参数的深度，诱导结构稀疏性。在多个参数规模上的广泛实验表明，SGT在相同设置下持续优于训练时静态块级循环基线，同时将额外训练FLOPs开销从约16-20%降低至仅1-3%（相对于标准Transformer骨干网络）。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the inefficiency of static depth allocation in Transformers, which causes unnecessary computational redundancy, the paper proposes the Sparse Growing Transformer (SGT), a training-time sparse depth allocation method that progressively extends recurrence from deeper to shallower layers by looping attention on high-entropy heads. This targeted approach induces structural sparsity, increasing depth only for a small subset of parameters as training evolves. Experimental results across multiple scales show SGT outperforms static block-level baselines while reducing additional training FLOPs overhead from 16–20% to just 1–3% relative to a standard Transformer.
为了解决Transformer训练中静态深度分配导致的计算冗余问题，本文提出稀疏增长Transformer（SGT），该方法通过在训练过程中逐步从深层向浅层扩展注意力循环，仅对高熵注意力头进行深度增长，从而实现结构稀疏性。实验表明，SGT在多个规模上优于静态块级循环基线，同时将额外训练FLOPs开销从16–20%降低至仅1–3%。
MetFuse: Figurative Fusion between Metonymy and Metaphor
Authors: Saptarshi Ghosh, Tianyu Jiang
Venue: ACL 2026
First: 2026-04-14T16:02:24+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-14T16:02:24+00:00
Comments: ACL 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Metonymy and metaphor often co-occur in natural language, yet computational work has studied them largely in isolation. We introduce a framework that transforms a literal sentence into three figurative variants: metonymic, metaphoric, and hybrid. Using this framework, we construct MetFuse, the first dedicated dataset of figurative fusion between metonymy and metaphor, containing 1,000 human-verified meaning-aligned quadruplets totaling 4,000 sentences. Extrinsic experiments on eight existing benchmarks show that augmenting training data with MetFuse consistently improves both metonymy and metaphor classification, with hybrid examples yielding the largest gains on metonymy tasks. Using this dataset, we also analyze how the presence of one figurative type impacts another. Our findings show that both human annotators and large language models better identify metonymy in hybrid sentences than in metonymy-only sentences, demonstrating that the presence of a metaphor makes a metonymic noun more explicit. Our dataset is publicly available at: https://github.com/cincynlp/MetFuse.
中文标题/摘要
标题：MetFuse：隐喻与转喻的意象融合
转喻与隐喻常在自然语言中共同出现，但计算研究多将其独立处理。我们提出一种框架，可将字面句转化为三种意象变体：转喻型、隐喻型和混合型。基于此框架，我们构建了MetFuse，这是首个专注于转喻与隐喻意象融合的专用数据集，包含1000组经人工验证的语义对齐四元组，共计4000句。在八个现有基准上的外在实验表明，用MetFuse扩充训练数据可一致提升转喻与隐喻分类性能，其中混合型样本在转喻任务上收益最大。利用该数据集，我们还分析了一种意象类型对另一种的影响：发现人类标注者与大语言模型在混合句中比仅含转喻句中更易识别转喻，表明隐喻的存在使转喻名词更显性。数据集公开获取地址：https://github.com/cincynlp/MetFuse。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the frequent co-occurrence of metonymy and metaphor in natural language but their limited joint study in computational models, the authors introduce MetFuse, a framework that generates three figurative variants—metonymic, metaphoric, and hybrid—from literal sentences. Using this framework, they construct a dataset of 1,000 human-verified quadruplets (4,000 sentences) specifically designed to capture figurative fusion. Experiments on eight benchmarks show that incorporating MetFuse improves both metonymy and metaphor classification, with hybrid examples delivering the strongest gains for metonymy. Further analysis reveals that hybrid sentences enhance metonymy detection for both humans and large language models, suggesting metaphor enhances metonymic clarity.
鉴于隐喻与转喻在自然语言中常共现但计算模型对其联合研究不足，作者提出MetFuse框架，从字面句生成转喻、隐喻及混合三种修辞变体。基于此框架构建了1000组人工验证的语义对齐四元组（共4000句），专用于转喻与隐喻的融合研究。在八个基准测试中，加入MetFuse数据可提升转喻与隐喻分类效果，其中混合句对转喻任务提升最显著。进一步分析表明，混合句使人类标注者与大语言模型更易识别转喻，说明隐喻的存在增强了转喻名词的明确性。
Radar-Camera BEV Multi-Task Learning with Cross-Task Attention Bridge for Joint 3D Detection and Segmentation
Authors: Ahmet İnanç, Özgür Erkent
First: 2026-04-14T16:00:53+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-14T16:00:53+00:00
Comments: 8 pages, 5 figures, 3 Tables, submitted to a venue for consideration
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Bird's-eye-view (BEV) representations are the dominant paradigm for 3D perception in autonomous driving, providing a unified spatial canvas where detection and segmentation features are geometrically registered to the same physical coordinate system. However, existing radar-camera fusion methods treat these tasks in isolation, missing the opportunity to share complementary information between them: detection features encode object-level geometry that can sharpen segmentation boundaries, while segmentation features provide dense semantic context that can anchor detection. We propose \textbf{CTAB} (Cross-Task Attention Bridge), a bidirectional module that exchanges features between detection and segmentation branches via multi-scale deformable attention in shared BEV space. CTAB is integrated into a multi-task framework with an Instance Normalization-based segmentation decoder and learnable BEV upsampling to provide a more detailed BEV representation. On nuScenes, CTAB improves segmentation on 7 classes over the joint multi-task baseline at essentially neutral detection. On a 4-class subset (drivable area, pedestrian crossing, walkway, vehicle), our joint multi-task model reaches comparable mIoU on 4 classes while simultaneously providing 3D detection.
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to better integrate 3D detection and segmentation in radar-camera fusion for autonomous driving, the authors propose CTAB, a bidirectional attention module that exchanges multi-scale deformable features between detection and segmentation branches in a shared BEV space. By leveraging detection’s object-level geometry to refine segmentation boundaries and segmentation’s dense context to anchor detection, CTAB enhances joint learning. Experiments on nuScenes show CTAB improves segmentation mIoU across 7 classes without harming detection performance, and on a 4-class subset, achieves comparable segmentation accuracy while simultaneously delivering 3D detection.
为提升自动驾驶中雷达-相机融合下的3D检测与分割联合性能，本文提出CTAB模块，通过多尺度可变形注意力在共享的BEV空间中双向交换检测与分割分支的特征。该模块集成于多任务框架中，结合实例归一化与可学习BEV上采样，利用检测的物体几何信息优化分割边界，同时借助分割的密集语义上下文增强检测定位。在nuScenes数据集上，CTAB在7类分割任务中显著提升mIoU，且对检测性能影响微弱；在4类子集上，实现与基线相当的分割精度的同时，同步输出3D检测结果。
M3D-Stereo: A Multiple-Medium and Multiple-Degradation Dataset for Stereo Image Restoration
Authors: Deqing Yang, Yingying Liu, Qicong Wang, Zhi Zeng, Dajiang Lu, Yibin Tian
First: 2026-04-14T16:00:42+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-14T16:00:42+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Image restoration under adverse conditions, such as underwater, haze or fog, and low-light environments, remains a highly challenging problem due to complex physical degradations and severe information loss. Existing datasets are predominantly limited to a single degradation type or heavily rely on synthetic data without stereo consistency, inherently restricting their applicability in real-world scenarios. To address this, we introduce M3D-Stereo, a stereo dataset with 7904 high-resolution image pairs for image restoration research acquired in multiple media with multiple controlled degradation levels. It encompasses four degradation scenarios: underwater scatter, haze/fog, underwater low-light, and haze low-light. Each scenario forms a subset, and is divided into six levels of progressive degradation, allowing fine-grained evaluations of restoration methods with increasing severity of degradation. Collected via a laboratory setup, the dataset provides aligned stereo image pairs along with their pixel-wise consistent clear ground truths. Two restoration tasks, single-level and mixed-level degradation, were performed to verify its validity. M3D-Stereo establishes a better controlled and more realistic benchmark to evaluate image restoration and stereo matching methods in complex degradation environments. It is made public under LGPLv3 license.
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for realistic stereo image restoration benchmarks under diverse degradations, M3D-Stereo introduces a dataset of 7904 high-resolution stereo image pairs captured across four degradation types—underwater scatter, haze/fog, underwater low-light, and haze low-light—each with six progressive severity levels. Collected in a controlled lab environment with aligned stereo pairs and pixel-wise ground truths, the dataset enables fine-grained evaluation of restoration methods under mixed and single-level degradation. Experimental validation confirms its utility in assessing both image restoration and stereo matching performance under complex, real-world-like conditions.
为解决现有数据集在单一退化类型或合成数据上缺乏立体一致性的问题，M3D-Stereo构建了一个包含7904对高分辨率立体图像的公开数据集，涵盖水下散射、雾霾、水下低光和雾霾低光四种退化场景，每种场景细分为六个渐进退化等级。数据在实验室环境下采集，提供配对立体图像与像素级清晰真值，支持单级与混合退化场景下的恢复方法评估。实验验证表明，该数据集能有效用于复杂退化环境下的图像恢复与立体匹配性能评估。
E2E-Fly: An Integrated Training-to-Deployment System for End-to-End Quadrotor Autonomy
Authors: Fangyu Sun, Fanxing Li, Linzuo Zhang, Yu Hu, Renbiao Jin, Shuyu Wu, Wenxian Yu, Danping Zou
First: 2026-04-14T16:00:22+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-14T16:00:22+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Training and transferring learning-based policies for quadrotors from simulation to reality remains challenging due to inefficient visual rendering, physical modeling inaccuracies, unmodeled sensor discrepancies, and the absence of a unified platform integrating differentiable physics learning into end-to-end training. While recent work has demonstrated various end-to-end quadrotor control tasks, few systems provide a systematic, zero-shot transfer pipeline, hindering reproducibility and real-world deployment. To bridge this gap, we introduce E2E-Fly, an integrated framework featuring an agile quadrotor platform coupled with a full-stack training, validation, and deployment workflow. The training framework incorporates a high-performance simulator with support for differentiable physics learning and reinforcement learning, alongside structured reward design tailored to common quadrotor tasks. We further introduce a two-stage validation strategy using sim-to-sim transfer and hardware-in-the-loop testing, and deploy policies onto two physical quadrotor platforms via a dedicated low-level control interface and a comprehensive sim-to-real alignment methodology, encompassing system identification, domain randomization, latency compensation, and noise modeling. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first work to systematically unify differentiable physical learning with training, validation, and real-world deployment for quadrotors. Finally, we demonstrate the effectiveness of our framework for training six end-to-end control tasks and deploy them in the real world.
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the challenges of transferring learning-based quadrotor control from simulation to reality—such as inefficient rendering, modeling inaccuracies, and lack of unified platforms—the authors introduce E2E-Fly, an integrated system combining a high-performance simulator with differentiable physics learning and reinforcement learning, along with structured reward design. The framework employs a two-stage validation process involving sim-to-sim transfer and hardware-in-the-loop testing, and deploys trained policies on physical platforms using a dedicated low-level control interface and sim-to-real alignment techniques including system identification, domain randomization, latency compensation, and noise modeling. Experimental results demonstrate successful deployment of six end-to-end control tasks in real-world environments, showcasing the system’s ability to bridge simulation and reality with high fidelity and reproducibility.
为解决从仿真到现实环境中学习型四旋翼飞行器控制策略转移困难的问题——包括渲染效率低、物理建模误差、传感器差异未建模及缺乏统一平台——作者提出了E2E-Fly系统，该系统结合高性能仿真器、可微物理学习与强化学习，并配备针对常见任务的结构化奖励设计。该框架采用两阶段验证策略，包括仿真到仿真的迁移和硬件在环测试，并通过专用底层控制接口与仿真到现实对齐技术（如系统辨识、域随机化、延迟补偿和噪声建模）将策略部署到真实四旋翼平台上。实验结果表明，该系统成功实现了六个端到端控制任务在真实环境中的部署，验证了其在仿真与现实之间实现高保真、可复现转移的能力。
CoDe-R: Refining Decompiler Output with LLMs via Rationale Guidance and Adaptive Inference
Authors: Qiang Zhang, Zhongnian Li
First: 2026-04-14T15:58:38+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-14T15:58:38+00:00
Comments: 10 pages, 7 figures, 6 tables. Accepted by IJCNN 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Binary decompilation is a critical reverse engineering task aimed at reconstructing high-level source code from stripped executables. Although Large Language Models (LLMs) have recently shown promise, they often suffer from "logical hallucinations" and "semantic misalignment" due to the irreversible semantic loss during compilation, resulting in generated code that fails to re-execute. In this study, we propose Cognitive Decompiler Refinement with Robustness (CoDe-R), a lightweight two-stage code refinement framework. The first stage introduces Semantic Cognitive Enhancement (SCE), a Rationale-Guided Semantic Injection strategy that trains the model to recover high-level algorithmic intent alongside code. The second stage introduces a Dynamic Dual-Path Fallback (DDPF) mechanism during inference, which adaptively balances semantic recovery and syntactic stability via a hybrid verification strategy. Evaluation on the HumanEval-Decompile benchmark demonstrates that CoDe-R (using a 1.3B backbone) establishes a new State-of-the-Art (SOTA) in the lightweight regime. Notably, it is the first 1.3B model to exceed an Average Re-executability Rate of 50.00%, significantly outperforming the baseline and effectively bridging the gap between efficient models and expert-level performance. Our code is available at https://github.com/Theaoi/CoDe-R.
Summary / 总结
Binary decompilation is a critical reverse engineering task aimed at reconstructing high-level source code from stripped executables.
Round-Trip Translation Reveals What Frontier Multilingual Benchmarks Miss
Authors: Ronald Skorobogat, Ameya Prabhu, Matthias Bethge
First: 2026-04-14T15:58:21+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-14T15:58:21+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Multilingual benchmarks guide the development of frontier models. Yet multilingual evaluations reported by frontier models are structured similar to popular reasoning and knowledge benchmarks, but across many languages. We show such benchmarks, and consequently multilingual evaluations, measure mathematical reasoning and factual recall, not multilingual proficiency. For example, thinking variants dramatically outperform instruct variants on these benchmarks, yet often perform worse on real-world multilingual tasks, such as LMArena. We propose a simple alternative: evaluate multilingual capability via round-trip translation. Given text in a source language, translate it to a target language and back; semantic gaps between the original and result expose failures in multilingual generation capabilities. Round-trip translation correlates almost perfectly (\r{ho} = 0.94) with user ratings on LMArena with our benchmark, requires no human reference translations, and does not require a more capable multilingual judge than tested models. Lastly, we introduce Lost in Translation (LiT), a challenging round-trip translation benchmark spanning widely spoken languages worldwide, for realistic evaluation of multilingual frontier models.
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the observation that existing multilingual benchmarks fail to capture true multilingual proficiency and instead favor mathematical reasoning and factual recall, the paper proposes round-trip translation as a simple, effective evaluation method. This method involves translating text from a source language to a target language and back, with semantic discrepancies revealing multilingual generation weaknesses. Experiments show that round-trip translation correlates nearly perfectly (rho = 0.94) with human ratings on LMArena, requires no reference translations, and does not demand a more capable judge than the models being evaluated. The authors also introduce Lost in Translation (LiT), a new benchmark designed to realistically assess multilingual frontier models across widely spoken languages.
鉴于现有多语言基准未能有效衡量真实多语言能力，而偏向数学推理和事实记忆，本文提出通过往返翻译（round-trip translation）进行评估：将文本从源语言翻译至目标语言再译回原语言，语义差异揭示多语言生成缺陷。实验证明该方法与LMArena人工评分高度相关（rho=0.94），无需参考译文，也不需比被测模型更强大的评判者。作者同时推出Lost in Translation（LiT）基准，覆盖全球广泛使用的语言，以更真实地评估前沿多语言模型能力。
Tree Learning: A Multi-Skill Continual Learning Framework for Humanoid Robots
Authors: Yifei Yan, Linqi Ye
First: 2026-04-14T15:57:27+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-14T15:57:27+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
As reinforcement learning for humanoid robots evolves from single-task to multi-skill paradigms, efficiently expanding new skills while avoiding catastrophic forgetting has become a key challenge in embodied intelligence. Existing approaches either rely on complex topology adjustments in Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) models or require training extremely large-scale models, making lightweight deployment difficult. To address this, we propose Tree Learning, a multi-skill continual learning framework for humanoid robots. The framework adopts a root-branch hierarchical parameter inheritance mechanism, providing motion priors for branch skills through parameter reuse to fundamentally prevent catastrophic forgetting. A multi-modal feedforward adaptation mechanism combining phase modulation and interpolation is designed to support both periodic and aperiodic motions. A task-level reward shaping strategy is also proposed to accelerate skill convergence. Unity-based simulation experiments show that, in contrast to simultaneous multi-task training, Tree Learning achieves higher rewards across various representative locomotion skills while maintaining a 100% skill retention rate, enabling seamless multi-skill switching and real-time interactive control. We further validate the performance and generalization capability of Tree Learning on two distinct Unity-simulated tasks: a Super Mario-inspired interactive scenario and autonomous navigation in a classical Chinese garden environment.
Summary / 总结
As reinforcement learning for humanoid robots evolves from single-task to multi-skill paradigms, efficiently expanding new skills while avoiding catastrophic forgetting has become a key challenge in embodied intelligence.
Robotic Manipulation is Vision-to-Geometry Mapping ($f(v) \rightarrow G$): Vision-Geometry Backbones over Language and Video Models
Authors: Zijian Song, Qichang Li, Jiawei Zhou, Zhenlong Yuan, Tianshui Chen, Liang Lin, Guangrun Wang
First: 2026-04-14T15:57:16+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-14T15:57:16+00:00
Comments: 18 pages, 10 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
At its core, robotic manipulation is a problem of vision-to-geometry mapping ($f(v) \rightarrow G$). Physical actions are fundamentally defined by geometric properties like 3D positions and spatial relationships. Consequently, we argue that the foundation for generalizable robotic control should be a vision-geometry backbone, rather than the widely adopted vision-language or video models. Conventional VLA and video-predictive models rely on backbones pretrained on large-scale 2D image-text or temporal pixel data. While effective, their representations are largely shaped by semantic concepts or 2D priors, which do not intrinsically align with the precise 3D geometric nature required for physical manipulation. Driven by this insight, we propose the Vision-Geometry-Action (VGA) model, which directly conditions action generation on pretrained native 3D representations. Specifically, VGA replaces conventional language or video backbones with a pretrained 3D world model, establishing a seamless vision-to-geometry mapping that translates visual inputs directly into physical actions. To further enhance geometric consistency, we introduce a Progressive Volumetric Modulation module and adopt a joint training strategy. Extensive experiments validate the effectiveness of our approach. In simulation benchmarks, VGA outperforms top-tier VLA baselines including $π_{0.5}$ and GeoVLA, demonstrating its superiority in precise manipulation. More importantly, VGA exhibits remarkable zero-shot generalization to unseen viewpoints in real-world deployments, consistently outperforming $π_{0.5}$. These results highlight that operating on native 3D representations-rather than translating through language or 2D video priors-is a highly promising direction for achieving generalizable physical intelligence.
Summary / 总结
At its core, robotic manipulation is a problem of vision-to-geometry mapping ($f(v) \rightarrow G$).
Frequency-aware Decomposition Learning for Sensorless Wrench Forecasting on a Vibration-rich Hydraulic Manipulator
Authors: Hyeonbeen Lee, Min-Jae Jung, Tae-Kyeong Yeu, Jong-Boo Han, Daegil Park, Jin-Gyun Kim
First: 2026-04-14T15:54:29+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-14T15:54:29+00:00
Comments: 11 pages, 6 figures, submitted to IEEE/ASME Transactions on Mechatronics
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Force and torque (F/T) sensing is critical for robot-environment interaction, but physical F/T sensors impose constraints in size, cost, and fragility. To mitigate this, recent studies have estimated force/wrench sensorlessly from robot internal states. While existing methods generally target relatively slow interactions, tasks involving rapid interactions, such as grinding, can induce task-critical high-frequency vibrations, and estimation in such robotic settings remains underexplored. To address this gap, we propose a Frequency-aware Decomposition Network (FDN) for short-term forecasting of vibration-rich wrench from proprioceptive history. FDN predicts spectrally decomposed wrench with asymmetric deterministic and probabilistic heads, modeling the high-frequency residual as a learned conditional distribution. It further incorporates frequency-awareness to adaptively enhance input spectra with learned filtering and impose a frequency-band prior on the outputs. We pretrain FDN on a large-scale open-source robot dataset and transfer the learned proprioception-to-wrench representation to the downstream. On real-world grinding excavation data from a 6-DoF hydraulic manipulator and under a delayed estimation setting, FDN outperforms baseline estimators and forecasters in the high-frequency band and remains competitive in the low-frequency band. Transfer learning provides additional gains, suggesting the potential of large-scale pretraining and transfer learning for robotic wrench estimation. Code and data will be made available upon acceptance.
Summary / 总结
Force and torque (F/T) sensing is critical for robot-environment interaction, but physical F/T sensors impose constraints in size, cost, and fragility.
One View Is Enough! Monocular Training for In-the-Wild Novel View Generation
Authors: Adrien Ramanana Rahary, Nicolas Dufour, Patrick Perez, David Picard
First: 2026-03-24T17:54:25+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-14T15:54:29+00:00
Comments: 36 pages, 17 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Monocular novel-view synthesis has long required multi-view image pairs for supervision, limiting training data scale and diversity. We argue it is not necessary: one view is enough. We present OVIE, trained entirely on unpaired internet images. We leverage a monocular depth estimator as a geometric scaffold at training time: we lift a source image into 3D, apply a sampled camera transformation, and project to obtain a pseudo-target view. To handle disocclusions, we introduce a masked training formulation that restricts geometric, perceptual, and textural losses to valid regions, enabling training on 30 million uncurated images. At inference, OVIE is geometry-free, requiring no depth estimator or 3D representation. Trained exclusively on in-the-wild images, OVIE outperforms prior methods in a zero-shot setting, while being 600x faster than the second-best baseline. Code and models are publicly available at https://github.com/AdrienRR/ovie.
中文标题/摘要
标题：一个视图就足够！野外新视角生成的单目训练
单目新视角合成长期以来需要多视角图像对进行监督，限制了训练数据的规模和多样性。我们认为这并非必要：一个视图就足够。我们提出了OVIE，完全基于未配对的互联网图像进行训练。训练时，我们利用单目深度估计器作为几何骨架：将源图像提升至3D，应用采样的相机变换，并投影以获得伪目标视图。为处理遮挡问题，我们引入了掩码训练形式，将几何、感知和纹理损失限制在有效区域，从而能够在3000万张未筛选的图像上进行训练。推理时，OVIE无需几何信息，不需要深度估计器或3D表示。仅在野外图像上训练，OVIE在零样本设置下优于先前方法，且比次优基线快600倍。代码和模型可在https://github.com/AdrienRR/ovie获取。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the scarcity of multi-view training data for monocular novel-view synthesis, this work argues that one view suffices for training. To handle disocclusions, it employs masked losses that constrain training to valid regions, enabling training on 30 million uncurated images. At inference, OVIE is geometry-free and requires no depth or 3D representation. Evaluated in a zero-shot setting on in-the-wild data, OVIE outperforms prior methods and is 600x faster than the second-best baseline.

A Sanity Check on Composed Image Retrieval
Authors: Yikun Liu, Jiangchao Yao, Weidi Xie, Yanfeng Wang
First: 2026-04-14T15:52:22+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-14T15:52:22+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Composed Image Retrieval (CIR) aims to retrieve a target image based on a query composed of a reference image, and a relative caption that specifies the desired modification. Despite the rapid development of CIR models, their performance is not well characterized by existing benchmarks, which inherently contain indeterminate queries degrading the evaluation (i.e., multiple candidate images, rather than solely the target image, meet the query criteria), and have not considered their effectiveness in the context of the multi-round system. Motivated by this, we consider improving the evaluation procedure from two aspects: 1) we introduce FISD, a Fully-Informed Semantically-Diverse benchmark, which employs generative models to precisely control the variables of reference-target image pairs, enabling a more accurate evaluation of CIR methods across six dimensions, without query ambiguity; 2) we propose an automatic multi-round agentic evaluation framework to probe the potential of the existing models in the interactive scenarios. By observing how models adapt and refine their choices over successive rounds of queries, this framework provides a more realistic appraisal of their efficacy in practical applications. Extensive experiments and comparisons prove the value of our novel evaluation on typical CIR methods.
中文标题/摘要
标题：关于合成图像检索的合理性检验
合成图像检索（CIR）旨在根据由参考图像和指定修改的相对描述组成的查询，检索目标图像。尽管CIR模型发展迅速，但现有基准测试未能充分评估其性能，因为这些基准固有地包含模糊查询（即多个候选图像而非仅目标图像满足查询条件），且未考虑其在多轮系统中的有效性。为此，我们从两个方面改进评估流程：1）我们引入FISD（完全知情语义多样）基准，利用生成模型精确控制参考-目标图像对的变量，从而在六个维度上无歧义地更准确评估CIR方法；2）我们提出自动多轮代理评估框架，以探究现有模型在交互场景中的潜力。通过观察模型在多轮查询中如何调整和优化选择，该框架更真实地评估其在实际应用中的效能。大量实验和对比证明了我们新评估方法对典型CIR方法的价值。
Summary / 总结
Composed Image Retrieval (CIR) aims to retrieve a target image based on a query composed of a reference image, and a relative caption that specifies the desired modification.
Deep QP Safety Filter: Model-free Learning for Reachability-based Safety Filter
Authors: Byeongjun Kim, H. Jin Kim
First: 2026-01-29T05:49:48+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-14T15:50:23+00:00
Comments: Accepted to the 8th Annual Learning for Dynamics and Control Conference (L4DC 2026)
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We introduce Deep QP Safety Filter, a fully data-driven safety layer for black-box dynamical systems. Our method learns a Quadratic-Program (QP) safety filter without model knowledge by combining Hamilton-Jacobi (HJ) reachability with model-free learning. We construct contraction-based losses for both the safety value and its derivatives, and train two neural networks accordingly. In the exact setting, the learned critic converges to the viscosity solution (and its derivative), even for non-smooth values. Across diverse dynamical systems -- even including a hybrid system -- and multiple RL tasks, Deep QP Safety Filter substantially reduces pre-convergence failures while accelerating learning toward higher returns than strong baselines, offering a principled and practical route to safe, model-free control.
中文标题/摘要
标题：深度QP安全滤波器：基于可达性的无模型安全滤波学习
我们提出了深度QP安全滤波器，这是一种完全数据驱动的安全层，适用于黑盒动态系统。该方法通过结合哈密顿-雅可比（HJ）可达性与无模型学习，在无需模型知识的情况下学习二次规划（QP）安全滤波器。我们为安全值及其导数构建了基于收缩的损失函数，并相应训练两个神经网络。在精确设定下，即使对于非光滑值，所学习的批评者（critic）仍收敛至粘性解（及其导数）。在多种动态系统（包括混合系统）和多个强化学习任务中，深度QP安全滤波器显著减少了预收敛失败，同时加速学习，获得比强基线更高的回报，提供了一条原理清晰且实用的无模型安全控制路径。
Summary / 总结
We introduce Deep QP Safety Filter, a fully data-driven safety layer for black-box dynamical systems.
为实现黑盒动态系统的安全、无模型控制，本文提出Deep QP Safety Filter方法，结合哈密顿-雅可比可达性与无模型学习，直接学习二次规划安全滤波器。该方法通过针对安全值及其导数设计的收缩损失函数，训练两个神经网络，即使在非光滑值函数下也能收敛至粘性解。在多种动力系统（含混合系统）和强化学习任务中，该方法显著降低预收敛失败率，加速学习过程，最终回报优于多个强基线方法。
Physics and causally constrained discrete-time neural models of turbulent dynamical systems
Authors: Fabrizio Falasca, Laure Zanna
First: 2026-02-14T18:43:52+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-14T15:49:12+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We present a framework for constructing physics and causally constrained neural models of turbulent dynamical systems from data. We first formulate a finite-time flow map with strict energy-preserving nonlinearities for stable modeling of temporally discrete trajectories. We then impose causal constraints to suppress spurious interactions across degrees of freedom. The resulting neural models accurately capture stationary statistics and responses to both small and large external forcings. We demonstrate the framework on the stochastic Charney-DeVore equations and on a symmetry-broken Lorenz-96 system. The framework is broadly applicable to reduced-order modeling of turbulent dynamical systems from observational data.
中文标题/摘要
标题：湍流动力系统中基于物理与因果约束的离散时间神经模型
我们提出了一种从数据构建湍流动力系统物理与因果约束神经模型的框架。首先，我们构建具有严格能量守恒非线性的有限时间流映射，以稳定建模时序离散轨迹；随后施加因果约束以抑制自由度间的虚假相互作用。所得神经模型能准确捕捉稳态统计特性及对小至大外部强迫的响应。我们在随机Charney-DeVore方程和对称破缺的Lorenz-96系统中验证了该框架，其广泛适用于从观测数据对湍流动力系统的降阶建模。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for accurate, physically consistent neural models of turbulent systems, this work proposes a framework that constructs discrete-time neural networks constrained by energy preservation and causality. The method defines a finite-time flow map with nonlinearities that strictly conserve energy, then enforces causal constraints to eliminate spurious cross-interactions among system variables. Experimental results on stochastic Charney-DeVore equations and a symmetry-broken Lorenz-96 system show that the models faithfully reproduce stationary statistics and respond correctly to both small and large external forcings, demonstrating broad applicability for reduced-order modeling from observational data.
为构建湍流动力系统中物理一致且因果约束的神经模型，该研究提出了一种框架，通过有限时间流映射结合严格能量守恒的非线性项，并施加因果约束以消除变量间虚假相互作用。实验在随机Charney-DeVore方程和对称破缺的Lorenz-96系统上验证，结果显示模型能准确再现稳态统计特性，并对小至大尺度外部扰动做出正确响应，表明该框架适用于从观测数据中进行湍流系统的降阶建模。
BEAM: Bi-level Memory-adaptive Algorithmic Evolution for LLM-Powered Heuristic Design
Authors: Chuyang Xiang, Yichen Wei, Jiale Ma, Handing Wang, Junchi Yan
First: 2026-04-14T15:46:47+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-14T15:46:47+00:00
Comments: 24 pages, 11 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Large Language Model-based Hyper Heuristic (LHH) has recently emerged as an efficient way for automatic heuristic design. However, most existing LHHs just perform well in optimizing a single function within a pre-defined solver. Their single-layer evolution makes them not effective enough to write a competent complete solver. While some variants incorporate hyperparameter tuning or attempt to generate complex code through iterative local modifications, they still lack a high-level algorithmic modeling, leading to limited exploration efficiency. To address this, we reformulate heuristic design as a Bi-level Optimization problem and propose \textbf{BEAM} (Bi-level Memory-adaptive Algorithmic Evolution). BEAM's exterior layer evolves high-level algorithmic structures with function placeholders through genetic algorithm (GA), while the interior layer realizes these placeholders via Monte Carlo Tree Search (MCTS). We further introduce an Adaptive Memory module to facilitate complex code generation. To support the evaluation for complex code generation, we point out the limitations of starting LHHs from scratch or from code templates and introduce a Knowledge Augmentation (KA) Pipeline. Experimental results on several optimization problems demonstrate that BEAM significantly outperforms existing LHHs, notably reducing the optimality gap by 37.84\% on aggregate in CVRP hybrid algorithm design. BEAM also designs a heuristic that outperforms SOTA Maximum Independent Set (MIS) solver KaMIS.
中文标题/摘要
标题：BEAM：面向大语言模型启发式设计的双层自适应算法演化
基于大语言模型的超启发式（LHH）近期成为自动启发式设计的有效方法。然而，现有大多数LHH仅在预定义求解器内优化单一目标函数，其单层演化机制难以生成完整有效的求解器。尽管部分变体引入超参数调优或通过迭代局部修改生成复杂代码，仍缺乏高层算法建模，导致探索效率有限。为此，我们将启发式设计重构为双层优化问题，并提出\textbf{BEAM}（双层自适应算法演化）。BEAM外层通过遗传算法（GA）演化高层算法结构并填充函数占位符，内层则通过蒙特卡洛树搜索（MCTS）实现占位符。我们进一步引入自适应记忆模块以促进复杂代码生成。为支持复杂代码生成的评估，我们指出从零或代码模板启动LHH的局限性，并提出知识增强（KA）流水线。在多个优化问题上的实验表明，BEAM显著优于现有LHH，例如在CVRP混合算法设计中整体最优性差距减少37.84%。BEAM还设计出优于当前最优最大独立集（MIS）求解器KaMIS的启发式算法。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitation of existing Large Language Model-based Hyper Heuristics (LHHs) that lack high-level algorithmic modeling and struggle to generate complete solvers, the authors propose BEAM, a bi-level memory-adaptive algorithmic evolution framework. BEAM employs a genetic algorithm at the outer layer to evolve high-level algorithmic structures with function placeholders, while the inner layer uses Monte Carlo Tree Search to instantiate these placeholders into executable code. An adaptive memory module enhances code generation complexity, and a knowledge augmentation pipeline addresses evaluation challenges from scratch or template-based initialization. Experiments across multiple optimization problems show BEAM significantly outperforms prior LHHs, reducing the optimality gap by 37.84% on average in CVRP hybrid solver design and producing a heuristic that surpasses the state-of-the-art Maximum Independent Set solver KaMIS.
为了解决现有基于大语言模型的超启发式方法缺乏高层算法建模、难以生成完整求解器的问题，作者提出了BEAM（双层自适应记忆算法演化）框架。该框架外层使用遗传算法演化高层算法结构并填充函数占位符，内层通过蒙特卡洛树搜索实现占位符的具体化。引入自适应记忆模块提升复杂代码生成能力，并设计知识增强管道解决评估难题。实验表明，BEAM在CVRP混合算法设计中平均最优差距降低37.84%，所生成启发式算法优于当前最优的MIS求解器KaMIS。
Don't Show Pixels, Show Cues: Unlocking Visual Tool Reasoning in Language Models via Perception Programs
Authors: Muhammad Kamran Janjua, Hugo Silva, Di Niu, Bahador Rashidi
Venue: CVPR 2026
First: 2026-04-14T15:45:22+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-14T15:45:22+00:00
Comments: Accepted to CVPR 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Multimodal language models (MLLMs) are increasingly paired with vision tools (e.g., depth, flow, correspondence) to enhance visual reasoning. However, despite access to these tool-generated visual cues, MLLMs often fail to benefit from them. Existing approaches typically feed raw tool outputs into the model, but these dense, pixel-level representations are misaligned with the language-native reasoning strengths of LLMs, leading to weak perception and reliance on language priors. We argue that, in problems where vision tools can provide the necessary visual cues, the bottleneck is not more tool calls or larger MLLMs, it is how tool outputs are represented. We introduce Perception Programs (P$^2$), a training-free, model-agnostic method that rewrites tool outputs into compact, structured, language-native summaries that MLLMs can directly parse and reason over. Across six perception-centric tasks in BLINK, P$^2$ consistently yields large improvements over base models and raw tool-augmented baselines. With GPT-5 Mini as the base model, P$^2$ raises its accuracy from 41.35\% to 86.47\% on multi-view reasoning, from 52.42\% to 81.45\% on relative depth, and achieves a 22\% average gain across tasks, setting new state-of-the-art results. Even on smaller MLLMs, e.g., InternVL3.5-4B and Qwen3VL-4B, we observe 15-40\% absolute gains from P$^2$, surpassing prior agentic, supervised, and RL-based tool-use methods-without any training or model modifications.
中文标题/摘要
标题：不要显示像素，显示线索：通过感知程序解锁语言模型中的视觉工具推理
多模态语言模型（MLLMs）越来越多地与视觉工具（如深度、光流、对应关系）结合以增强视觉推理。然而，尽管可以访问这些工具生成的视觉线索，MLLMs 通常无法从中受益。现有方法通常将原始工具输出直接输入模型，但这些密集的像素级表示与LLMs的语言原生推理能力不匹配，导致感知能力弱且依赖语言先验。我们认为，在视觉工具能够提供必要视觉线索的问题中，瓶颈并非更多工具调用或更大的MLLMs，而是工具输出的表示方式。我们提出了感知程序（P²），一种无需训练、模型无关的方法，将工具输出重写为紧凑、结构化、语言原生的摘要，使MLLMs可直接解析和推理。在BLINK的六个以感知为中心的任务中，P²始终显著优于基线模型和原始工具增强基线。以GPT-5 Mini为基线模型时，P²在多视角推理任务中准确率从41.35%提升至86.47%，在相对深度任务中从52.42%提升至81.45%，平均任务增益达22%，创下新SOTA。即使在较小的MLLMs（如InternVL3.5-4B和Qwen3VL-4B）上，P²也带来15-40%的绝对增益，超越此前的代理、监督和基于强化学习的工具使用方法——无需任何训练或模型修改。

Multimodal language models (MLLMs) are increasingly paired with vision tools (e.g., depth, flow, correspondence) to enhance visual reasoning.
Thinking Sparks!: Emergent Attention Heads in Reasoning Models During Post Training
Authors: Yein Park, Minbyul Jeong, Jaewoo Kang
First: 2025-09-30T04:23:43+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-14T15:45:09+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
The remarkable capabilities of modern large reasoning models are largely unlocked through post-training techniques such as supervised fine-tuning (SFT) and reinforcement learning (RL). However, the architectural mechanisms behind such improvements remain largely opaque. In this work, we use circuit analysis to demonstrate that post-training for complex reasoning sparks the emergence of novel, functionally specialized attention heads. These heads collectively support structured reasoning and computation. Our comparative analysis across various model families reveals that these emergent heads evolve differently under different training regimes. Distillation and SFT foster a cumulative addition of stable reasoning heads. In contrast, group relative policy optimization (GRPO) operates in a dynamic search mode: relatively few attention heads are iteratively activated, evaluated, and pruned, with their survival closely tracking fluctuations in the task reward signal. Furthermore, we find that controllable "think on/off" models do not possess dedicated "thinking" heads. Instead, turning off explicit reasoning triggers a broader-but less efficient-set of compensatory heads. Through ablation and qualitative analyses, we connect these circuit-level dynamics to a crucial performance trade-off: strengthened heads enable sophisticated problem-solving strategies for difficult problems but can also introduce "over-thinking" failure modes, such as calculation errors or logical loops on simpler tasks. These findings connect circuit-level dynamics to macro-level performance, identifying an inherent tension where complex reasoning comes at the cost of elementary computations. More broadly, our work points to future directions for training policy design, emphasizing the need to balance the development of effective reasoning strategies with the assurance of reliable, flawless execution.
中文标题/摘要
标题：思维火花！：推理模型后训练期间涌现的注意力头
现代大型推理模型的卓越能力主要通过监督微调（SFT）和强化学习（RL）等后训练技术解锁。然而，这些改进背后的架构机制仍不透明。本研究通过电路分析表明，复杂推理的后训练会催生出功能特化的新型注意力头，共同支持结构化推理与计算。跨多种模型家族的比较分析显示，不同训练范式下这些涌现头的演化方式各异：蒸馏与SFT促进稳定推理头的累积添加；而群体相对策略优化（GRPO）则处于动态搜索模式：少量注意力头被迭代激活、评估与剪枝，其存活情况紧密跟随任务奖励信号的波动。此外，我们发现可控的“思考开/关”模型并无专用“思考”头；关闭显式推理会触发一组更广泛但效率更低的补偿性头。通过消融与定性分析，我们将电路级动态与关键性能权衡联系起来：强化头可支持复杂难题的解法策略，但也可能引入“过度思考”失败模式，如简单任务中的计算错误或逻辑循环。这些发现将电路级动态与宏观性能关联，揭示了复杂推理与基础计算之间固有的张力。更广泛而言，本研究指明了未来策略训练方向，强调需在有效推理策略发展与可靠无误执行保障间取得平衡。
Summary / 总结
The remarkable capabilities of modern large reasoning models are largely unlocked through post-training techniques such as supervised fine-tuning (SFT) and reinforcement learning (RL).
Representing 3D Faces with Learnable B-Spline Volumes
Authors: Prashanth Chandran, Daoye Wang, Timo Bolkart
Venue: CVPR 2026 Highlight
First: 2026-04-14T15:42:48+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-14T15:42:48+00:00
Comments: Accepted to CVPR 2026 (Highlight)
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We present CUBE (Control-based Unified B-spline Encoding), a new geometric representation for human faces that combines B-spline volumes with learned features, and demonstrate its use as a decoder for 3D scan registration and monocular 3D face reconstruction. Unlike existing B-spline representations with 3D control points, CUBE is parametrized by a lattice (e.g., 8 x 8 x 8) of high-dimensional control features, increasing the model's expressivity. These features define a continuous, two-stage mapping from a 3D parametric domain to 3D Euclidean space via an intermediate feature space. First, high-dimensional control features are locally blended using the B-spline bases, yielding a high-dimensional feature vector whose first three values define a 3D base mesh. A small MLP then processes this feature vector to predict a residual displacement from the base shape, yielding the final refined 3D coordinates. To reconstruct 3D surfaces in dense semantic correspondence, CUBE is queried at 3D coordinates sampled from a fixed template mesh. Crucially, CUBE retains the local support property of traditional B-spline representations, enabling local surface editing by updating individual control features. We demonstrate the strengths of this representation by training transformer-based encoders to predict CUBE's control features from unstructured point clouds and monocular images, achieving state-of-the-art scan registration results compared to recent baselines.
中文标题/摘要
标题：使用可学习B样条体积表示3D人脸
我们提出了CUBE（基于控制点的统一B样条编码），这是一种结合B样条体积与学习特征的新几何表示方法，并将其作为3D扫描配准和单目3D人脸重建的解码器。与现有使用3D控制点的B样条表示不同，CUBE通过高维控制特征的格点（如8x8x8）进行参数化，从而提升模型表达能力。这些特征通过中间特征空间，实现从3D参数域到3D欧几里得空间的连续两阶段映射。首先，利用B样条基函数局部混合高维控制特征，生成高维特征向量，其前三个值定义基础3D网格；随后，一个小型MLP处理该特征向量，预测相对于基础形状的残差位移，从而得到最终优化的3D坐标。为重建具有密集语义对应关系的3D表面，CUBE在固定模板网格采样的3D坐标处进行查询。关键的是，CUBE保留了传统B样条表示的局部支撑特性，允许通过更新单个控制特征实现局部表面编辑。我们通过训练基于Transformer的编码器，从无结构点云和单目图像预测CUBE的控制特征，实现了优于近期基线的扫描配准效果。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for expressive yet locally editable 3D face representations, CUBE introduces a novel geometric encoding that combines B-spline volumes with learned high-dimensional control features arranged in a lattice. The method maps 3D parametric coordinates through an intermediate feature space to generate a base mesh and then refines it via a residual displacement predicted by a small MLP. Evaluated on 3D scan registration and monocular reconstruction tasks, CUBE achieves state-of-the-art performance when trained with transformer-based encoders to predict control features from point clouds or images, while preserving local support for targeted surface editing.
为提升3D人脸表示的表达能力并支持局部编辑，CUBE提出一种结合B样条体积与可学习高维控制特征的新几何编码方法。该方法通过中间特征空间将3D参数坐标映射为基网格，再经小型MLP预测残差位移以精炼最终3D坐标。在3D扫描配准与单目重建任务中，当使用Transformer编码器从点云或图像预测控制特征时，CUBE达到当前最优性能，同时保留局部支撑特性，支持对特定区域的表面编辑。
Detecting Complex Money Laundering Patterns with Incremental and Distributed Graph Modeling
Authors: Haseeb Tariq, Alen Kaja, Marwan Hassani
First: 2026-04-01T18:40:58+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-14T15:41:34+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Money launderers take advantage of limitations in existing detection approaches by hiding their financial footprints in a deceitful manner. They manage this by replicating transaction patterns that the monitoring systems cannot easily distinguish. As a result, criminally gained assets are pushed into legitimate financial channels without drawing attention. Algorithms developed to monitor money flows often struggle with scale and complexity. The difficulty of identifying such activities is further intensified by the (persistent) inability of current solutions to control the excessive number of false positive signals produced by rigid, risk-based rules systems. We propose a framework called ReDiRect (REduce, DIstribute, and RECTify), specifically designed to overcome these challenges. The primary contribution of our work is a novel framing of this problem in an unsupervised setting; where a large transaction graph is fuzzily partitioned into smaller, manageable components to enable fast processing in a distributed manner. In addition, we define a refined evaluation metric that better captures the effectiveness of exposed money laundering patterns. Through comprehensive experimentation, we demonstrate that our framework achieves superior performance compared to existing and state-of-the-art techniques, particularly in terms of efficiency and real-world applicability. For validation, we used the real (open source) Libra dataset and the recently released synthetic datasets by IBM Watson. Our code and datasets are available at https://github.com/mhaseebtariq/redirect.
中文标题/摘要
标题：利用增量分布式图建模检测复杂洗钱模式
洗钱者利用现有检测方法的局限性，通过欺骗性手段隐藏其金融足迹，通过复制监控系统难以区分的交易模式实现此目标，从而将犯罪所得资产推入合法金融渠道而不引起注意。用于监控资金流动的算法常因规模和复杂性而力不从心，而当前解决方案在控制由僵化风险规则系统产生的大量误报方面仍存在（持续）困难。我们提出名为ReDiRect（REduce, DIstribute, and RECTify）的框架，专门应对这些挑战。本工作的主要贡献是将该问题在无监督环境下重新定义：将大型交易图模糊分割为更小、可管理的组件，以支持分布式快速处理。此外，我们定义了一项改进的评估指标，更准确地衡量暴露洗钱模式的有效性。通过全面实验，我们证明该框架在效率和实际应用方面优于现有及最先进的技术。为验证，我们使用了真实（开源）Libra数据集和IBM Watson最近发布的合成数据集。代码和数据集可在https://github.com/mhaseebtariq/redirect获取。
Summary / 总结
Money launderers take advantage of limitations in existing detection approaches by hiding their financial footprints in a deceitful manner.
metasnf: Meta Clustering with Similarity Network Fusion in R
Authors: Prashanth S Velayudhan, Xiaoqiao Xu, Prajkta Kallurkar, Ana Patricia Balbon, Maria T Secara, Adam Taback, Denise Sabac, Nicholas Chan, Shihao Ma, Bo Wang, Daniel Felsky, Stephanie H Ameis, Brian Cox, Colin Hawco, Lauren Erdman, Anne L Wheeler
First: 2024-10-23T15:39:08+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-14T15:41:26+00:00
Comments: 66 pages, 26 figures, provisionally accepted at Journal of Statistical Software
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
metasnf is an R package that enables users to apply meta clustering, a method for efficiently searching a broad space of cluster solutions by clustering the solutions themselves, to clustering workflows based on similarity network fusion (SNF). SNF is a multi-modal data integration algorithm commonly used for biomedical subtype discovery. The package also contains functions to assist with cluster visualization, characterization, and validation. This package can help researchers identify SNF-derived cluster solutions that are guided by context-specific utility over context-agnostic measures of quality.

metasnf 是一个R包，使用户能够将元聚类方法应用于基于相似性网络融合（SNF）的聚类工作流程。元聚类通过聚类解决方案本身，高效地在广泛的聚类解空间中进行搜索。SNF是一种常用于生物医学亚型发现的多模态数据整合算法。该包还包含辅助聚类可视化、表征和验证的功能。该包可帮助研究人员识别由SNF导出的聚类解，这些解由特定情境下的实用性引导，而非情境无关的质量度量。

TCL: Enabling Fast and Efficient Cross-Hardware Tensor Program Optimization via Continual Learning
Authors: Chaoyao Shen, Linfeng Jiang, Yixian Shen, Tao Xu, Guoqing Li, Anuj Pathania, Andy D. Pimentel, Meng Zhang
First: 2026-04-14T15:40:30+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-14T15:40:30+00:00
Comments: introduces TCL framework for cross-hardware tensor program optimization with active learning, Mamba-based cost model, and continual knowledge distillation; includes extensive experiments on CPU and GPU platforms
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Deep learning (DL) compilers rely on cost models and auto-tuning to optimize tensor programs for target hardware. However, existing approaches depend on large offline datasets, incurring high collection costs and offering suboptimal transferability across platforms. In this paper, we introduce TCL, a novel efficient and transferable compiler framework for fast tensor program optimization across diverse hardware platforms to address these challenges. Specifically, TCL is built on three core enablers: (1) the RDU Sampler, a data-efficient active learning strategy that selects only 10% of tensor programs by jointly optimizing Representativeness, Diversity, and Uncertainty, substantially reducing data collection costs while maintaining near-original model accuracy; (2) a new Mamba-based cost model that efficiently captures long-range schedule dependencies while achieving a favorable trade-off between prediction accuracy and computational cost through reduced parameterization and lightweight sequence modeling; and (3) a continuous knowledge distillation framework that effectively and progressively transfers knowledge across multiple hardware platforms while avoiding the parameter explosion and data dependency issues typically caused by traditional multi-task learning. Extensive experiments validate the effectiveness of each individual enabler and the holistic TCL framework. When optimizing a range of mainstream DL models on both CPU and GPU platforms, TCL achieves, on average, 16.8x and 12.48x faster tuning time, and 1.20x and 1.13x lower inference latency, respectively, compared to Tenset-MLP.
中文标题/摘要
标题：TCL：通过持续学习实现跨硬件张量程序优化的快速高效方法
深度学习编译器依赖成本模型和自动调优来优化针对特定硬件的张量程序。然而，现有方法依赖大型离线数据集，收集成本高且跨平台迁移效果不佳。本文提出TCL，一种面向多种硬件平台的高效、可迁移的编译框架，以解决上述问题。具体而言，TCL基于三个核心组件：(1) RDU采样器，一种数据高效的主动学习策略，通过联合优化代表性、多样性和不确定性，仅选择10%的张量程序，大幅降低数据收集成本同时保持接近原始模型的精度；(2) 基于Mamba的新成本模型，高效捕捉长程调度依赖，通过参数缩减和轻量级序列建模，在预测精度与计算成本间取得良好平衡；(3) 持续知识蒸馏框架，有效且渐进式地在多个硬件平台间迁移知识，避免传统多任务学习常导致的参数爆炸和数据依赖问题。大量实验验证了各组件及整体TCL框架的有效性。在CPU和GPU平台上优化主流深度学习模型时，TCL平均分别比Tenset-MLP快16.8倍和12.48倍，推理延迟分别降低1.20倍和1.13倍。
Summary / 总结
To address the high cost and poor transferability of existing DL compiler optimization methods, TCL introduces a novel framework for fast cross-hardware tensor program tuning. It employs an RDU Sampler for data-efficient active learning, selecting only 10% of programs while preserving model accuracy; a Mamba-based cost model that captures long-range schedule dependencies with reduced parameters and low computational overhead; and a continual knowledge distillation mechanism that transfers optimization knowledge across platforms without parameter explosion or data dependency. Experiments show TCL achieves 16.8x and 12.48x faster tuning times, and 1.20x and 1.13x lower inference latency on CPU and GPU respectively, compared to Tenset-MLP.
为解决现有深度学习编译器优化方法成本高、跨平台迁移性差的问题，TCL提出了一种面向多硬件平台的高效张量程序调优框架。该框架包含三个核心组件：RDU采样器通过联合优化代表性、多样性和不确定性，仅选取10%的程序样本即可保持模型精度；基于Mamba的代价模型能高效捕捉长程调度依赖，兼顾精度与计算开销；连续知识蒸馏机制可跨平台渐进式迁移优化知识，避免传统多任务学习的参数爆炸与数据依赖问题。实验表明，与Tenset-MLP相比，TCL在CPU和GPU上分别实现16.8倍和12.48倍的调优加速，以及1.20倍和1.13倍的推理延迟降低。
